"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

export default function CadastroPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [acceptedTerms, setAcceptedTerms] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function handleSignup(
    event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const normalizedName = name.trim();
    const normalizedEmail = email.trim();

    if (!normalizedName) {
      setError("Informe seu nome completo.");
      return;
    }

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError("A senha precisa ter pelo menos 8 caracteres.");
      return;
    }

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("As senhas não conferem.");
      return;
    }

    if (!acceptedTerms) {
      setError(
        "É necessário aceitar os Termos de Uso e estar ciente da Política de Privacidade.",
      );
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    const { error: signupError } =
      await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email: normalizedEmail,
        password,
        options: {
          data: {
            full_name: normalizedName,
            user_type: "client",
            accepted_terms: true,
          },
        },
      });

    if (signupError) {
      setError(
        `Não foi possível criar a conta: ${signupError.message}`,
      );
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setMessage(
      "Conta criada com sucesso. Você já pode entrar na VemGO.",
    );

    setLoading(false);

    setTimeout(() => {
      router.replace("/login");
    }, 1500);
  }

  function handleProfessionalSignup() {
    router.push("/cadastro-profissional");
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[85vh] max-w-md flex-col justify-center">
        <div className="mb-8 text-center">
          <Image
            src="/logo-vemgo.png"
            alt="VemGO"
            width={360}
            height={200}
            className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            priority
          />

          <h1 className="mt-6 text-3xl font-bold">
            Criar conta
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">
            Escolha como você deseja usar a VemGO.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-1.5">
          <p className="mb-2 px-3 pt-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
            Criar conta como
          </p>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-black shadow-lg"
            >
              Cliente
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleProfessionalSignup}
              className="rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-zinc-400 transition hover:bg-zinc-800 hover:text-orange-400"
            >
              Profissional
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form
          onSubmit={handleSignup}
          className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-zinc-900/40 p-6"
        >
          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="name"
              className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
            >
              Nome completo
            </label>

            <input
              id="name"
              type="text"
              autoComplete="name"
              value={name}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setName(event.target.value)
              }
              required
              placeholder="Seu nome completo"
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5">
            <label
              htmlFor="email"
              className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
            >
              E-mail
            </label>

            <input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setEmail(event.target.value)
              }
              required
              placeholder="seu@email.com"
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5">
            <label
              htmlFor="password"
              className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
            >
              Senha
            </label>

            <input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setPassword(event.target.value)
              }
              required
              placeholder="Mínimo de 8 caracteres"
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5">
            <label
              htmlFor="confirmPassword"
              className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
            >
              Confirmar senha
            </label>

            <input
              id="confirmPassword"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)
              }
              required
              placeholder="Digite a senha novamente"
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/60 p-4">
            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={acceptedTerms}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setAcceptedTerms(event.target.checked)
                }
                className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-emerald-500"
              />

              <span className="text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                Li e aceito os{" "}
                <a
                  href="/termos"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="font-semibold text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300"
                >
                  Termos de Uso
                </a>{" "}
                da VemGO e estou ciente da{" "}
                <a
                  href="/privacidade"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="font-semibold text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300"
                >
                  Política de Privacidade
                </a>
                .
              </span>
            </label>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {message && (
            <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-900 bg-emerald-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-300">
              {message}
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? "Criando..." : "Criar conta"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-zinc-400">
          <p>
            Já tem uma conta?{" "}
            <a
              href="/login"
              className="font-semibold text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300"
            >
              Entrar
            </a>
          </p>

          <p className="mt-4">
            Quer trabalhar com a VemGO?{" "}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleProfessionalSignup}
              className="font-semibold text-orange-400 hover:text-orange-300"
            >
              Cadastro profissional →
            </button>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cadastro-profissional\area-atendimento\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";
import {
  getNextProfessionalOnboardingStep,
  getProfessionalOnboardingRoute,
  PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS,
} from "@/lib/professional-onboarding";

type Region = {
  id?: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  country: string;
  max_distance_km: number;
  is_active: boolean;
};

const DEFAULT_DISTANCE = 20;
const TOTAL_STEPS = 9;

export default function CadastroProfissionalAreaAtendimentoPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [region, setRegion] = useState<Region>({
    city: "",
    state: "",
    country: "Brasil",
    max_distance_km: DEFAULT_DISTANCE,
    is_active: true,
  });

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const progressStep = PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS.SERVICE_AREA;

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadRegion() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const {
          data: { user },
          error: userError,
        } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (userError || !user) {
          router.replace("/login?role=professional");
          return;
        }

        const { data, error: regionError } = await supabase
          .from("professional_regions")
          .select("id, city, state, country, max_distance_km, is_active")
          .eq("professional_id", user.id)
          .eq("is_active", true)
          .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
          .limit(1)
          .maybeSingle();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (regionError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível carregar sua área de atendimento: ${regionError.message}`,
          );
          return;
        }

        if (data) {
          setRegion({
            id: data.id,
            city: data.city ?? "",
            state: data.state ?? "",
            country: data.country ?? "Brasil",
            max_distance_km:
              Number(data.max_distance_km) || DEFAULT_DISTANCE,
            is_active: Boolean(data.is_active),
          });
        }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao carregar sua área de atendimento.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadRegion();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  function updateField(
    field: keyof Region,
    value: string | number | boolean,
  ) {
    setRegion((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [field]: value,
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  }

  async function handleSave(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const city = region.city.trim();
    const state = region.state.trim().toUpperCase();
    const country = region.country.trim() || "Brasil";
    const distance = Number(region.max_distance_km);

    if (!city) {
      setError("Informe a cidade onde você atende.");
      return;
    }

    if (!state) {
      setError("Informe o estado.");
      return;
    }

    if (state.length !== 2) {
      setError("Informe a sigla do estado com duas letras. Ex.: SP.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(distance) || distance <= 0) {
      setError("Informe uma distância de atendimento válida.");
      return;
    }

    if (distance > 500) {
      setError("A distância máxima permitida é de 500 km.");
      return;
    }

    if (!country) {
      setError("Informe o país.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      const {
        data: { user },
        error: userError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (userError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login?role=professional");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("user_type")
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (
        profileError ||
        !profile ||
        profile.user_type !== "professional"
      ) {
        setError("Não foi possível validar seu perfil profissional.");
        return;
      }

      const { error: deactivateError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_regions")
        .update({
          is_active: false,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        })
        .eq("professional_id", user.id)
        .eq("is_active", true);

      if (deactivateError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível atualizar sua área de atendimento: ${deactivateError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const { error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_regions")
        .insert({
          professional_id: user.id,
          city,
          state,
          country,
          max_distance_km: distance,
          is_active: true,
        });

      if (insertError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível salvar sua área de atendimento: ${insertError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const nextStep = getNextProfessionalOnboardingStep(
        PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS.SERVICE_AREA,
      );

      const { error: onboardingError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .update({
          professional_onboarding_step: nextStep,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        })
        .eq("id", user.id);

      if (onboardingError) {
        setError(
          `Área salva, mas não foi possível atualizar o progresso: ${onboardingError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      setMessage("Área de atendimento salva com sucesso.");

      window.setTimeout(() => {
        router.push(getProfessionalOnboardingRoute(nextStep));
      }, 700);
    } catch {
      setError(
        "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao salvar sua área de atendimento.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[85vh] max-w-2xl flex-col justify-center">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <Image
            src="/logo-vemgo.png"
            alt="VemGO"
            width={360}
            height={200}
            priority
            className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
          />

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-400">
              Etapa {progressStep} de {TOTAL_STEPS}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-9 gap-1.5"
            role="progressbar"
            aria-label="Progresso do cadastro"
            aria-valuemin={1}
            aria-valuemax={TOTAL_STEPS}
            aria-valuenow={progressStep}
          >
            {Array.from({ length: TOTAL_STEPS }).map((_, index) => {
              const step = index + 1;

              return (
                <div
                  key={step}
                  className={`h-1.5 rounded-full ${
                    step <= progressStep
                      ? "bg-orange-500"
                      : "bg-zinc-800"
                  }`}
                />
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-8">
            <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-orange-400">
              Onde você atende
            </p>

            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
              Defina sua área de atendimento
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
              Informe sua cidade principal e a distância máxima que aceita
              atender. Essas informações ajudam a encontrar oportunidades
              compatíveis com sua região.
            </p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-red-900/60 bg-red-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-300"
            >
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {message && (
            <div
              role="status"
              aria-live="polite"
              className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-green-400"
            >
              {message}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-zinc-800" />
              <div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-zinc-800" />
              <div className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-zinc-800" />
              <div className="h-20 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-zinc-800" />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="space-y-6">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="city"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
                >
                  Cidade principal
                </label>

                <input
                  id="city"
                  name="city"
                  type="text"
                  value={region.city}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateField("city", event.target.value)
                  }
                  placeholder="Ex.: Jaú"
                  autoComplete="address-level2"
                  required
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-orange-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-60"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="state"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
                >
                  Estado (UF)
                </label>

                <input
                  id="state"
                  name="state"
                  type="text"
                  value={region.state}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateField("state", event.target.value.toUpperCase())
                  }
                  placeholder="Ex.: SP"
                  maxLength={2}
                  autoComplete="address-level1"
                  required
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 uppercase text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-orange-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-60"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="country"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
                >
                  País
                </label>

                <input
                  id="country"
                  name="country"
                  type="text"
                  value={region.country}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateField("country", event.target.value)
                  }
                  autoComplete="country-name"
                  required
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-orange-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-60"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                  <label
                    htmlFor="distance"
                    className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    Distância máxima de atendimento
                  </label>

                  <span className="shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400">
                    {region.max_distance_km} km
                  </span>
                </div>

                <input
                  id="distance"
                  name="distance"
                  type="range"
                  min={1}
                  max={500}
                  step={1}
                  value={region.max_distance_km}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateField(
                      "max_distance_km",
                      Number(event.target.value),
                    )
                  }
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full cursor-pointer accent-orange-500 disabled:opacity-60"
                />

                <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  <span>1 km</span>
                  <span>500 km</span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-4">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200">
                    Como funciona?
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                    A distância informada será utilizada como referência
                    para encontrar oportunidades de serviços na sua região.
                    A disponibilidade de cada oportunidade ainda dependerá
                    das regras da plataforma.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    router.push(
                      "/cadastro-profissional/disponibilidade",
                    )
                  }
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Voltar
                </button>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar e continuar"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </section>

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-600">
          Você poderá atualizar sua área de atendimento posteriormente.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: app\cadastro-profissional\competencias\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Category = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
};

type Service = {
  id: string;
  category_id: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description: string | null;
};

const TOTAL_STEPS = 9;
const CURRENT_STEP = 5;

export default function ProfessionalCompetenciesPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [services, setServices] = useState<Service[]>([]);
  const [selectedServices, setSelectedServices] = useState<Set<string>>(
    new Set(),
  );

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadData() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const {
          data: { user },
          error: authError,
        } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (authError || !user) {
          router.replace(
            "/login?redirect=/cadastro-profissional/competencias",
          );
          return;
        }

        const [
          categoriesResult,
          servicesResult,
          professionalServicesResult,
        ] = await Promise.all([
          supabase
            .from("categories")
            .select("id, name, slug")
            .eq("is_active", true)
            .order("display_order", { ascending: true })
            .order("name", { ascending: true }),

          supabase
            .from("services")
            .select("id, category_id, code, name, slug, description")
            .eq("is_active", true)
            .order("display_order", { ascending: true })
            .order("name", { ascending: true }),

          supabase
            .from("professional_services")
            .select("service_id")
            .eq("professional_id", user.id)
            .eq("is_active", true),
        ]);

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (categoriesResult.error || servicesResult.error) {
          setError("Não foi possível carregar os serviços disponíveis.");
          return;
        }

        if (professionalServicesResult.error) {
          setError("Não foi possível carregar suas competências salvas.");
          return;
        }

        setCategories(categoriesResult.data ?? []);
        setServices(servicesResult.data ?? []);
        setSelectedServices(
          new Set(
            (professionalServicesResult.data ?? []).map(
              (item) => item.service_id,
            ),
          ),
        );
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError("Ocorreu um erro ao carregar os serviços. Tente novamente.");
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadData();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const filteredServices = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    return services.filter((service) => {
      const matchesCategory = selectedCategory
        ? service.category_id === selectedCategory
        : true;

      const searchableText =
        `${service.code} ${service.name} ${service.description ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch = normalizedSearch
        ? searchableText.includes(normalizedSearch)
        : true;

      return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [search, selectedCategory, services]);

  function toggleService(serviceId: string) {
    setSelectedServices((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);

      if (next.has(serviceId)) {
        next.delete(serviceId);
      } else {
        next.add(serviceId);
      }

      return next;
    });

    setError("");
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (selectedServices.size === 0) {
      setError("Selecione pelo menos um serviço que você realiza.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      const {
        data: { user },
        error: authError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (authError || !user) {
        router.replace(
          "/login?redirect=/cadastro-profissional/competencias",
        );
        return;
      }

      const { error: deactivateError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_services")
        .update({ is_active: false })
        .eq("professional_id", user.id);

      if (deactivateError) {
        setError("Não foi possível atualizar suas competências.");
        return;
      }

      const rows = Array.from(selectedServices).map((serviceId) => ({
        professional_id: user.id,
        service_id: serviceId,
        is_active: true,
      }));

      const { error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_services")
        .upsert(rows, {
          onConflict: "professional_id,service_id",
        });

      if (insertError) {
        setError("Não foi possível salvar suas competências.");
        return;
      }

      router.push("/cadastro-profissional/precos");
    } catch {
      setError("Ocorreu um erro ao salvar suas competências. Tente novamente.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <header className="mx-auto mb-8 max-w-md text-center">
          <Image
            src="/logo-vemgo.png"
            alt="VemGO"
            width={360}
            height={200}
            className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            priority
          />

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-400">
              Etapa {CURRENT_STEP} de {TOTAL_STEPS}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            className="mt-3 flex gap-1.5"
            role="progressbar"
            aria-label="Progresso do cadastro"
            aria-valuemin={1}
            aria-valuemax={TOTAL_STEPS}
            aria-valuenow={CURRENT_STEP}
          >
            {Array.from({ length: TOTAL_STEPS }).map((_, index) => (
              <div
                key={index}
                className={`h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full ${
                  index < CURRENT_STEP ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-zinc-800"
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="mt-8">
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
              Quais serviços você realiza?
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
              Selecione todos os serviços que você está preparado para
              realizar. Os serviços disponíveis são definidos pela VemGO.
            </p>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-40 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-6">
              <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
                Carregando serviços...
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[240px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                <aside className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-3">
                  <p className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
                    Categorias
                  </p>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedCategory("")}
                      className={`w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                        !selectedCategory
                          ? "bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400"
                          : "text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 hover:text-white"
                      }`}
                    >
                      Todas as categorias
                    </button>

                    {categories.map((category) => (
                      <button
                        key={category.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(category.id)}
                        className={`w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium transition ${
                          selectedCategory === category.id
                            ? "bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400"
                            : "text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 hover:text-white"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {category.name}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </aside>

                <div className="min-w-0 space-y-5">
                  <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <label
                          htmlFor="serviceSearch"
                          className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200"
                        >
                          Buscar serviço
                        </label>

                        <input
                          id="serviceSearch"
                          type="search"
                          placeholder="Ex.: instalação de tomada"
                          value={search}
                          onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-zinc-500 outline-none transition focus:border-orange-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-orange-500"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/5 px-4 py-3 sm:min-w-32">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                            Serviços selecionados
                          </p>
                          <p className="mt-0.5 text-lg font-bold text-orange-400">
                            {selectedServices.size}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </section>

                  {filteredServices.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                      {filteredServices.map((service) => {
                        const selected = selectedServices.has(service.id);

                        return (
                          <button
                            key={service.id}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => toggleService(service.id)}
                            aria-pressed={selected}
                            className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition ${
                              selected
                                ? "border-orange-500/50 bg-orange-500/10"
                                : "border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 hover:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900"
                            }`}
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                              <span
                                aria-hidden="true"
                                className={`mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-xs font-bold ${
                                  selected
                                    ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-500 text-black"
                                    : "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 text-transparent"
                                }`}
                              >
                                ✓
                              </span>

                              <div className="min-w-0">
                                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500">
                                  {service.code}
                                </p>

                                <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-white">
                                  {service.name}
                                </p>

                                {service.description && (
                                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">
                                    {service.description}
                                  </p>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-8 text-center">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-zinc-300">
                        Nenhum serviço encontrado.
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
                        Tente outra busca ou selecione outra categoria.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {error && (
                    <div
                      role="alert"
                      className="rounded-xl border border-red-900/60 bg-red-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300"
                    >
                      {error}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        router.push("/cadastro-profissional/identidade")
                      }
                      disabled={saving}
                      className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-zinc-900 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      Voltar
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={saving || loading}
                      className="rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar e continuar"}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cadastro-profissional\disponibilidade\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Day = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  start: string;
  end: string;
};

const initialDays: Day[] = [
  { id: 1, name: "Segunda-feira", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "18:00" },
  { id: 2, name: "Terça-feira", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "18:00" },
  { id: 3, name: "Quarta-feira", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "18:00" },
  { id: 4, name: "Quinta-feira", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "18:00" },
  { id: 5, name: "Sexta-feira", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "18:00" },
  { id: 6, name: "Sábado", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "12:00" },
  { id: 0, name: "Domingo", enabled: false, start: "08:00", end: "12:00" },
];

const TOTAL_STEPS = 9;
const CURRENT_STEP = 7;

export default function CadastroProfissionalDisponibilidadePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [days, setDays] = useState<Day[]>(initialDays);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadAvailability() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const {
          data: { user },
          error: userError,
        } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (userError || !user) {
          router.replace("/login");
          return;
        }

        const { data, error: availabilityError } = await supabase
          .from("professional_availability")
          .select("day_of_week,start_time,end_time,is_active")
          .eq("professional_id", user.id);

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (availabilityError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível carregar sua disponibilidade: ${availabilityError.message}`,
          );
          return;
        }

        if (data && data.length > 0) {
          setDays((current) =>
            current.map((day) => {
              const saved = data.find(
                (item) => item.day_of_week === day.id,
              );

              if (!saved) return day;

              return {
                ...day,
                enabled: saved.is_active,
                start: saved.start_time?.slice(0, 5) || day.start,
                end: saved.end_time?.slice(0, 5) || day.end,
              };
            }),
          );
        }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao carregar sua disponibilidade.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadAvailability();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  function toggleDay(dayId: number) {
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    setDays((current) =>
      current.map((day) =>
        day.id === dayId
          ? { ...day, enabled: !day.enabled }
          : day,
      ),
    );
  }

  function updateTime(
    dayId: number,
    field: "start" | "end",
    value: string,
  ) {
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    setDays((current) =>
      current.map((day) =>
        day.id === dayId
          ? { ...day, [field]: value }
          : day,
      ),
    );
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const selectedDays = days.filter((day) => day.enabled);

    if (selectedDays.length === 0) {
      setError("Selecione pelo menos um dia de atendimento.");
      return;
    }

    for (const day of selectedDays) {
      if (!day.start || !day.end) {
        setError(
          `Informe o horário de atendimento de ${day.name}.`,
        );
        return;
      }

      if (day.start >= day.end) {
        setError(
          `O horário final precisa ser maior que o inicial em ${day.name}.`,
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      const {
        data: { user },
        error: userError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (userError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("user_type, status")
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (
        profileError ||
        !profile ||
        profile.user_type !== "professional"
      ) {
        setError("Não foi possível validar seu perfil profissional.");
        return;
      }

      if (profile.status === "blocked") {
        setError(
          "Seu perfil está bloqueado. Entre em contato com o suporte.",
        );
        return;
      }

      const { error: deleteError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_availability")
        .delete()
        .eq("professional_id", user.id);

      if (deleteError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível atualizar sua disponibilidade: ${deleteError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const rows = selectedDays.map((day) => ({
        professional_id: user.id,
        day_of_week: day.id,
        start_time: day.start,
        end_time: day.end,
        is_active: true,
      }));

      const { error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from("professional_availability")
        .insert(rows);

      if (insertError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível salvar sua disponibilidade: ${insertError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const { error: stepError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .update({ professional_onboarding_step: 8 })
        .eq("id", user.id);

      if (stepError) {
        setMessage(
          "Disponibilidade salva. Não foi possível atualizar o progresso do cadastro.",
        );
        return;
      }

      setMessage("Disponibilidade salva com sucesso.");

      window.setTimeout(() => {
        router.push("/cadastro-profissional/area-atendimento");
      }, 700);
    } catch {
      setError(
        "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao salvar sua disponibilidade. Tente novamente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[85vh] max-w-3xl flex-col justify-center">
        <header className="mb-8 text-center">
          <Image
            src="/logo-vemgo.png"
            alt="VemGO"
            width={360}
            height={200}
            className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            priority
          />

          <div className="mx-auto mt-6 flex max-w-2xl items-center justify-between">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-400">
              Etapa {CURRENT_STEP} de {TOTAL_STEPS}
            </p>

          </div>

          <div
            className="mx-auto mt-3 flex max-w-2xl gap-1.5"
            role="progressbar"
            aria-label="Progresso do cadastro"
            aria-valuemin={1}
            aria-valuemax={TOTAL_STEPS}
            aria-valuenow={CURRENT_STEP}
          >
            {Array.from({ length: TOTAL_STEPS }).map((_, index) => (
              <div
                key={index}
                className={`h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full ${
                  index < CURRENT_STEP
                    ? "bg-orange-500"
                    : "bg-zinc-800"
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-8 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Sua disponibilidade
          </h1>

          <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
            Informe os dias e horários em que você normalmente está
            disponível para receber serviços pela VemGO.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-7">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-32 items-center justify-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
                Carregando sua disponibilidade...
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-6">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
                  Dias e horários de atendimento
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                  Marque os dias em que pode atender e defina o horário
                  inicial e final de cada um.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {days.map((day) => (
                  <div
                    key={day.id}
                    className={`rounded-xl border p-4 transition ${
                      day.enabled
                        ? "border-orange-500/60 bg-orange-500/5"
                        : "border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                      <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={day.enabled}
                          onChange={() => toggleDay(day.id)}
                          disabled={saving}
                          className="h-5 w-5 accent-orange-500"
                        />

                        <span className="font-semibold">
                          {day.name}
                        </span>
                      </label>

                      {day.enabled && (
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr] items-end gap-2 sm:max-w-[310px]">
                          <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label
                              htmlFor={`start-${day.id}`}
                              className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-400"
                            >
                              Início
                            </label>

                            <input
                              id={`start-${day.id}`}
                              type="time"
                              value={day.start}
                              onChange={(event) =>
                                updateTime(
                                  day.id,
                                  "start",
                                  event.target.value,
                                )
                              }
                              disabled={saving}
                              required
                              className="w-full min-w-0 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-orange-500 disabled:opacity-60"
                            />
                          </div>

                          <span className="pb-3 text-xs text-zinc-500">
                            até
                          </span>

                          <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label
                              htmlFor={`end-${day.id}`}
                              className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-400"
                            >
                              Fim
                            </label>

                            <input
                              id={`end-${day.id}`}
                              type="time"
                              value={day.end}
                              onChange={(event) =>
                                updateTime(
                                  day.id,
                                  "end",
                                  event.target.value,
                                )
                              }
                              disabled={saving}
                              required
                              className="w-full min-w-0 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-2 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-orange-500 disabled:opacity-60"
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/5 px-4 py-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                  Importante
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                  Informe horários em que realmente consegue atender.
                  Sua disponibilidade ajuda a VemGO a encaminhar
                  oportunidades compatíveis com sua agenda.
                </p>
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div
                  role="alert"
                  className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-900/60 bg-red-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-300"
                >
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              {message && (
                <div
                  role="status"
                  aria-live="polite"
                  className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-green-400"
                >
                  {message}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    router.push("/cadastro-profissional/precos")
                  }
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Voltar
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleSave}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar e continuar"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </section>

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-600">
          Você poderá atualizar sua disponibilidade posteriormente.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cadastro-profissional\endereco\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";
import {
  getNextProfessionalOnboardingStep,
  getProfessionalOnboardingRoute,
  PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS,
} from "@/lib/professional-onboarding";

const inputClassName =
  "w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-500 focus:border-orange-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500/20";

const labelClassName =
  "mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200";

type ViaCepResponse = {
  logradouro?: string;
  bairro?: string;
  localidade?: string;
  uf?: string;
  erro?: boolean;
};

export default function CadastroProfissionalEnderecoPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState("");
  const [label, setLabel] = useState("Principal");
  const [postalCode, setPostalCode] = useState("");
  const [street, setStreet] = useState("");
  const [number, setNumber] = useState("");
  const [complement, setComplement] = useState("");
  const [neighborhood, setNeighborhood] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [state, setState] = useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchingCep, setSearchingCep] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadAddress() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const {
        data: { user },
        error: userError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (userError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login?role=professional");
        return;
      }

      setUserId(user.id);

      const { data: address, error: addressError } = await supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .select(
          "id, label, recipient_name, postal_code, street, number, complement, neighborhood, city, state, country",
        )
        .eq("profile_id", user.id)
        .order("is_default", { ascending: false })
        .order("created_at", { ascending: true })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (addressError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível carregar seu endereço: ${addressError.message}`,
        );
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (address) {
        setLabel(address.label || "Principal");

        const digits = (address.postal_code || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
        setPostalCode(
          digits.length > 5
            ? `${digits.slice(0, 5)}-${digits.slice(5, 8)}`
            : digits,
        );

        setStreet(address.street || "");
        setNumber(address.number || "");
        setComplement(address.complement || "");
        setNeighborhood(address.neighborhood || "");
        setCity(address.city || "");
        setState(address.state || "");
      }

      setLoading(false);
    }

    void loadAddress();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  async function handlePostalCodeChange(value: string) {
    const digits = value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 8);

    const formatted =
      digits.length > 5
        ? `${digits.slice(0, 5)}-${digits.slice(5)}`
        : digits;

    setPostalCode(formatted);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    if (digits.length !== 8) return;

    setSearchingCep(true);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `https://viacep.com.br/ws/${digits}/json/`,
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("Não foi possível consultar o CEP.");
      }

      const data: ViaCepResponse = await response.json();

      if (data.erro) {
        setError("CEP não encontrado. Confira o número informado.");
        return;
      }

      setStreet(data.logradouro || "");
      setNeighborhood(data.bairro || "");
      setCity(data.localidade || "");
      setState((data.uf || "").toUpperCase());
      setMessage("Endereço localizado pelo CEP. Confira os dados.");
    } catch {
      setError(
        "Não foi possível consultar o CEP. Preencha o endereço manualmente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSearchingCep(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSave(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const normalizedLabel = label.trim() || "Principal";
    const normalizedPostalCode = postalCode.replace(/\D/g, "");
    const normalizedStreet = street.trim();
    const normalizedNumber = number.trim();
    const normalizedComplement = complement.trim();
    const normalizedNeighborhood = neighborhood.trim();
    const normalizedCity = city.trim();
    const normalizedState = state.trim().toUpperCase();

    if (!userId) {
      setError("Usuário não identificado. Entre novamente na sua conta.");
      return;
    }

    if (normalizedPostalCode.length !== 8) {
      setError("Informe um CEP válido com 8 dígitos.");
      return;
    }

    if (!normalizedStreet) {
      setError("Informe a rua ou avenida.");
      return;
    }

    if (!normalizedNumber) {
      setError("Informe o número do endereço.");
      return;
    }

    if (!normalizedNeighborhood) {
      setError("Informe o bairro.");
      return;
    }

    if (!normalizedCity) {
      setError("Informe a cidade.");
      return;
    }

    if (normalizedState.length !== 2) {
      setError("Informe a UF com duas letras.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      const { data: existingAddress, error: existingError } =
        await supabase
          .from("addresses")
          .select("id")
          .eq("profile_id", userId)
          .order("is_default", { ascending: false })
          .order("created_at", { ascending: true })
          .limit(1)
          .maybeSingle();

      if (existingError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível verificar seu endereço: ${existingError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const addressPayload = {
        profile_id: userId,
        label: normalizedLabel,
        recipient_name: "",
        postal_code: normalizedPostalCode,
        street: normalizedStreet,
        number: normalizedNumber,
        complement: normalizedComplement || null,
        neighborhood: normalizedNeighborhood,
        city: normalizedCity,
        state: normalizedState,
        country: "BR",
        is_default: true,
      };

      if (existingAddress?.id) {
        const { error: updateError } = await supabase
          .from("addresses")
          .update(addressPayload)
          .eq("id", existingAddress.id)
          .eq("profile_id", userId);

        if (updateError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível salvar seu endereço: ${updateError.message}`,
          );
          return;
        }
      } else {
        const { error: insertError } = await supabase
          .from("addresses")
          .insert(addressPayload);

        if (insertError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível salvar seu endereço: ${insertError.message}`,
          );
          return;
        }
      }

      const nextStep = getNextProfessionalOnboardingStep(
        PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS.ADDRESS,
      );

      const { error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .update({
          professional_onboarding_step: nextStep,
        })
        .eq("id", userId)
        .eq("user_type", "professional");

      if (profileError) {
        setError(
          `Endereço salvo, mas não foi possível atualizar o progresso: ${profileError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      setMessage("Endereço salvo com sucesso.");

      window.setTimeout(() => {
        router.replace(getProfessionalOnboardingRoute(nextStep));
      }, 700);
    } catch {
      setError(
        "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao salvar o endereço. Tente novamente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-8 text-white sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-md flex-col justify-center">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <div className="text-center">
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              priority
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-400">
              Etapa 2 de 9
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            className="mt-3 flex gap-1.5"
            role="progressbar"
            aria-label="Progresso do cadastro profissional"
            aria-valuemin={0}
            aria-valuemax={9}
            aria-valuenow={2}
          >
            {Array.from({ length: 9 }).map((_, index) => (
              <div
                key={index}
                className={`h-1.5 min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-full ${
                  index < 2 ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-zinc-800"
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="mb-6">

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
            Seu endereço
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
            Informe seu endereço principal. Usaremos essas informações
            para configurar sua área de atendimento.
          </p>
        </section>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-8 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
              Carregando seu endereço...
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form
            onSubmit={handleSave}
            className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6"
          >
            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="label" className={labelClassName}>
                  Identificação do endereço
                </label>
                <input
                  id="label"
                  name="label"
                  type="text"
                  value={label}
                  onChange={(event) => setLabel(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Principal"
                  className={inputClassName}
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="postalCode" className={labelClassName}>
                  CEP
                </label>
                <input
                  id="postalCode"
                  name="postalCode"
                  type="text"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  autoComplete="postal-code"
                  value={postalCode}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    void handlePostalCodeChange(event.target.value)
                  }
                  placeholder="00000-000"
                  maxLength={9}
                  className={inputClassName}
                  required
                />
                {searchingCep && (
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-400">
                    Consultando CEP...
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="street" className={labelClassName}>
                  Rua / Avenida
                </label>
                <input
                  id="street"
                  name="street"
                  type="text"
                  autoComplete="address-line1"
                  value={street}
                  onChange={(event) => setStreet(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Nome da rua ou avenida"
                  className={inputClassName}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="number" className={labelClassName}>
                    Número
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="number"
                    name="number"
                    type="text"
                    value={number}
                    onChange={(event) => setNumber(event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Nº"
                    className={inputClassName}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="complement" className={labelClassName}>
                    Complemento
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="complement"
                    name="complement"
                    type="text"
                    autoComplete="address-line2"
                    value={complement}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setComplement(event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="Opcional"
                    className={inputClassName}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="neighborhood" className={labelClassName}>
                  Bairro
                </label>
                <input
                  id="neighborhood"
                  name="neighborhood"
                  type="text"
                  autoComplete="address-level3"
                  value={neighborhood}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    setNeighborhood(event.target.value)
                  }
                  placeholder="Nome do bairro"
                  className={inputClassName}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div className="col-span-2">
                  <label htmlFor="city" className={labelClassName}>
                    Cidade
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="city"
                    name="city"
                    type="text"
                    autoComplete="address-level2"
                    value={city}
                    onChange={(event) => setCity(event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Sua cidade"
                    className={inputClassName}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="state" className={labelClassName}>
                    UF
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="state"
                    name="state"
                    type="text"
                    autoComplete="address-level1"
                    value={state}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setState(
                        event.target.value.toUpperCase().slice(0, 2),
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="SP"
                    maxLength={2}
                    className={inputClassName}
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div
                  role="alert"
                  className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-300"
                >
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              {message && (
                <div
                  role="status"
                  className="rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-green-400"
                >
                  {message}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving || searchingCep}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {saving ? "Salvando endereço..." : "Salvar e continuar"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-600">
          Seus dados de endereço são utilizados para configurar
          seu cadastro profissional na VemGO.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cadastro-profissional\foto\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState, type ChangeEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

const ALLOWED_TYPES = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"];

export default function CadastroProfissionalFotoPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState("");
  const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState("");
  const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentAvatarUrl, setCurrentAvatarUrl] = useState("");
  const [currentImageUrl, setCurrentImageUrl] = useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadProfile() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const {
          data: { user },
          error: userError,
        } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (userError || !user) {
          router.replace("/login");
          return;
        }

        setUserId(user.id);

        const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .select("avatar_url")
          .eq("id", user.id)
          .maybeSingle();

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (profileError) {
          setError("Não foi possível carregar os dados do seu perfil.");
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }

        const avatarPath = profile?.avatar_url ?? "";

        if (avatarPath) {
          setCurrentAvatarUrl(avatarPath);

          if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(avatarPath)) {
            setCurrentImageUrl(avatarPath);
          } else {
            const { data: signedData, error: signedError } =
              await supabase.storage
                .from("profile-photos")
                .createSignedUrl(avatarPath, 60 * 60);

            if (cancelled) return;

            if (!signedError && signedData?.signedUrl) {
              setCurrentImageUrl(signedData.signedUrl);
            } else {
              setError(
                "A foto está cadastrada, mas não foi possível carregar sua imagem. Você pode selecionar outra foto para substituí-la.",
              );
            }
          }
        }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError("Não foi possível carregar seu perfil. Tente novamente.");
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadProfile();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (previewUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
      }
    };
  }, [previewUrl]);

  function handleFileChange(event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const file = event.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
      setError("Escolha uma imagem JPG, PNG ou WEBP.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
      setError("A foto precisa ter no máximo 5 MB.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    setPreviewUrl(URL.createObjectURL(file));
    setSelectedFile(file);
  }

  async function handleUpload() {
    if (!userId) {
      setError("Usuário não identificado.");
      return;
    }

    if (!selectedFile) {
      setError(
        currentAvatarUrl
          ? "Sua foto já está cadastrada. Escolha outra somente se desejar substituí-la."
          : "Selecione uma foto para continuar.",
      );
      return;
    }

    setUploading(true);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const fileExtension =
        selectedFile.name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase() || "jpg";

      const filePath = `${userId}/profile.${fileExtension}`;

      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
        .from("profile-photos")
        .upload(filePath, selectedFile, {
          cacheControl: "3600",
          upsert: true,
          contentType: selectedFile.type,
        });

      if (uploadError) {
        setError(`Não foi possível enviar a foto: ${uploadError.message}`);
        return;
      }

      const { error: avatarError } = await supabase.rpc(
        "set_profile_avatar_url",
        {
          p_avatar_url: filePath,
        },
      );

      if (avatarError) {
        setError(
          `A foto foi enviada, mas não foi possível atualizar o perfil: ${avatarError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const { data: signedData } = await supabase.storage
        .from("profile-photos")
        .createSignedUrl(filePath, 60 * 60);

      const savedImageUrl = signedData?.signedUrl ?? previewUrl;

      setCurrentAvatarUrl(filePath);
      setCurrentImageUrl(savedImageUrl);
      setMessage("Foto salva com sucesso.");
      setSelectedFile(null);
      setPreviewUrl("");

      const input = document.getElementById(
        "profile-photo",
      ) as HTMLInputElement | null;

      if (input) input.value = "";

      window.setTimeout(() => {
        router.push("/cadastro-profissional/identidade");
      }, 1200);
    } catch {
      setError(
        "Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao salvar sua foto. Tente novamente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleSkip() {
    if (currentAvatarUrl) {
      router.push("/cadastro-profissional/identidade");
      return;
    }

    setError("A foto de perfil é obrigatória para continuar.");
  }

  const displayImageUrl = previewUrl || currentImageUrl;

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[85vh] max-w-md flex-col justify-center">
        <header className="mb-8 text-center">
          <Image
            src="/logo-vemgo.png"
            alt="VemGO"
            width={360}
            height={200}
            className="mx-auto h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            priority
          />

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-400">
              Etapa 3 de 9
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
