                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <aside className="space-y-6">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                Profissional
              </p>
              <h2 className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold">
                {professional?.full_name || (order.professional_id ? "Profissional selecionado" : "Aguardando seleção")}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                {appointment?.status === "scheduled"
                  ? "O profissional precisa responder à solicitação de horário."
                  : appointment?.status === "confirmed"
                    ? "O horário foi confirmado pelo profissional."
                    : appointment?.status === "cancelled"
                      ? "O horário solicitado não foi aceito. Escolha outra opção."
                      : "O status será atualizado conforme o andamento do atendimento."}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                Local do atendimento
              </p>
              <h2 className="mt-2 font-bold">{address?.label || "Endereço do serviço"}</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-300">
                {addressLine || "Endereço não disponível."}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                Próximos passos
              </p>
              <ol className="mt-4 space-y-4">
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-500 text-sm font-bold text-black">1</span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold">Horário solicitado</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">A data e a hora ficam registradas no pedido.</p>
                  </div>
                </li>
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className={`flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${appointment?.status === "confirmed" ? "bg-emerald-500 text-black" : "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300"}`}>2</span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold">Resposta do profissional</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">O profissional aceita ou recusa o horário.</p>
                  </div>
                </li>
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className={`flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${appointment?.status === "confirmed" ? "bg-emerald-500 text-black" : "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300"}`}>3</span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold">Atendimento</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">Se aceito, o horário fica confirmado.</p>
                  </div>
                </li>
              </ol>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\agendamento\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type OrderInfo = {
  id: string;
  request_id: string | null;
  client_id: string;
  professional_id: string;
  service_id: string;
  status: string;
  price: number;
};

type ServiceInfo = { name: string; code: string };
type ProfessionalInfo = { display_name: string | null };
type Availability = {
  day_of_week: number;
  start_time: string;
  end_time: string;
};
type Appointment = {
  id: string;
  starts_at: string;
  ends_at: string;
  status: string;
};

const SLOT_MINUTES = 60;
const DAYS_AHEAD = 21;

function localDateKey(date: Date) {
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function combineLocalDateTime(dateKey: string, time: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = dateKey.split("-").map(Number);
  const [hour, minute] = time.slice(0, 5).split(":").map(Number);
  return new Date(year, month - 1, day, hour, minute, 0, 0);
}

function formatDate(dateKey: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = dateKey.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    weekday: "long",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day, 12));
}

function formatTime(value: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(value);
}

function addMinutes(date: Date, minutes: number) {
  return new Date(date.getTime() + minutes * 60_000);
}

function ClienteAgendamentoContent() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const orderId = searchParams.get("orderId");
  const supabase = useMemo(() => createSupabaseBrowserClient(), []);

  const [order, setOrder] = useState<OrderInfo | null>(null);
  const [service, setService] = useState<ServiceInfo | null>(null);
  const [professional, setProfessional] = useState<ProfessionalInfo | null>(null);
  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState<Availability[]>([]);
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState<Appointment[]>([]);
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState("");
  const [selectedStart, setSelectedStart] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    if (!orderId) {
      setError("Pedido não informado. Abra o agendamento a partir de Meus serviços.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (authError || !user) {
      router.replace(`/login?redirect=/cliente/agendamento?orderId=${orderId}`);
      return;
    }

    const { data: orderData, error: orderError } = await supabase
      .from("orders")
      .select("id, request_id, client_id, professional_id, service_id, status, price")
      .eq("id", orderId)
      .eq("client_id", user.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (orderError || !orderData) {
      setError("Não foi possível localizar este pedido para sua conta.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (!orderData.professional_id) {
      setError("Este pedido ainda não tem um profissional selecionado.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const [
      { data: serviceData, error: serviceError },
      { data: professionalData, error: professionalError },
      { data: availabilityData, error: availabilityError },
      { data: appointmentData, error: appointmentError },
    ] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from("services").select("name, code").eq("id", orderData.service_id).maybeSingle(),
      supabase.from("professionals").select("display_name").eq("id", orderData.professional_id).maybeSingle(),
      supabase.from("professional_availability")
        .select("day_of_week, start_time, end_time")
        .eq("professional_id", orderData.professional_id)
        .eq("is_active", true)
        .order("day_of_week")
        .order("start_time"),
      supabase.from("appointments")
        .select("id, starts_at, ends_at, status")
        .eq("professional_id", orderData.professional_id)
        .in("status", ["scheduled", "confirmed", "in_progress"]),
    ]);

    if (serviceError || professionalError || availabilityError || appointmentError) {
      console.error("[VemGO] Erro ao carregar dados do agendamento", {
        serviceError, professionalError, availabilityError, appointmentError,
      });
      setError("Não foi possível carregar os horários. Tente novamente.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setOrder(orderData as OrderInfo);
    setService(serviceData as ServiceInfo | null);
    setProfessional(professionalData as ProfessionalInfo | null);
    setAvailability((availabilityData ?? []) as Availability[]);
    setAppointments((appointmentData ?? []) as Appointment[]);
    setLoading(false);
  }, [orderId, router, supabase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => void load(), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [load]);

  const dates = useMemo(() => {
    const result: { key: string; label: string; day: number }[] = [];
    const now = new Date();
    for (let offset = 0; offset < DAYS_AHEAD; offset++) {
      const date = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + offset);
      const key = localDateKey(date);
      if (availability.some((a) => a.day_of_week === date.getDay())) {
        result.push({ key, label: formatDate(key), day: date.getDay() });
      }
    }
    return result;
  }, [availability]);

  const slots = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedDate) return [];
    const [year, month, day] = selectedDate.split("-").map(Number);
    const weekday = new Date(year, month - 1, day, 12).getDay();
    const today = new Date();
    const result: { start: Date; end: Date; key: string }[] = [];

    for (const window of availability.filter((a) => a.day_of_week === weekday)) {
      const startText = window.start_time.slice(0, 5);
      const endText = window.end_time.slice(0, 5);
      let cursor = combineLocalDateTime(selectedDate, startText);
      const windowEnd = combineLocalDateTime(selectedDate, endText);

      while (addMinutes(cursor, SLOT_MINUTES) <= windowEnd) {
        const end = addMinutes(cursor, SLOT_MINUTES);
        const isFuture = cursor.getTime() > today.getTime();
        const collides = appointments.some((appointment) => {
          const existingStart = new Date(appointment.starts_at).getTime();
          const existingEnd = new Date(appointment.ends_at).getTime();
          return cursor.getTime() < existingEnd && end.getTime() > existingStart;
        });

        if (isFuture && !collides) {
          result.push({ start: new Date(cursor), end, key: cursor.toISOString() });
        }
        cursor = end;
      }
    }

    return result;
  }, [selectedDate, availability, appointments]);

  async function confirmAppointment() {
    if (!order || !selectedStart) {
      setError("Selecione uma data e um horário.");
      return;
    }

    const chosen = slots.find((slot) => slot.key === selectedStart);
    if (!chosen) {
      setError("Esse horário não está mais disponível. Atualize os horários e escolha outro.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    const startIso = chosen.start.toISOString();
    const endIso = chosen.end.toISOString();

    // A RPC valida o pedido, o profissional e a disponibilidade no servidor.
    const { error: bookingError } = await supabase.rpc("request_appointment_slot", {
      p_order_id: order.id,
      p_starts_at: startIso,
      p_ends_at: endIso,
    });

    if (bookingError) {
      console.error("[VemGO] Falha ao solicitar horário:", bookingError.message);
      setError(bookingError.message || "Não foi possível solicitar esse horário. Atualize e tente novamente.");
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }

    setSuccess("Horário solicitado com sucesso.");
    setSaving(false);
    await load();
    router.push(`/cliente/agendamento/${order.id}`);
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Carregando horários disponíveis...</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-6 py-5">
          <Image src="/logo-vemgo.png" alt="VemGO" width={360} height={200} className="h-auto w-[170px] object-contain" priority />
          <button onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/servicos")} className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-400 hover:text-green-400">
            ← Meus serviços
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
        <span className="rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green-400">
          AGENDAMENTO
        </span>
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold">Escolha data e horário</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">Selecione um horário disponível na agenda do profissional.</p>

        {error && <div role="alert" className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}
        {success && <div role="status" className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-green-300">{success}</div>}

        {order && (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-5">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">Serviço</p>
            <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold">{service?.name ?? "Serviço VemGO"}</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">{service?.code ?? ""}</p>
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-zinc-300">Profissional: <strong>{professional?.display_name?.trim() || "Profissional VemGO"}</strong></p>
          </div>
        )}

        {availability.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-yellow-500/30 bg-yellow-500/5 p-5 text-sm text-yellow-200">
            Este profissional ainda não cadastrou horários disponíveis. Entre em contato com o profissional ou tente novamente mais tarde.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[0.8fr_1.2fr]">
            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 p-5">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">1. Escolha o dia</h2>
              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-2">
                {dates.map((date) => (
                  <button key={date.key} onClick={() => { setSelectedDate(date.key); setSelectedStart(""); }}
                    className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm capitalize transition ${selectedDate === date.key ? "border-green-500 bg-green-500/10 text-green-300" : "border-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-600"}`}>
                    {date.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
                {dates.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Não há dias disponíveis nos próximos {DAYS_AHEAD} dias.</p>}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 p-5">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">2. Escolha o horário</h2>
              {!selectedDate ? (
                <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-zinc-500">Escolha um dia para visualizar os horários.</p>
              ) : slots.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-zinc-500">Não há horários livres neste dia. Escolha outra data.</p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                  {slots.map((slot) => (
                    <button key={slot.key} onClick={() => setSelectedStart(slot.key)}
                      className={`rounded-xl border px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition ${selectedStart === slot.key ? "border-green-500 bg-green-500 text-black" : "border-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 hover:border-green-500"}`}>
                      {formatTime(slot.start)}–{formatTime(slot.end)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              <button disabled={!selectedStart || saving} onClick={() => void confirmAppointment()}
                className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40">
                {saving ? "Confirmando horário..." : "Confirmar agendamento"}
              </button>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default function ClienteAgendamentoPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Carregando agendamento...</p>
        </main>
      }
    >
      <ClienteAgendamentoContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\enderecos\novo\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";

import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/Input";
import Spinner from "@/components/ui/Spinner";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type AddressForm = {
  label: string;
  recipient_name: string;
  postal_code: string;
  street: string;
  number: string;
  complement: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
};

const initialForm: AddressForm = {
  label: "",
  recipient_name: "",
  postal_code: "",
  street: "",
  number: "",
  complement: "",
  neighborhood: "",
  city: "",
  state: "",
};

function NovoEnderecoPageContent() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const addressId = searchParams.get("id");
  const isEditing = Boolean(addressId);

  const [form, setForm] = useState<AddressForm>(initialForm);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [searchingCep, setSearchingCep] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [cepMessage, setCepMessage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadAddress() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {
        router.replace("/login?redirect=/cliente/enderecos/novo");
        return;
      }

      if (!addressId) {
        const { data: profile } = await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .select("full_name")
          .eq("id", user.id)
          .maybeSingle();

        setForm((current) => ({
          ...current,
          recipient_name: profile?.full_name ?? "",
        }));

        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data, error: addressError } = await supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .select(
          "id, label, recipient_name, postal_code, street, number, complement, neighborhood, city, state",
        )
        .eq("id", addressId)
        .eq("profile_id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (addressError || !data) {
        setError("Não foi possível encontrar este endereço.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setForm({
        label: data.label ?? "",
        recipient_name: data.recipient_name ?? "",
        postal_code: data.postal_code ?? "",
        street: data.street ?? "",
        number: data.number ?? "",
        complement: data.complement ?? "",
        neighborhood: data.neighborhood ?? "",
        city: data.city ?? "",
        state: data.state ?? "",
      });

      setLoading(false);
    }

    void loadAddress();
  }, [addressId, router, supabase]);

  function updateField(field: keyof AddressForm, value: string) {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [field]: value,
    }));
  }

  function formatCep(value: string) {
    const digits = value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 8);

    if (digits.length > 5) {
      return `${digits.slice(0, 5)}-${digits.slice(5)}`;
    }

    return digits;
  }

  async function handleCepBlur() {
    const cep = form.postal_code.replace(/\D/g, "");

    if (cep.length !== 8) {
      return;
    }

    setSearchingCep(true);
    setCepMessage("");
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `https://viacep.com.br/ws/${cep}/json/`,
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("Falha ao consultar CEP.");
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      if (data.erro) {
        setCepMessage("CEP não encontrado.");
        setSearchingCep(false);
        return;
      }

      setForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        street: data.logradouro ?? current.street,
        neighborhood: data.bairro ?? current.neighborhood,
        city: data.localidade ?? current.city,
        state: data.uf ?? current.state,
      }));

      setCepMessage("Endereço localizado automaticamente.");
    } catch {
      setCepMessage(
        "Não foi possível consultar o CEP. Preencha o endereço manualmente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSearchingCep(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmit(
    event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");

    const {
      label,
      recipient_name,
      postal_code,
      street,
      number,
      complement,
      neighborhood,
      city,
      state,
    } = form;

    if (!label.trim()) {
      setError("Informe um nome para este endereço.");
      return;
    }

    if (!recipient_name.trim()) {
      setError("Informe o nome de quem receberá o atendimento.");
      return;
    }

    if (postal_code.replace(/\D/g, "").length !== 8) {
      setError("Informe um CEP válido.");
      return;
    }

    if (!street.trim()) {
      setError("Informe a rua.");
      return;
    }

    if (!number.trim()) {
      setError("Informe o número.");
      return;
    }

    if (!neighborhood.trim()) {
      setError("Informe o bairro.");
      return;
    }

    if (!city.trim()) {
      setError("Informe a cidade.");
      return;
    }

    if (!state.trim()) {
      setError("Informe o estado.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login?redirect=/cliente/enderecos/novo");
      return;
    }

    const addressData = {
      profile_id: user.id,
      label: label.trim(),
      recipient_name: recipient_name.trim(),
      postal_code: postal_code.replace(/\D/g, ""),
      street: street.trim(),
      number: number.trim(),
      complement: complement.trim() || null,
      neighborhood: neighborhood.trim(),
      city: city.trim(),
      state: state.trim().toUpperCase(),
      country: "BR",
    };

    if (isEditing && addressId) {
      const { error: updateError } = await supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .update(addressData)
        .eq("id", addressId)
        .eq("profile_id", user.id);

      if (updateError) {
        setError("Não foi possível atualizar o endereço.");
        setSaving(false);
        return;
      }
    } else {
      const { data: existingDefault } = await supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .select("id")
        .eq("profile_id", user.id)
        .eq("is_default", true)
        .maybeSingle();

      const { error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .insert({
          ...addressData,
          is_default: !existingDefault,
        });

      if (insertError) {
        setError("Não foi possível cadastrar o endereço.");
        setSaving(false);
        return;
      }
    }

    router.push("/cliente/enderecos");
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
        <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center">
          <Spinner
            size="lg"
            variant="client"
            label="Carregando endereço..."
          />
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-900 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-4xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-6 py-5">
          <Link href="/cliente" aria-label="VemGO">
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              priority
            />
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/cliente/enderecos"
            className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-emerald-400"
          >
            Voltar
          </Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-8 sm:py-10">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-400">
            Meus endereços
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
            {isEditing ? "Editar endereço" : "Novo endereço"}
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-500">
            Informe o local onde o atendimento será realizado.
          </p>
        </div>

        <Card padding="lg">
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            {error && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-400"
              >
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Input
                id="label"
                label="Nome do endereço"
                placeholder="Ex.: Casa, Trabalho"
                value={form.label}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField("label", event.target.value)
                }
                required
              />

              <Input
                id="recipient_name"
                label="Nome do responsável"
                placeholder="Quem receberá o profissional"
                value={form.recipient_name}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField(
                    "recipient_name",
                    event.target.value,
                  )
                }
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-[180px_1fr]">
              <Input
                id="postal_code"
                label="CEP"
                placeholder="00000-000"
                value={form.postal_code}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField(
                    "postal_code",
                    formatCep(event.target.value),
                  )
                }
                onBlur={handleCepBlur}
                inputMode="numeric"
                maxLength={9}
                required
              />

              <div className="flex items-end">
                {searchingCep ? (
                  <Spinner
                    size="sm"
                    variant="client"
                    label="Consultando CEP..."
                  />
                ) : cepMessage ? (
                  <p className="pb-3 text-xs text-zinc-500">
                    {cepMessage}
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <p className="pb-3 text-xs text-zinc-600">
                    Digite o CEP para preencher o endereço
                    automaticamente.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_160px]">
              <Input
                id="street"
                label="Rua / Logradouro"
                placeholder="Nome da rua"
                value={form.street}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField("street", event.target.value)
                }
                required
              />

              <Input
                id="number"
                label="Número"
                placeholder="123"
                value={form.number}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField("number", event.target.value)
                }
                required
              />
            </div>

            <Input
              id="complement"
              label="Complemento"
              placeholder="Apartamento, bloco, casa dos fundos..."
              value={form.complement}
              onChange={(event) =>
                updateField("complement", event.target.value)
              }
            />

            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Input
                id="neighborhood"
                label="Bairro"
                placeholder="Bairro"
                value={form.neighborhood}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField(
                    "neighborhood",
                    event.target.value,
                  )
                }
                required
              />

              <Input
                id="city"
                label="Cidade"
                placeholder="Cidade"
                value={form.city}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField("city", event.target.value)
                }
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Input
                id="state"
                label="Estado"
                placeholder="SP"
                value={form.state}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateField(
                    "state",
                    event.target.value.toUpperCase(),
                  )
                }
                maxLength={2}
                required
              />

              <div className="flex items-end">
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-zinc-500">
                  País:{" "}
                  <span className="text-zinc-300">
                    Brasil
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() =>
                  router.push("/cliente/enderecos")
                }
                disabled={saving}
              >
                Cancelar
              </Button>

              <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>
                {saving
                  ? "Salvando..."
                  : isEditing
                    ? "Salvar alterações"
                    : "Cadastrar endereço"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function NovoEnderecoFallback() {
  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
      <Spinner
        size="lg"
        variant="client"
        label="Carregando endereço..."
      />
    </main>
  );
}

export default function NovoEnderecoPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<NovoEnderecoFallback />}>
      <NovoEnderecoPageContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\enderecos\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
import Spinner from "@/components/ui/Spinner";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Address = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  recipient_name: string | null;
  postal_code: string | null;
  street: string;
  number: string;
  complement: string | null;
  neighborhood: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  is_default: boolean;
};

export default function ClienteEnderecosPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState<Address[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadAddresses = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login?redirect=/cliente/enderecos");
      return;
    }

    const { data, error: addressesError } = await supabase
      .from("addresses")
      .select(
        "id, label, recipient_name, postal_code, street, number, complement, neighborhood, city, state, is_default",
      )
      .eq("profile_id", user.id)
      .order("is_default", { ascending: false })
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (addressesError) {
      setError("Não foi possível carregar seus endereços.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setAddresses(data ?? []);
    setLoading(false);
  }, [router, supabase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void Promise.resolve().then(loadAddresses);
  }, [loadAddresses]);

  async function handleSetDefault(addressId: string) {
    setError("");
    setUpdating(addressId);

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login?redirect=/cliente/enderecos");
      return;
    }

    const { error: clearError } = await supabase
      .from("addresses")
      .update({ is_default: false })
      .eq("profile_id", user.id)
      .neq("id", addressId);

    if (clearError) {
      setError("Não foi possível atualizar o endereço principal.");
      setUpdating(null);
      return;
    }

    const { error: updateError } = await supabase
      .from("addresses")
      .update({ is_default: true })
      .eq("id", addressId)
      .eq("profile_id", user.id);

    if (updateError) {
      setError("Não foi possível definir o endereço principal.");
      setUpdating(null);
      return;
    }

    await loadAddresses();
    setUpdating(null);
  }

  async function handleDelete(address: Address) {
    if (address.is_default && addresses.length > 1) {
      setError(
        "Defina outro endereço como principal antes de excluir este.",
      );
      return;
    }

    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Deseja excluir o endereço "${address.label}"?`,
    );

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setDeleting(address.id);

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login?redirect=/cliente/enderecos");
      return;
    }

    const { error: deleteError } = await supabase
      .from("addresses")
      .delete()
      .eq("id", address.id)
      .eq("profile_id", user.id);

    if (deleteError) {
      setError("Não foi possível excluir este endereço.");
      setDeleting(null);
      return;
    }

    await loadAddresses();
    setDeleting(null);
  }

  function formatAddress(address: Address) {
    const firstLine = `${address.street}, ${address.number}`;

    const secondLine = [
      address.complement,
      address.neighborhood,
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" · ");

    const thirdLine = `${address.city} - ${address.state}`;

    return {
      firstLine,
      secondLine,
      thirdLine,
    };
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            aria-label="Ir para minha área"
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              priority
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:text-green-300"
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">←</span>
            <span>Voltar para minha área</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-6 py-8 sm:py-10">
        <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-green-400">
  Cliente
</span>

            <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
              Meus endereços
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-500">
              Gerencie os locais onde você costuma solicitar
              atendimentos.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/enderecos/novo")}
            className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400"
          >
            + Novo endereço
          </button>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-400"
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex min-h-64 items-center justify-center">
            <Spinner
              size="lg"
              variant="client"
              label="Carregando endereços..."
            />
          </div>
        ) : addresses.length === 0 ? (
          <Card padding="lg">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-10 text-center">
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-3xl text-green-400">
                📍
              </div>

              <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-bold text-white">
                Nenhum endereço cadastrado
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 max-w-md text-sm leading-relaxed text-zinc-600">
                Cadastre seu primeiro endereço para poder solicitar
                serviços na VemGO.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    router.push("/cliente/enderecos/novo")
                  }
                  className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400"
                >
                  Cadastrar endereço
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {addresses.map((address) => {
              const formatted = formatAddress(address);

              return (
                <Card
                  key={address.id}
                  padding="lg"
                  className={
                    address.is_default
                      ? "border-green-500/30"
                      : ""
                  }
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 gap-4">
                      <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-xl text-green-400">
                        📍
                      </div>

                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                          <h2 className="font-bold text-white">
                            {address.label}
                          </h2>

                          {address.is_default && (
                            <span className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-green-500/40 bg-green-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green-400">
                              Principal
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        {address.recipient_name && (
                          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">
                            {address.recipient_name}
                          </p>
                        )}

                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
                          {formatted.firstLine}
                        </p>

                        {formatted.secondLine && (
                          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
                            {formatted.secondLine}
                          </p>
                        )}

                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
                          {formatted.thirdLine}
                        </p>

                        {address.postal_code && (
                          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-700">
                            CEP {address.postal_code}
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap gap-2 sm:justify-end">
                      {!address.is_default && (
                        <Button
                          type="button"
                          variant="outline"
                          disabled={updating === address.id}
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleSetDefault(address.id)
                          }
                        >
                          {updating === address.id
                            ? "Atualizando..."
                            : "Definir principal"}
                        </Button>
                      )}

                      <Button
                        type="button"
                        variant="outline"
                        onClick={() =>
                          router.push(
                            `/cliente/enderecos/novo?id=${address.id}`,
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        Editar
                      </Button>

                      <Button
                        type="button"
                        variant="danger"
                        disabled={deleting === address.id}
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(address)}
                      >
                        {deleting === address.id
                          ? "Excluindo..."
                          : "Excluir"}
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";



import Image from "next/image";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";



type Address = {

  id: string;

  label: string;

  recipient_name: string | null;

  street: string;

  number: string;

  complement: string | null;

  neighborhood: string;

  city: string;

  state: string;

  is_default: boolean;

};



type ActiveRequest = {
  id: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
  service: { name: string; code: string } | null;
};

type VerificationStatus =

  | "unverified"

  | "pending"

  | "verified"

  | "rejected"

  | null;



export default function ClientePage() {

  const router = useRouter();



  const [name, setName] = useState("Cliente");
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");

  const [urgency, setUrgency] = useState<"normal" | "urgent">("normal");



  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState<Address[]>([]);

  const [selectedAddressId, setSelectedAddressId] = useState("");
  const [activeRequests, setActiveRequests] = useState<ActiveRequest[]>([]);



  const [verificationStatus, setVerificationStatus] =

    useState<VerificationStatus>(null);



  const [emailVerified, setEmailVerified] = useState(false);



  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [error, setError] = useState("");



  useEffect(() => {

    let cancelled = false;



    async function loadClient() {

      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



      const {

        data: { user },

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();



      if (!user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      if (cancelled) {

        return;

      }



      setEmailVerified(Boolean(user.email_confirmed_at));



      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase

        .from("profiles")

        .select(

          "full_name, avatar_url, user_type, status, client_verification_status",

        )

        .eq("id", user.id)

        .maybeSingle();



      if (cancelled) {

        return;

      }



      if (

        profileError ||

        !profile ||

        profile.user_type !== "client" ||

        profile.status === "blocked"

      ) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      setName(profile.full_name || "Cliente");

      // O avatar pode ser uma URL completa ou um caminho no bucket privado.
      setAvatarUrl(null);
      if (profile.avatar_url) {
        const storedAvatar = String(profile.avatar_url);
        if (storedAvatar.startsWith("https://") || storedAvatar.startsWith("http://")) {
          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);
        } else {
          const { data: signedImage, error: imageError } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(storedAvatar, 3600);
          if (!imageError && signedImage?.signedUrl) {
            setAvatarUrl(signedImage.signedUrl);
          }
        }
      }



      setVerificationStatus(

        (profile.client_verification_status as VerificationStatus) ||

          "unverified",

      );



      const { data: addressData, error: addressError } =

        await supabase

          .from("addresses")

          .select(

            "id, label, recipient_name, street, number, complement, neighborhood, city, state, is_default",

          )

          .eq("profile_id", user.id)

          .order("is_default", { ascending: false })

          .order("created_at", { ascending: false });



      if (cancelled) {

        return;

      }



      if (addressError) {

        setError("Não foi possível carregar seus endereços.");

      } else {

        const loadedAddresses = addressData || [];



        setAddresses(loadedAddresses);



        const defaultAddress =

          loadedAddresses.find((address) => address.is_default) ||

          loadedAddresses[0];



        if (defaultAddress) {

          setSelectedAddressId(defaultAddress.id);

        }

      }



      const { data: requestData, error: activeRequestsError } = await supabase
        .from("requests")
        .select("id, description, status, created_at, service:services(name, code)")
        .eq("client_id", user.id)
        .not("status", "in", '("completed","cancelled","expired")')
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
        .limit(5);

      if (!cancelled) {
        if (activeRequestsError) {
          console.warn("[VemGO] Não foi possível carregar solicitações em aberto.", activeRequestsError.message);
        } else {
          setActiveRequests((requestData ?? []) as unknown as ActiveRequest[]);
        }
        setLoading(false);
      }

    }



    void Promise.resolve().then(loadClient);



    return () => {

      cancelled = true;

    };

  }, [router]);



  async function handleLogout() {

    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



    await supabase.auth.signOut();



    router.replace("/login");

  }



  function classifyService(text: string): string | null {
    const normalized = text
      .toLowerCase()
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
      .replace(/[.,!?;:]/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    // Lâmpadas: diferenciar instalação, troca e diagnóstico.
    if (normalized.includes("lampada") || normalized.includes("lampadas")) {
      if (/\b(instalar|instalacao|colocar|montar)\b/.test(normalized)) {
        if (/\b(3|tres)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-026";
        if (/\b(2|duas)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-025";
        return "ELE-024";
      }

      if (/\b(queimou|queimada|queimadas|trocar|troca|substituir|substituicao)\b/.test(normalized)) {
        if (/\b(3|tres)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-029";
        if (/\b(2|duas)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-028";
        return "ELE-027";
      }

      return null;
    }

    // Tomadas: diagnosticar falta de energia antes de instalação/troca.
    if (normalized.includes("tomada") || normalized.includes("tomadas")) {
      if (
        normalized.includes("sem energia") ||
        normalized.includes("nao funciona") ||
        normalized.includes("nao tem energia")
      ) {
        return "ELE-004";
      }

      if (/\b(instalar|instalacao|colocar)\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return normalized.includes("20a") ? "ELE-011" : "ELE-009";
      }

      if (/\b(trocar|troca|substituir|substituicao)\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return normalized.includes("20a") ? "ELE-012" : "ELE-010";
      }

      return null;
    }

    // Chuveiro: instalação, troca, resistência ou diagnóstico.
    if (normalized.includes("chuveiro")) {
      if (normalized.includes("resistencia")) return "ELE-062";
      if (/\b(instalar|instalacao|colocar)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-060";
      if (/\b(trocar|troca|substituir|substituicao)\b/.test(normalized)) return "ELE-061";
      return "ELE-007";
    }

    if (
      normalized.includes("disjuntor") &&
      (normalized.includes("desarma") || normalized.includes("cai") || normalized.includes("desarmando"))
    ) {
      return "ELE-003";
    }

    if (
      normalized.includes("falta de energia") ||
      normalized.includes("sem energia no circuito")
    ) {
      return "ELE-002";
    }

    if (normalized.includes("curto circuito") || normalized.includes("curto-circuito")) {
      return "ELE-006";
    }

    // Iluminação genérica: diagnóstico, não visita técnica automática.
    if (
      normalized.includes("iluminacao") ||
      normalized.includes("luz") ||
      normalized.includes("luminaria")
    ) {
      return "ELE-005";
    }

    return null;
  }

  async function handleRequest() {

    setMessage("");

    setError("");



    if (!emailVerified) {

      setError(

        "Confirme seu e-mail antes de solicitar um serviço.",

      );

      router.push("/cliente/verificacao");

      return;

    }



    const trimmedDescription = description.trim();



    if (!trimmedDescription) {

      setError("Descreva o que você precisa.");

      return;

    }



    if (!selectedAddressId) {

      setError(

        "Selecione um endereço ou cadastre um novo endereço.",

      );

      return;

    }



    setSending(true);



    try {

      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



      const {

        data: { user },

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();



      if (!user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      if (!user.email_confirmed_at) {

        setEmailVerified(false);

        setError(

          "Confirme seu e-mail antes de solicitar um serviço.",

        );

        router.push("/cliente/verificacao");

        return;

      }



      const serviceCode = classifyService(trimmedDescription);



      if (!serviceCode) {

        setError(

          "Ainda não conseguimos identificar automaticamente este serviço. No MVP atual, experimente descrever uma necessidade de forma mais específica.",

        );

        return;

      }



      const { data: service, error: serviceError } = await supabase

        .from("services")

        .select("id, code, name")

        .eq("code", serviceCode)

        .eq("is_active", true)

        .maybeSingle();



      if (serviceError || !service) {

        setError(

          "O serviço identificado ainda não está disponível no catálogo.",

        );

        return;

      }



      const { data: request, error: requestError } = await supabase

        .from("requests")

        .insert({

          client_id: user.id,

          service_id: service.id,

          address_id: selectedAddressId,

          description: trimmedDescription,

          urgency,

          status: "pending",

        })

        .select("id")

        .single();



      if (requestError || !request) {

        setError(

          requestError?.message ||

            "Não foi possível criar sua solicitação.",

        );

        return;

      }



      const { error: classifyError } = await supabase.rpc(

        "classify_request",

        {

          p_request_id: request.id,

        },

      );



      if (classifyError) {

        setError(

          classifyError.message ||

            "Não foi possível classificar sua solicitação.",

        );

        return;

      }



      const { error: matchError } = await supabase.rpc(

        "match_request",

        {

          p_request_id: request.id,

        },

      );



      if (matchError) {

        setError(

          matchError.message ||

            "Não foi possível encontrar profissionais para sua solicitação.",

        );

        return;

      }



      setDescription("");



      setMessage(

        `Solicitação criada para o serviço "${service.name}".`,

      );



      setTimeout(() => {

        router.push(`/cliente/agendamento/${request.id}`);

      }, 500);

    } catch {

      setError("Ocorreu um erro ao criar sua solicitação.");

    } finally {

      setSending(false);

    }

  }



  const selectedAddress = addresses.find(

    (address) => address.id === selectedAddressId,

  );



  function getVerificationLabel() {

    switch (verificationStatus) {

      case "verified":

        return "Cliente verificado";



      case "pending":

        return "Verificação em análise";



      case "rejected":

        return "Verificação precisa de atenção";



      default:

        return "Verifique sua conta";

    }

  }



  function getVerificationDescription() {

    switch (verificationStatus) {

      case "verified":

        return "Sua identidade foi verificada. Sua conta está protegida.";



      case "pending":

        return "Sua documentação está em análise. Avisaremos quando houver uma atualização.";



      case "rejected":

        return "Existe uma pendência na sua verificação. Acesse para consultar e corrigir.";



      default:

        return "Confirme seu e-mail, celular e identidade para aumentar a segurança da sua conta.";

    }

  }



  if (loading) {

    return (

      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">

        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">

          Carregando sua área...

        </p>

      </main>

    );

  }



  return (

    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">

      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[112px] max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-label="Ir para o painel do cliente"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              priority
              unoptimized
            />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-sm text-green-400">Área do cliente</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white sm:text-2xl">Olá, {name}!</p>
              <div className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-end gap-2 text-sm">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/perfil")}
                  className="font-medium text-green-400 transition hover:text-green-300"
                >
                  Meu perfil
                </button>
                <span className="text-zinc-700">·</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-green-400"
                >
                  Sair
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/perfil")}
              aria-label="Abrir meu perfil"
              className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 transition hover:border-green-500"
            >
              {avatarUrl ? (
                <Image
                  src={avatarUrl}
                  alt={`Foto de ${name}`}
                  width={56}
                  height={56}
                  unoptimized
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-green-400">
                  {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "C"}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>



      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-10">

        <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-green-400">

          SERVIÇO RÁPIDO. PREÇO CERTO. CHAMA QUE VEM!

        </div>



        <div className="mt-5">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">

            O que você precisa resolver hoje?

          </h1>



          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">

            Conte sua necessidade e a VemGO encontrará o serviço

            adequado.

          </p>

        </div>



        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">

          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/servicos")}

            className="group rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/5 p-6 text-left transition hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-500/10"

          >

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-xl text-green-400">

                ✓

              </div>



              <span className="text-green-400 transition group-hover:translate-x-1">

                →

              </span>

            </div>



            <h2 className="mt-5 text-lg font-semibold">

              Meus serviços

            </h2>



            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">

              Acompanhe pedidos, agendamentos e histórico.

            </p>

          </button>



          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/enderecos")}

            className="group rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6 text-left transition hover:border-green-500/60"

          >

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-xl text-green-400">

                ⌂

              </div>



              <span className="text-green-400 transition group-hover:translate-x-1">

                →

              </span>

            </div>



            <h2 className="mt-5 text-lg font-semibold">

              Meus endereços

            </h2>



            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">

              Gerencie os locais onde você solicita serviços.

            </p>

          </button>



          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/verificacao")}

            className={`group rounded-2xl p-6 text-left transition ${

              verificationStatus === "verified"

                ? "border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/5 hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-500/10"

                : verificationStatus === "pending"

                  ? "border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/5 hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-500/10"

                  : "border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 hover:border-green-500/60"

            }`}

          >

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

              <div

                className={`flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border text-xl ${

                  verificationStatus === "verified"

                    ? "border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-green-400"

                    : verificationStatus === "pending"

                      ? "border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-green-400"

                      : "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-green-400"

                }`}

              >

                {verificationStatus === "verified" ? "✓" : "●"}

              </div>



              <span

                className={

                  verificationStatus === "pending"

                    ? "text-orange-400 transition group-hover:translate-x-1"

                    : "text-green-400 transition group-hover:translate-x-1"

                }

              >

                →

              </span>

            </div>



            <h2 className="mt-5 text-lg font-semibold">

              {getVerificationLabel()}

            </h2>



            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">

              {getVerificationDescription()}

            </p>

          </button>



          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">

            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-xl text-green-400">

              ?

            </div>



            <h2 className="mt-5 text-lg font-semibold">

              Precisa de ajuda?

            </h2>



            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">

              Em breve você poderá acessar o suporte diretamente

              pela plataforma.

            </p>

          </div>

        </div>



        {activeRequests.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-green-500/40 bg-green-500/5 p-6 md:p-8" aria-labelledby="solicitacoes-abertas">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-green-400">
                  Acompanhamento prioritário
                </p>
                <h2 id="solicitacoes-abertas" className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">
                  Você tem {activeRequests.length === 1 ? "uma solicitação em aberto" : `${activeRequests.length} solicitações em aberto`}
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">
                  Acompanhe o andamento sem precisar procurar em “Meus serviços”.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/servicos")}
                className="rounded-xl border border-green-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:bg-green-500 hover:text-black"
              >
                Ver todos os serviços
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
              {activeRequests.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black/70 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">
                      {item.service?.name || "Solicitação de serviço"}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">
                      {item.service?.code || "VemGO"} · {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" }).format(new Date(item.created_at))}
                    </p>
                    {item.description && (
                      <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">{item.description}</p>
                    )}
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-400">
                      {item.status === "matched" ? "Profissional selecionado" :
                       item.status === "scheduled" ? "Agendamento confirmado" :
                       item.status === "accepted" ? "Atendimento confirmado" :
                       item.status === "in_progress" ? "Atendimento em andamento" :
                       item.status === "classified" || item.status === "matching" ? "Buscando profissional" :
                       "Solicitação em andamento"}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => router.push(`/cliente/agendamento/${item.id}`)}
                    className="shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400"
                  >
                    Acompanhar →
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!emailVerified && (

          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-500/5 p-5">

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">

              <div>

                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400">

                  E-mail ainda não confirmado

                </p>



                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">

                  Confirme seu e-mail para liberar as solicitações de

                  serviços.

                </p>

              </div>



              <button

                type="button"

                onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/verificacao")}

                className="rounded-xl border border-green-500/40 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:border-green-400 hover:bg-green-500/10"

              >

                Verificar agora

              </button>

            </div>

          </div>

        )}



        <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6 md:p-8">

          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center">

            <div>

              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-green-400">

                Nova solicitação

              </p>



              <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">

                Conte o que está acontecendo

              </h2>



              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                Descreva sua necessidade com suas próprias palavras.

              </p>

            </div>



            <div className="rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-green-400">

              VemGO

            </div>

          </div>



          <div className="mt-7">

            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300">

              Descreva o que você precisa

            </label>



            <textarea

              value={description}

              onChange={(event) =>

                setDescription(event.target.value)

              }

              placeholder="Ex.: minha tomada está sem energia no quarto"

              rows={5}

              className="w-full resize-none rounded-2xl border border-zinc-700 bg-black px-4 py-4 text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-green-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500/20"

            />

          </div>



          <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-5">

            <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">

              <div>

                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-green-400">

                  Local do serviço

                </p>



                {selectedAddress ? (

                  <>

                    <h3 className="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold">

                      {selectedAddress.label}

                    </h3>



                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">

                      {selectedAddress.street},{" "}

                      {selectedAddress.number}

                      {selectedAddress.complement

                        ? ` — ${selectedAddress.complement}`

                        : ""}

                    </p>



                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                      {selectedAddress.neighborhood} —{" "}

                      {selectedAddress.city}/

                      {selectedAddress.state}

                    </p>

                  </>

                ) : (

                  <>

                    <h3 className="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold">

                      Nenhum endereço cadastrado

                    </h3>



                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                      Cadastre um endereço para solicitar serviços.

                    </p>

                  </>

                )}

              </div>



              <button

                type="button"

                onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/enderecos")}

                className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:border-green-500"

              >

                Gerenciar

              </button>

            </div>



            {addresses.length > 1 && (

              <div className="mt-5 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-5">

                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm text-zinc-400">

                  Usar outro endereço

                </label>



                <select

                  value={selectedAddressId}

                  onChange={(event) =>

                    setSelectedAddressId(event.target.value)

                  }

                  className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-green-500"

                >

                  {addresses.map((address) => (

                    <option key={address.id} value={address.id}>

                      {address.label} — {address.street},{" "}

                      {address.number}

                    </option>

                  ))}

                </select>

              </div>

            )}

          </div>



          <div className="mt-6">

            <p className="mb-3 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300">

              Prioridade

            </p>



            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">

              <button

                type="button"

                onClick={() => setUrgency("normal")}

                className={`rounded-xl border p-4 text-left transition ${

                  urgency === "normal"

                    ? "border-green-500 bg-green-500/10"

                    : "border-zinc-700 bg-black hover:border-zinc-600"

                }`}

              >

                <p

                  className={`font-semibold ${

                    urgency === "normal"

                      ? "text-green-400"

                      : "text-white"

                  }`}

                >

                  Normal

                </p>



                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">

                  Quero resolver normalmente.

                </p>

              </button>



              <button

                type="button"

                onClick={() => setUrgency("urgent")}

                className={`rounded-xl border p-4 text-left transition ${

                  urgency === "urgent"

                    ? "border-green-500 bg-green-500/10"

                    : "border-zinc-700 bg-black hover:border-zinc-600"

                }`}

              >

                <p

                  className={`font-semibold ${

                    urgency === "urgent"

                      ? "text-green-400"

                      : "text-white"

                  }`}

                >

                  Urgente

                </p>



                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">

                  Preciso resolver o quanto antes.

                </p>

              </button>

            </div>

          </div>



          {error && (

            <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">

              {error}

            </div>

          )}



          {message && (

            <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-300">

              {message}

            </div>

          )}



          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={handleRequest}

            disabled={sending || !selectedAddressId || !emailVerified}

            className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-6 py-4 text-base font-bold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"

          >

            {!emailVerified

              ? "Confirme seu e-mail para continuar"

              : sending

                ? "Criando solicitação..."

                : "Solicitar serviço →"}

          </button>

        </div>

      </section>

    </main>

  );

}
// TODO: adicionar link Meu perfil ao cabeçalho; marcador não encontrado automaticamente.


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\perfil\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type AccountProfile = {
  full_name: string | null;
  phone: string | null;
  avatar_url: string | null;
  user_type: "client" | "professional";
  status: string;
};

export default function MeuPerfilPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<AccountProfile | null>(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [newEmail, setNewEmail] = useState("");
  const [newPhone, setNewPhone] = useState("");
  const [phoneCode, setPhoneCode] = useState("");
  const [pendingPhone, setPendingPhone] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    async function load() {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
      const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (authError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }
      const { data, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("full_name, phone, avatar_url, user_type, status")
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (!active) return;
      if (profileError || !data || !["client", "professional"].includes(data.user_type)) {
        setError("Não foi possível carregar os dados da conta.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (data.status === "blocked") {
        await supabase.auth.signOut();
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }
      const current = data as AccountProfile;
      setProfile(current);
      setName(current.full_name ?? "");
      setPhone(current.phone ?? "");
      setEmail(user.email ?? "");
      setNewEmail(user.email ?? "");
      setNewPhone(current.phone ?? "");
      if (current.avatar_url) {
        if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(current.avatar_url)) {
          setAvatarPreview(current.avatar_url);
        } else {
          const { data: signedAvatar } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(current.avatar_url, 60 * 60);
          if (signedAvatar?.signedUrl && active) setAvatarPreview(signedAvatar.signedUrl);
        }
      }
      setLoading(false);
    }
    void load();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [router]);

  async function savePersonalData(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
