    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setError("Informe seu nome completo.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login");
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    const { error: updateError } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .update({ full_name: name.trim() })
      .eq("id", user.id);
    if (updateError) {
      setError("Não foi possível salvar o nome. " + updateError.message);
    } else {
      setProfile((old) => old ? { ...old, full_name: name.trim() } : old);
      setMessage("Nome atualizado.");
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function requestEmailChange(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    const candidate = newEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!candidate || !candidate.includes("@")) {
      setError("Informe um e-mail válido.");
      return;
    }
    if (candidate === email.toLowerCase()) {
      setError("O novo e-mail é igual ao atual.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { error: updateError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser(
      { email: candidate },
      { emailRedirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback?next=/cliente/perfil` }
    );
    if (updateError) setError("Não foi possível solicitar a troca de e-mail. " + updateError.message);
    else setMessage("Solicitação enviada. Confirme o novo e-mail pelo link enviado pela VemGO. O endereço só será atualizado após a confirmação.");
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function requestPhoneChange(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    const normalized = newPhone.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");
    if (!/^\+?[1-9]\d{9,14}$/.test(normalized)) {
      setError("Informe o celular com DDD. Para SMS internacional, use o formato +55...");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { error: updateError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ phone: normalized });
    if (updateError) {
      setError("Não foi possível iniciar a confirmação por SMS. Verifique se a autenticação por telefone está habilitada no Supabase. " + updateError.message);
    } else {
      setPendingPhone(normalized);
      setPhoneCode("");
      setMessage("Enviamos um código de confirmação para o novo celular. Digite-o abaixo para concluir a alteração.");
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function confirmPhoneChange(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (!pendingPhone || !/^\d{6}$/.test(phoneCode.trim())) {
      setError("Informe o código SMS de 6 dígitos.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    const { error: verifyError } = await supabase.auth.verifyOtp({
      phone: pendingPhone,
      token: phoneCode.trim(),
      type: "phone_change",
    });
    if (verifyError) {
      setError("Código inválido ou expirado. " + verifyError.message);
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    if (!user) {
      setError("Sua sessão expirou. Entre novamente.");
      router.replace("/login");
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    const { error: profileError } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .update({ phone: pendingPhone, phone_verified_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq("id", user.id);
    if (profileError) {
      setError("O celular foi confirmado na autenticação, mas não foi possível atualizar o perfil. Entre em contato com o suporte. " + profileError.message);
    } else {
      setPhone(pendingPhone);
      setNewPhone(pendingPhone);
      setPendingPhone("");
      setPhoneCode("");
      setMessage("Celular alterado e confirmado com sucesso.");
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function changePassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (newPassword.length < 8) {
      setError("A senha deve ter pelo menos 8 caracteres.");
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("As senhas não coincidem.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { error: passwordError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: newPassword });
    if (passwordError) setError("Não foi possível alterar a senha. " + passwordError.message);
    else {
      setNewPassword("");
      setConfirmPassword("");
      setMessage("Senha alterada com sucesso.");
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function uploadPhoto(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (!photo) {
      setError("Escolha uma imagem primeiro.");
      return;
    }
    if (!photo.type.startsWith("image/") || photo.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError("Escolha uma imagem de até 5 MB.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) {
      router.replace("/login");
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    const ext = photo.name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase() || "jpg";
    const path = `${user.id}/avatar-${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
    const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from("profile-photos").upload(path, photo, { upsert: false, contentType: photo.type });
    if (uploadError) {
      setError("Não foi possível enviar a foto. " + uploadError.message);
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    const { error: updateError } = await supabase.from("profiles").update({ avatar_url: path }).eq("id", user.id);
    if (updateError) {
      setError("A foto foi enviada, mas não foi possível salvar no perfil. " + updateError.message);
      setSaving(false);
      return;
    }
    const { data: signedAvatar } = await supabase.storage
      .from("profile-photos")
      .createSignedUrl(path, 60 * 60);
    if (signedAvatar?.signedUrl) setAvatarPreview(signedAvatar.signedUrl);
    setPhoto(null);
    setMessage("Foto atualizada com sucesso.");
    setSaving(false);
  }

  if (loading) return <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-zinc-950 text-white">Carregando perfil...</main>;

  const isProfessional = profile?.user_type === "professional";

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-8 text-zinc-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
        <header className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <Image src="/logo-vemgo.png" alt="VemGO" width={180} height={100} className="h-auto w-36" priority />
          <button type="button" onClick={() => router.push(isProfessional ? "/profissional" : "/cliente")} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:border-green-500">Voltar ao painel</button>
        </header>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Meu perfil</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">Atualize seus dados de acesso e contato. CPF e documentos de identidade não podem ser alterados por esta tela.</p>
        {message && <div role="status" className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-green-300">{message}</div>}
        {error && <div role="alert" className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}

        <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Dados pessoais</h2>
          <form onSubmit={savePersonalData} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
            <label className="block text-sm">Nome completo<input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} autoComplete="name" required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <label className="block text-sm">Celular atual<input value={phone || "Não informado"} readOnly className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-zinc-500" /></label>
            <button disabled={saving} className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar nome"}</button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">E-mail de acesso</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">Atual: {email || "Não informado"}</p>
          <form onSubmit={requestEmailChange} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
            <label className="block text-sm">Novo e-mail<input type="email" value={newEmail} onChange={(e) => setNewEmail(e.target.value)} autoComplete="email" required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <button disabled={saving} className="rounded-xl border border-green-500/40 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-green-300 disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Processando..." : "Solicitar alteração e confirmar e-mail"}</button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Celular</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">A alteração só será concluída após a confirmação do código enviado por SMS.</p>
          <form onSubmit={requestPhoneChange} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
            <label className="block text-sm">Novo celular com DDD<input type="tel" value={newPhone} onChange={(e) => setNewPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="+5511999999999" autoComplete="tel" required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <button disabled={saving} className="rounded-xl border border-green-500/40 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-green-300 disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Enviando..." : "Enviar código SMS"}</button>
          </form>
          {pendingPhone && <form onSubmit={confirmPhoneChange} className="mt-5 space-y-4 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-5">
            <label className="block text-sm">Código SMS de 6 dígitos<input inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="one-time-code" maxLength={6} value={phoneCode} onChange={(e) => setPhoneCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6))} required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <button disabled={saving} className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Confirmando..." : "Confirmar celular"}</button>
          </form>}
        </section>

        <section className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Senha</h2>
          <form onSubmit={changePassword} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
            <label className="block text-sm">Nova senha<input type="password" value={newPassword} onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)} minLength={8} autoComplete="new-password" required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <label className="block text-sm">Confirmar nova senha<input type="password" value={confirmPassword} onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} minLength={8} autoComplete="new-password" required className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3" /></label>
            <button disabled={saving} className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Salvando..." : "Alterar senha"}</button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Foto de perfil</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">Selecione uma imagem para habilitar o botão de atualização. Formatos de imagem, até 5 MB.</p>
          {avatarPreview && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-4">
              <Image src={avatarPreview} alt="Foto de perfil atual" className="h-16 w-16 rounded-full border border-green-500/40 object-cover" />
              <span className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Foto atual</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {photo && <p className="mt-3 break-all text-sm text-green-300">Selecionada: {photo.name}</p>}
          <form onSubmit={uploadPhoto} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
            <label className="block text-sm">Escolher imagem (máximo 5 MB)
              <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" onChange={(e) => setPhoto(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} className="mt-2 block w-full text-sm text-zinc-300 file:mr-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-green-500 file:px-4 file:py-2 file:font-semibold file:text-black hover:file:bg-green-400" />
            </label>
            <button type="submit" disabled={saving || !photo} className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50">{saving ? "Enviando..." : photo ? "Atualizar foto" : "Escolha uma imagem primeiro"}</button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Documento de identidade</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">CPF e documentos usados na verificação não podem ser alterados pelo perfil. Se houver erro, solicite correção ao suporte da VemGO para manter o histórico de verificação.</p>
          {!isProfessional && <button type="button" onClick={() => router.push("/cliente/verificacao/identidade")} className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-green-500/40 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-green-300 transition hover:bg-green-500/10">Consultar verificação de identidade</button>}
          {isProfessional && <button type="button" onClick={() => router.push("/cadastro-profissional/identidade")} className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-green-500/40 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-green-300 transition hover:bg-green-500/10">Consultar verificação de identidade</button>}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\servicos\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Order = {
  id: string;
  request_id: string | null;
  service_id: string;
  address_id: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  status: string;
  scheduled_start: string | null;
  scheduled_end: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type Service = {
  id: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
};

type Address = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  street: string;
  number: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
};

type Appointment = {
  id: string;
  order_id: string;
  starts_at: string;
  ends_at: string;
  status: string;
};

type DisplayOrder = {
  order: Order;
  service?: Service;
  address?: Address;
  appointment?: Appointment;
};

type Filter =
  | "all"
  | "pending"
  | "waiting_schedule"
  | "scheduled"
  | "in_progress"
  | "completed"
  | "cancelled";

function formatCurrency(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "BRL",
  }).format(value);
}

function formatDate(value: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(new Date(value));
}

function formatDateTime(value: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(new Date(value));
}

function formatTime(value: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(new Date(value));
}

function getDisplayStatus(item: DisplayOrder) {
  const { order, appointment } = item;

  if (
    order.status === "cancelled" ||
    appointment?.status === "cancelled"
  ) {
    return {
      key: "cancelled" as Filter,
      label: "Cancelado",
      description: "Este serviço foi cancelado.",
      className:
        "border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 text-red-400",
    };
  }

  if (
    order.status === "completed" ||
    appointment?.status === "completed"
  ) {
    return {
      key: "completed" as Filter,
      label: "Concluído",
      description: order.scheduled_end
        ? `Serviço realizado em ${formatDate(order.scheduled_end)}`
        : "Serviço realizado.",
      className:
        "border-green-500/40 bg-green-500/10 text-green-400",
    };
  }

  if (
    order.status === "in_progress" ||
    appointment?.status === "in_progress"
  ) {
    return {
      key: "in_progress" as Filter,
      label: "Em andamento",
      description: "O serviço está em execução.",
      className:
        "border-blue-500/40 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400",
    };
  }

  if (appointment?.status === "confirmed") {
    return {
      key: "scheduled" as Filter,
      label: "Agendado",
      description: `${formatDate(appointment.starts_at)} às ${formatTime(appointment.starts_at)}`,
      className:
        "border-blue-500/40 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400",
    };
  }

  if (appointment?.status === "scheduled") {
    return {
      key: "waiting_schedule" as Filter,
      label: "Aguardando confirmação",
      description: `Horário solicitado em ${formatDateTime(appointment.starts_at)}`,
      className:
        "border-yellow-500/40 bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400",
    };
  }

  if (
    order.status === "confirmed" ||
    order.status === "matched"
  ) {
    return {
      key: "waiting_schedule" as Filter,
      label: "Aguardando agendamento",
      description:
        "O serviço está confirmado. Escolha um horário disponível.",
      className:
        "border-yellow-500/40 bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400",
    };
  }

  return {
    key: "pending" as Filter,
    label: "Aguardando profissional",
    description:
      "Estamos buscando o profissional adequado.",
    className:
      "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-300",
  };
}

function getFilterLabel(filter: Filter) {
  switch (filter) {
    case "all":
      return "Todos";
    case "pending":
      return "Aguardando profissional";
    case "waiting_schedule":
      return "Aguardando agendamento";
    case "scheduled":
      return "Agendado";
    case "in_progress":
      return "Em andamento";
    case "completed":
      return "Concluído";
    case "cancelled":
      return "Cancelado";
  }
}

function hasActiveAppointment(appointment?: Appointment) {
  return (
    !!appointment &&
    ["scheduled", "confirmed", "in_progress"].includes(
      appointment.status,
    )
  );
}

export default function ClienteServicosPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<DisplayOrder[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>("all");

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMessage("");

    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user) {
      router.replace("/login");
      return;
    }

    const { data: profile, error: profileError } =
      await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("user_type, status")
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

    if (
      profileError ||
      !profile ||
      profile.user_type !== "client" ||
      profile.status === "blocked"
    ) {
      router.replace("/login");
      return;
    }

    const { data: orderData, error: orderError } =
      await supabase
        .from("orders")
        .select(
          "id, request_id, service_id, address_id, price, currency, status, scheduled_start, scheduled_end, created_at",
        )
        .eq("client_id", user.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (orderError) {
      console.error(
        "[VemGO] Erro ao carregar pedidos:",
        orderError.message,
      );
      setErrorMessage("Não foi possível carregar seus serviços.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const orderList = orderData ?? [];

    if (orderList.length === 0) {
      setOrders([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const serviceIds = [
      ...new Set(orderList.map((order) => order.service_id)),
    ];

    const addressIds = [
      ...new Set(orderList.map((order) => order.address_id)),
    ];

    const orderIds = orderList.map((order) => order.id);

    const [
      { data: serviceData, error: serviceError },
      { data: addressData, error: addressError },
      { data: appointmentData, error: appointmentError },
    ] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from("services")
        .select("id, code, name")
        .in("id", serviceIds),

      supabase
        .from("addresses")
        .select(
          "id, label, street, number, neighborhood, city, state",
        )
        .in("id", addressIds),

      supabase
        .from("appointments")
        .select("id, order_id, starts_at, ends_at, status")
        .in("order_id", orderIds)
        .order("starts_at", { ascending: false }),
    ]);

    if (serviceError || addressError || appointmentError) {
      console.error("[VemGO] Erro ao carregar detalhes:", {
        services: serviceError?.message,
        addresses: addressError?.message,
        appointments: appointmentError?.message,
      });

      setErrorMessage(
        "Alguns detalhes dos serviços não puderam ser carregados. Atualize a página.",
      );
    }

    const serviceMap: Record<string, Service> = {};
    const addressMap: Record<string, Address> = {};
    const appointmentMap: Record<string, Appointment> = {};

    for (const service of serviceData ?? []) {
      serviceMap[service.id] = service;
    }

    for (const address of addressData ?? []) {
      addressMap[address.id] = address;
    }

    // A consulta está ordenada do mais recente para o mais antigo.
    // Mantemos o agendamento mais recente de cada pedido.
    for (const appointment of appointmentData ?? []) {
      if (!appointmentMap[appointment.order_id]) {
        appointmentMap[appointment.order_id] = appointment;
      }
    }

    setOrders(
      orderList.map((order) => ({
        order,
        service: serviceMap[order.service_id],
        address: addressMap[order.address_id],
        appointment: appointmentMap[order.id],
      })),
    );

    setLoading(false);
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      void loadData();
    }, 0);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    if (filter === "all") {
      return orders;
    }

    return orders.filter(
      (item) => getDisplayStatus(item).key === filter,
    );
  }, [orders, filter]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          Carregando seus serviços...
        </p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            aria-label="Ir para minha área"
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              priority
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:text-green-300"
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">←</span>
            <span>Voltar para minha área</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-green-400">
            SERVIÇO RÁPIDO. PREÇO CERTO. CHAMA QUE VEM!
          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-5 text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Meus serviços
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">
            Acompanhe seus pedidos, agendamentos e histórico de
            serviços.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
            Consulte os serviços solicitados e seus respectivos
            status.
          </p>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400"
          >
            + Solicitar novo serviço
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 grid overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black md:grid-cols-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter("all")}
            className={`border-b border-zinc-800 p-5 text-left transition md:border-b-0 md:border-r ${
              filter === "all"
                ? "bg-green-500/10"
                : "hover:bg-zinc-900"
            }`}
          >
            <p
              className={`font-semibold ${
                filter === "all" ? "text-green-400" : "text-white"
              }`}
            >
              Meus serviços
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Acompanhe seus pedidos
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter("scheduled")}
            className={`border-b border-zinc-800 p-5 text-left transition md:border-b-0 md:border-r ${
              filter === "scheduled"
                ? "bg-green-500/10"
                : "hover:bg-zinc-900"
            }`}
          >
            <p
              className={`font-semibold ${
                filter === "scheduled"
                  ? "text-green-400"
                  : "text-white"
              }`}
            >
              Agendamentos
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Consulte seus horários
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter("completed")}
            className={`p-5 text-left transition ${
              filter === "completed"
                ? "bg-green-500/10"
                : "hover:bg-zinc-900"
            }`}
          >
            <p
              className={`font-semibold ${
                filter === "completed"
                  ? "text-green-400"
                  : "text-white"
              }`}
            >
              Histórico
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Serviços realizados
            </p>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          {(
            [
              "all",
              "pending",
              "waiting_schedule",
              "scheduled",
              "in_progress",
              "completed",
              "cancelled",
            ] as Filter[]
          ).map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter(item)}
              className={`rounded-full border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition ${
                filter === item
                  ? "border-green-500 bg-green-500 text-black"
                  : "border-zinc-700 bg-black text-zinc-400 hover:border-zinc-500 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {getFilterLabel(item)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {errorMessage && (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">
            {errorMessage}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void loadData()}
              className="ml-3 font-semibold underline"
            >
              Tentar novamente
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-10 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-2xl text-green-400">
              +
            </div>

            <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold">
              Nenhum serviço encontrado
            </h2>

            <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Seus pedidos, agendamentos e serviços realizados
              aparecerão aqui.
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
              className="mt-6 rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400"
            >
              Solicitar novo serviço
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 space-y-4">
            {filteredOrders.map((item) => {
              const status = getDisplayStatus(item);
              const { order, service, address, appointment } = item;

              const activeAppointment =
                hasActiveAppointment(appointment);

              const canSchedule =
                ["confirmed", "matched"].includes(order.status) &&
                !activeAppointment;

              const isCancelled =
                order.status === "cancelled" ||
                appointment?.status === "cancelled";

              return (
                <div
                  key={order.id}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-5 transition hover:border-zinc-700"
                >
                  <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.5fr_0.7fr_1fr_auto] lg:items-center">
                    <div className="flex gap-4">
                      <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-2xl text-green-400">
                        ⚡
                      </div>

                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                          {service?.name || "Serviço VemGO"}
                        </h2>

                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
                          {service?.code || "Serviço"}
                        </p>

                        {address && (
                          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-zinc-400">
                            {address.street}, {address.number} ·{" "}
                            {address.neighborhood} · {address.city}/
                            {address.state}
                          </p>
                        )}

                        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-600">
                          Solicitado em{" "}
                          {formatDateTime(order.created_at)}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="border-t border-zinc-800 pt-4 lg:border-l lg:border-t-0 lg:pl-6 lg:pt-0">
                      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                        Valor do serviço
                      </p>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
                        {formatCurrency(Number(order.price))}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="border-t border-zinc-800 pt-4 lg:border-l lg:border-t-0 lg:pl-6 lg:pt-0">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${status.className}`}
                      >
                        {status.label}
                      </span>

                      <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-zinc-400">
                        {status.description}
                      </p>

                      {appointment &&
                        ["scheduled", "confirmed"].includes(
                          appointment.status,
                        ) && (
                          <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400">
                            {formatDate(appointment.starts_at)} às{" "}
                            {formatTime(appointment.starts_at)}
                          </p>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="border-t border-zinc-800 pt-4 lg:border-t-0 lg:pt-0">
                      {canSchedule ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            router.push(
                              `/cliente/agendamento?orderId=${order.id}`,
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-green-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:bg-green-500 hover:text-black"
                        >
                          Agendar →
                        </button>
                      ) : appointment?.status === "scheduled" ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            router.push(
                              `/cliente/agendamento/${order.id}`,
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-green-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:bg-green-500 hover:text-black"
                        >
                          Ver agendamento →
                        </button>
                      ) : appointment?.status === "confirmed" ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            router.push(
                              `/cliente/agendamento/${order.id}`,
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300 transition hover:border-green-500 hover:text-green-400"
                        >
                          Ver agendamento →
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={isCancelled}
                          onClick={() =>
                            router.push(
                              `/cliente/agendamento/${order.id}`,
                            )
                          }
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300 transition hover:border-green-500 hover:text-green-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                        >
                          Ver detalhes →
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\verificacao\identidade\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import {
  ChangeEvent,
  FormEvent,
  useEffect,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type DocumentType = "rg" | "cnh";

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

const ALLOWED_TYPES = [
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
];

type IdentityStatus =
  | "unverified"
  | "pending"
  | "processing"
  | "manual_review"
  | "verified"
  | "approved"
  | "rejected";

export default function ClienteIdentidadePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState("");
  const [documentType, setDocumentType] =
    useState<DocumentType>("cnh");
  const [documentNumber, setDocumentNumber] = useState("");

  const [frontFile, setFrontFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [backFile, setBackFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [selfieFile, setSelfieFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  const [frontPreview, setFrontPreview] = useState("");
  const [backPreview, setBackPreview] = useState("");
  const [selfiePreview, setSelfiePreview] = useState("");

  const [identityStatus, setIdentityStatus] =
    useState<IdentityStatus>("unverified");

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadIdentity() {
      const {
        data: { user },
        error: userError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (cancelled) {
        return;
      }

      if (userError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } =
        await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .select(
            "user_type, status, identity_verified_at, client_verification_status",
          )
          .eq("id", user.id)
          .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) {
        return;
      }

      if (
        profileError ||
        !profile ||
        profile.user_type !== "client" ||
        profile.status === "blocked"
      ) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      setUserId(user.id);

      if (profile.identity_verified_at) {
        setIdentityStatus("verified");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data: verification } = await supabase
        .from("identity_verifications")
        .select("status")
        .eq("profile_id", user.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) {
        return;
      }

      if (verification?.status) {
        setIdentityStatus(
          verification.status as IdentityStatus,
        );
      } else if (profile.client_verification_status) {
        setIdentityStatus(
          profile.client_verification_status as IdentityStatus,
        );
      }

      setLoading(false);
    }

    void Promise.resolve().then(loadIdentity);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router, supabase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (frontPreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(frontPreview);
      }

      if (backPreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(backPreview);
      }

      if (selfiePreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(selfiePreview);
      }
    };
  }, [frontPreview, backPreview, selfiePreview]);

  function validateImage(file: File) {
    if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
      return "Escolha uma imagem JPG, PNG ou WEBP.";
    }

    if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
      return "A imagem precisa ter no máximo 8 MB.";
    }

    return "";
  }

  function handleImageChange(
    event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
    type: "front" | "back" | "selfie",
  ) {
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const file = event.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) {
      return;
    }

    const validationError = validateImage(file);

    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    const preview = URL.createObjectURL(file);

    if (type === "front") {
      if (frontPreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(frontPreview);
      }

      setFrontFile(file);
      setFrontPreview(preview);
    }

    if (type === "back") {
      if (backPreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(backPreview);
      }

      setBackFile(file);
      setBackPreview(preview);
    }

    if (type === "selfie") {
      if (selfiePreview) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(selfiePreview);
      }

      setSelfieFile(file);
      setSelfiePreview(preview);
    }
  }

  async function uploadFile(
    file: File,
    path: string,
  ) {
    const { error: uploadError } =
      await supabase.storage
        .from("identity-documents")
        .upload(path, file, {
          cacheControl: "3600",
          upsert: false,
          contentType: file.type,
        });

    if (uploadError) {
      throw new Error(uploadError.message);
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmit(
    event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    const normalizedNumber = documentNumber.trim();

    if (!userId) {
      setError("Usuário não identificado.");
      return;
    }

    if (!normalizedNumber) {
      setError("Informe o número do documento.");
      return;
    }

    if (!frontFile) {
      setError(
        "Envie a foto da frente do documento.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!backFile) {
      setError(
        "Envie também a foto do verso do documento.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!selfieFile) {
      setError("Envie uma selfie para continuar.");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    let frontPath = "";
    let backPath = "";
    let selfiePath = "";

    try {
      const timestamp = Date.now();

      const frontExtension =
        frontFile.name
          .split(".")
          .pop()
          ?.toLowerCase() || "jpg";

      const backExtension =
        backFile.name
          .split(".")
          .pop()
          ?.toLowerCase() || "jpg";

      const selfieExtension =
        selfieFile.name
          .split(".")
          .pop()
          ?.toLowerCase() || "jpg";

      frontPath =
        `${userId}/client-verification/front-${timestamp}.${frontExtension}`;

      backPath =
        `${userId}/client-verification/back-${timestamp}.${backExtension}`;

      selfiePath =
        `${userId}/client-verification/selfie-${timestamp}.${selfieExtension}`;

      await uploadFile(frontFile, frontPath);
      await uploadFile(backFile, backPath);
      await uploadFile(selfieFile, selfiePath);

      const {
        data: identityDocument,
        error: documentError,
      } = await supabase
        .from("identity_documents")
        .insert({
          profile_id: userId,
          document_type: documentType,
          document_number: normalizedNumber,
          document_front_path: frontPath,
          document_back_path: backPath,
          verification_status: "pending",
        })
        .select("id")
        .single();

      if (
        documentError ||
        !identityDocument
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          documentError?.message ||
            "Não foi possível registrar o documento.",
        );
      }

      const {
        error: verificationError,
      } = await supabase
        .from("identity_verifications")
        .insert({
          profile_id: userId,
          identity_document_id:
            identityDocument.id,
          verification_type:
            "full_identity",
          status: "pending",
          selfie_path: selfiePath,
        });

      if (verificationError) {
        throw new Error(
          verificationError.message,
        );
      }

      const { error: profileError } =
        await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .update({
            client_verification_status:
              "pending",
          })
          .eq("id", userId);

      if (profileError) {
        throw new Error(
          profileError.message,
        );
      }

      setIdentityStatus("pending");

      setMessage(
        "Documentos enviados com sucesso. Sua identidade está aguardando análise.",
      );

      setDocumentNumber("");
      setFrontFile(null);
      setBackFile(null);
      setSelfieFile(null);

      setFrontPreview("");
      setBackPreview("");
      setSelfiePreview("");
    } catch (submitError) {
      const pathsToRemove = [
        frontPath,
        backPath,
        selfiePath,
      ].filter(Boolean);

      if (pathsToRemove.length > 0) {
        await supabase.storage
          .from("identity-documents")
          .remove(pathsToRemove);
      }

      const errorMessage =
        submitError instanceof Error
          ? submitError.message
          : "Não foi possível enviar sua identidade.";

      setError(
        `Não foi possível concluir o envio: ${errorMessage}`,
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          Carregando sua validação...
        </p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (
    identityStatus === "pending" ||
    identityStatus === "processing" ||
    identityStatus === "manual_review"
  ) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
        <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between px-6 py-5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push("/cliente")
              }
              className="flex items-center"
              aria-label="Voltar para a área do cliente"
            >
              <Image
                src="/logo-vemgo.png"
                alt="VemGO"
                width={360}
                height={200}
                priority
                unoptimized
                className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push(
                  "/cliente/verificacao",
                )
              }
              className="text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-white"
            >
              Voltar
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[70vh] max-w-xl items-center px-6 py-10">
          <section className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-8 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 text-2xl">
              ⏳
            </div>

            <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold">
              Identidade em análise
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
              Recebemos seus documentos e selfie.
              Nossa equipe fará a análise e você
              poderá acompanhar o resultado pela
              área de verificação.
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push(
                  "/cliente/verificacao",
                )
              }
              className="mt-7 rounded-xl bg-white px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-zinc-200"
            >
              Voltar para verificação
            </button>
          </section>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (
    identityStatus === "verified" ||
    identityStatus === "approved"
  ) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
        <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between px-6 py-5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push("/cliente")
              }
              className="flex items-center"
              aria-label="Voltar para a área do cliente"
            >
              <Image
                src="/logo-vemgo.png"
                alt="VemGO"
                width={360}
                height={200}
                priority
                unoptimized
                className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push(
                  "/cliente/verificacao",
                )
              }
              className="text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-white"
            >
              Voltar
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[70vh] max-w-xl items-center px-6 py-10">
          <section className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-zinc-950 p-8 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 text-2xl">
              ✓
            </div>

            <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold">
              Identidade verificada
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
              Sua identidade já foi confirmada
              pela VemGO.
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push(
                  "/cliente/verificacao",
                )
              }
              className="mt-7 rounded-xl bg-white px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-zinc-200"
            >
              Voltar para verificação
            </button>
          </section>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              router.push("/cliente")
            }
            className="flex items-center"
            aria-label="Voltar para a área do cliente"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              priority
              unoptimized
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              router.push(
                "/cliente/verificacao",
              )
            }
            className="text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-white"
          >
            Voltar
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-400">
            ETAPA 3
          </p>

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Confirmar identidade
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
            Envie seu documento e uma selfie para
            confirmar que a conta pertence a você.
          </p>
        </div>

        {(message || error) && (
          <div
            className={[
              "mb-6 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm",
              error
                ? "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 text-red-300"
                : "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300",
            ].join(" ")}
          >
            {error || message}
          </div>
        )}

        <form
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
          className="space-y-6"
        >
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              1. Documento
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Use um documento oficial válido.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-5">
              <label
                htmlFor="documentType"
                className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300"
              >
                Tipo de documento
              </label>

              <select
                id="documentType"
                value={documentType}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setDocumentType(
                    event.target.value as DocumentType,
                  )
                }
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
              >
                <option value="cnh">CNH</option>
                <option value="rg">RG</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5">
              <label
                htmlFor="documentNumber"
                className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300"
              >
                Número do documento
              </label>

              <input
                id="documentNumber"
                type="text"
                value={documentNumber}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setDocumentNumber(
                    event.target.value,
                  )
                }
                placeholder="Digite o número do documento"
                autoComplete="off"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-emerald-500"
              />
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              2. Frente do documento
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Tire uma foto nítida, sem cortes e sem
              reflexos.
            </p>

            <label
              htmlFor="frontFile"
              className="mt-5 block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 p-6 text-center transition hover:border-zinc-500"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white">
                {frontFile
                  ? frontFile.name
                  : "Selecionar frente do documento"}
              </span>

              <span className="mt-2 block text-xs text-zinc-500">
                JPG, PNG ou WEBP — máximo 8 MB
              </span>

              <input
                id="frontFile"
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                onChange={(event) =>
                  handleImageChange(
                    event,
                    "front",
                  )
                }
                className="sr-only"
              />
            </label>

            {frontPreview && (
              <div className="relative mt-5 h-80 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black">
                <Image
                  src={frontPreview}
                  alt="Prévia da frente do documento"
                  fill
                  unoptimized
                  sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px"
                  className="object-contain"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              3. Verso do documento
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Envie o verso completo e legível.
            </p>

            <label
              htmlFor="backFile"
              className="mt-5 block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 p-6 text-center transition hover:border-zinc-500"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white">
                {backFile
                  ? backFile.name
                  : "Selecionar verso do documento"}
              </span>

              <span className="mt-2 block text-xs text-zinc-500">
                JPG, PNG ou WEBP — máximo 8 MB
              </span>

              <input
                id="backFile"
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                onChange={(event) =>
                  handleImageChange(
                    event,
                    "back",
                  )
                }
                className="sr-only"
              />
            </label>

            {backPreview && (
              <div className="relative mt-5 h-80 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black">
                <Image
                  src={backPreview}
                  alt="Prévia do verso do documento"
                  fill
                  unoptimized
                  sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px"
                  className="object-contain"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              4. Selfie
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">
              Tire uma selfie com boa iluminação,
              mostrando claramente seu rosto.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/5 p-4">
              <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-orange-200/80">
                Esta etapa comprova a identidade da
                conta. A VemGO ainda não realiza
                reconhecimento facial automático nesta
                versão.
              </p>
            </div>

            <label
              htmlFor="selfieFile"
              className="mt-5 block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 p-6 text-center transition hover:border-zinc-500"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white">
                {selfieFile
                  ? selfieFile.name
                  : "Selecionar selfie"}
              </span>

              <span className="mt-2 block text-xs text-zinc-500">
                JPG, PNG ou WEBP — máximo 8 MB
              </span>

              <input
                id="selfieFile"
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                capture="user"
                onChange={(event) =>
                  handleImageChange(
                    event,
                    "selfie",
                  )
                }
                className="sr-only"
              />
            </label>

            {selfiePreview && (
              <div className="relative mx-auto mt-5 h-96 max-w-sm overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black">
                <Image
                  src={selfiePreview}
                  alt="Prévia da selfie"
                  fill
                  unoptimized
                  sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 384px"
                  className="object-contain"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">
              Segurança e privacidade
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500">
              Seus documentos e selfie serão armazenados
              em área privada e utilizados exclusivamente
              para a análise de identidade da sua conta.
            </p>
          </section>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-500 px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-black transition hover:bg-emerald-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {submitting
              ? "Enviando documentos..."
              : "Enviar para análise"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\cliente\verificacao\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { FormEvent, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type VerificationStatus =
  | "unverified"
  | "pending"
  | "processing"
  | "manual_review"
  | "verified"
  | "rejected";

export default function ClienteVerificacaoPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [sendingCode, setSendingCode] = useState(false);
  const [verifyingCode, setVerifyingCode] = useState(false);

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");

  const [emailVerified, setEmailVerified] = useState(false);
  const [phoneVerified, setPhoneVerified] = useState(false);
  const [identityStatus, setIdentityStatus] =
    useState<VerificationStatus>("unverified");

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadVerification() {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select(
          "full_name, user_type, status, phone, phone_verified_at, identity_status, identity_verified_at, client_verification_status",
        )
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) {
        return;
      }

      if (
        profileError ||
        !profile ||
        profile.user_type !== "client" ||
        profile.status === "blocked"
      ) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      setEmail(user.email || "");
      setPhone(profile.phone || "");
      setEmailVerified(Boolean(user.email_confirmed_at));
      setPhoneVerified(Boolean(profile.phone_verified_at));

      const profileIdentityStatus = String(
        profile.identity_status || profile.client_verification_status || "",
      ).toLowerCase();

      if (
        profile.identity_verified_at ||
        profileIdentityStatus === "verified" ||
        profileIdentityStatus === "approved"
      ) {
        setIdentityStatus("verified");
      } else {
        const { data: verification } = await supabase
          .from("identity_verifications")
          .select("status")
          .eq("profile_id", user.id)
          .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
          .limit(1)
          .maybeSingle();

        if (!cancelled) {
          const verificationStatus = String(verification?.status || "").toLowerCase();

          if (verificationStatus === "verified" || verificationStatus === "approved") {
            setIdentityStatus("verified");
          } else if (
            verificationStatus === "pending" ||
            verificationStatus === "processing" ||
            verificationStatus === "manual_review"
          ) {
            setIdentityStatus(verificationStatus as VerificationStatus);
          } else if (verificationStatus === "rejected") {
            setIdentityStatus("rejected");
          } else if (
            profileIdentityStatus === "pending" ||
            profileIdentityStatus === "processing" ||
            profileIdentityStatus === "manual_review" ||
            profileIdentityStatus === "rejected"
          ) {
            setIdentityStatus(profileIdentityStatus as VerificationStatus);
          } else {
            setIdentityStatus("unverified");
          }
        }
      }

      if (!cancelled) {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void Promise.resolve().then(loadVerification);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router]);

  async function handleSendCode(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const normalizedPhone = phone.trim();

    if (!normalizedPhone) {
      setError("Informe seu número de celular.");
      return;
    }

    if (normalizedPhone.length < 10) {
      setError("Informe um celular válido com DDD.");
      return;
    }

    setSendingCode(true);

    try {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const { data, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc(
        "request_phone_verification",
        {
          p_phone: normalizedPhone,
        },
      );

      if (rpcError) {
        setError(
          rpcError.message || "Não foi possível enviar o código.",
        );
        return;
      }

      setPhone(normalizedPhone);

      /*
       * MODO DE DESENVOLVIMENTO.
       * O código é exibido enquanto o fluxo está sendo testado
       * sem provedor de SMS/WhatsApp.
       * Remover esta informação antes de publicar em produção.
       */
      if (data) {
        setMessage(`Código de teste: ${data}`);
      } else {
        setMessage("Código enviado. Confira seu celular.");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Não foi possível solicitar o código.");
    } finally {
      setSendingCode(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleVerifyCode(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const normalizedCode = code.trim();

    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(normalizedCode)) {
      setError("Digite o código de 6 dígitos.");
      return;
    }

    setVerifyingCode(true);

    try {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const { data, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc(
        "verify_phone_code",
        {
          p_code: normalizedCode,
        },
      );

      if (rpcError) {
        setError(
          rpcError.message || "Não foi possível validar o código.",
        );
        return;
      }

      if (data !== true) {
        setError("Não foi possível confirmar seu celular.");
        return;
      }

      setPhoneVerified(true);
      setCode("");
      setMessage("Celular verificado com sucesso.");
    } catch {
      setError("Não foi possível validar o código.");
    } finally {
      setVerifyingCode(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleResend() {
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const normalizedPhone = phone.trim();

    if (!normalizedPhone) {
      setError("Informe seu número de celular.");
      return;
    }

    setSendingCode(true);

    try {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const { data, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc(
        "request_phone_verification",
        {
          p_phone: normalizedPhone,
        },
      );

      if (rpcError) {
        setError(
          rpcError.message || "Não foi possível reenviar o código.",
        );
        return;
      }

      if (data) {
        setMessage(`Novo código de teste: ${data}`);
      } else {
        setMessage("Novo código enviado.");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Não foi possível reenviar o código.");
    } finally {
      setSendingCode(false);
    }
  }

  function getIdentityLabel() {
    switch (identityStatus) {
      case "verified":
        return "Verificada";

      case "pending":
      case "processing":
      case "manual_review":
        return "Em análise";

      case "rejected":
        return "Necessita nova análise";

      default:
        return "Não verificada";
    }
  }

  function getIdentityColor() {
    switch (identityStatus) {
      case "verified":
        return "text-green-400";

      case "pending":
      case "processing":
      case "manual_review":
        return "text-orange-400";

      case "rejected":
        return "text-red-400";

      default:
        return "text-zinc-400";
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          Carregando verificação...
        </p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-label="Ir para minha área"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              priority
              unoptimized
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/cliente")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-green-400 transition hover:text-green-300"
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">←</span>
            <span>Voltar para minha área</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <div className="mb-5 inline-flex rounded-full border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-green-400">
            SEGURANÇA DA CONTA
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Verificação da sua conta
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
            A VemGO utiliza verificações para aumentar a segurança
            dos clientes e profissionais durante os atendimentos.
          </p>
        </div>

        {(message || error) && (
          <div
            role="status"
            className={[
              "mb-6 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm",
              error
                ? "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 text-red-300"
                : "border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 text-green-400",
            ].join(" ")}
          >
            {error || message}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* E-MAIL */}
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                  Etapa 1
                </p>

                <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
                  E-mail
                </h2>
              </div>

              <span
                className={
                  emailVerified
                    ? "text-sm font-medium text-green-400"
                    : "text-sm font-medium text-orange-400"
                }
              >
                {emailVerified ? "Verificado" : "Pendente"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <p className="break-all text-sm text-zinc-300">
              {email || "E-mail não informado"}
            </p>

            {!emailVerified && (
              <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-orange-300">
                Confirme seu e-mail para aumentar a segurança da
                conta.
              </p>
            )}
          </section>

          {/* CELULAR */}
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                  Etapa 2
                </p>

                <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
                  Celular
                </h2>
              </div>

              <span
                className={
                  phoneVerified
                    ? "text-sm font-medium text-green-400"
                    : "text-sm font-medium text-orange-400"
                }
              >
                {phoneVerified ? "Verificado" : "Pendente"}
              </span>
            </div>

            {phoneVerified ? (
              <>
                <p className="text-sm text-zinc-300">{phone}</p>

                <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-green-400">
                  Seu celular foi confirmado com sucesso.
                </p>
              </>
            ) : (
              <form
                onSubmit={handleSendCode}
                className="space-y-4"
              >
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="phone"
                    className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300"
                  >
                    Número de celular
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="phone"
                    type="tel"
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setPhone(event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="(14) 99999-9999"
                    autoComplete="tel"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-green-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={sendingCode}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl bg-green-500 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-green-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {sendingCode ? "Enviando..." : "Enviar código"}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </section>

          {/* IDENTIDADE */}
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                  Etapa 3
                </p>

                <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
                  Identidade
                </h2>
              </div>

              <span
                className={`text-sm font-medium ${getIdentityColor()}`}
              >
                {getIdentityLabel()}
              </span>
            </div>

            <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
              Documento e selfie são utilizados para confirmar
              sua identidade.
            </p>

            {identityStatus === "verified" ? (
              <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 p-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-green-400">
                  Identidade confirmada
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-green-400/80">
                  Sua conta está com a identidade verificada.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : identityStatus === "pending" ||
              identityStatus === "processing" ||
              identityStatus === "manual_review" ? (
              <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/10 p-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-300">
                  Documentos em análise
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-orange-200/70">
                  Aguarde a conclusão da análise.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  router.push("/cliente/verificacao/identidade")
                }
                className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-zinc-800"
              >
                Enviar documento e selfie
              </button>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>

        {/* CÓDIGO DO CELULAR */}
        {!phoneVerified && phone && (
          <section className="mt-6 max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-6">
            <div className="mb-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                Confirmação
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
                Digite o código recebido
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                Informe o código de 6 dígitos para confirmar seu
                celular.
              </p>
            </div>

            <form
              onSubmit={handleVerifyCode}
              className="space-y-4"
            >
              <input
                type="text"
                inputMode="numeric"
                maxLength={6}
                value={code}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setCode(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))
                }
                placeholder="000000"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-4 text-center text-2xl font-bold tracking-[0.5em] text-white outline-none transition placeholder:text-zinc-700 focus:border-green-500"
              />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={verifyingCode || code.length !== 6}
