"use client";



/* eslint-disable react-hooks/exhaustive-deps */



import Image from "next/image";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";



type AppointmentStatus =

  | "scheduled"

  | "confirmed"

  | "in_progress"

  | "completed"

  | "cancelled"

  | "no_show";



type Appointment = {

  id: string;

  order_id: string;

  professional_id: string;

  starts_at: string;

  ends_at: string;

  status: AppointmentStatus;

  confirmed_at: string | null;

  cancelled_at: string | null;

  cancellation_reason: string | null;

};



type Order = {

  id: string;

  client_id: string;

  professional_id: string;

  service_id: string;

  price: number;

  currency: string;

  status: string;

};



type Service = {

  id: string;

  name: string;

  code: string;

};



function formatDate(date: string) {

  return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", {

    weekday: "long",

    day: "2-digit",

    month: "2-digit",

    year: "numeric",

  });

}



function formatTime(date: string) {

  return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", {

    hour: "2-digit",

    minute: "2-digit",

  });

}



function formatPrice(price: number) {

  return Number(price).toLocaleString("pt-BR", {

    style: "currency",

    currency: "BRL",

  });

}



function statusLabel(status: AppointmentStatus) {

  switch (status) {

    case "scheduled":

      return "Aguardando confirmação";



    case "confirmed":

      return "Confirmado";



    case "in_progress":

      return "Em andamento";



    case "completed":

      return "Concluído";



    case "cancelled":

      return "Cancelado";



    case "no_show":

      return "Não compareceu";



    default:

      return status;

  }

}



function statusClass(status: AppointmentStatus) {

  switch (status) {

    case "scheduled":

      return "border-orange-500/40 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400";



    case "confirmed":

      return "border-orange-500/40 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400";



    case "in_progress":

      return "border-orange-500/40 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400";



    case "completed":

      return "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-300";



    case "cancelled":

      return "border-red-900 bg-red-950/30 text-red-400";



    case "no_show":

      return "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-400";



    default:

      return "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-300";

  }

}



function AppointmentCard({

  appointment,

  order,

  service,

  pending = false,

}: {

  appointment: Appointment;

  order: Order | undefined;

  service: Service | undefined;

  pending?: boolean;

}) {

  const router = useRouter();



  const title = service?.name || "Serviço VemGO";



  return (

    <button

      type="button"

      onClick={() =>

        router.push(

          `/profissional/agendamento/${appointment.order_id}`,

        )

      }

      className={`w-full rounded-2xl border bg-black p-5 text-left transition ${

        pending

          ? "border-orange-500/60 hover:border-orange-400"

          : "border-zinc-800 hover:border-orange-600"

      }`}

    >

      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">

        <div className="min-w-0">

          {pending && (

            <div className="mb-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-orange-500/40 bg-orange-500/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-orange-400">

              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-orange-500" />

              Nova solicitação

            </div>

          )}



          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

            Serviço

          </p>



          <h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-white">

            {title}

          </h3>



          {service?.code && (

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-600">

              Código: {service.code}

            </p>

          )}



          <div className="mt-5">

            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

              Data

            </p>



            <p className="mt-1 font-medium text-zinc-200">

              {formatDate(appointment.starts_at)}

            </p>



            <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold text-orange-500">

              {formatTime(appointment.starts_at)} às{" "}

              {formatTime(appointment.ends_at)}

            </p>

          </div>

        </div>



        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-start gap-3 sm:items-end">

          <span

            className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold ${statusClass(

              appointment.status,

            )}`}

          >

            {statusLabel(appointment.status)}

          </span>



          {order && (

            <div className="text-left sm:text-right">

              <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                Valor

              </p>



              <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold text-white">

                {formatPrice(Number(order.price))}

              </p>

            </div>

          )}



          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400">

            Ver detalhes →

          </span>

        </div>

      </div>



      {pending && (

        <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/5 px-4 py-3">

          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-orange-300">

            O cliente solicitou este horário. Acesse para

            confirmar ou recusar.

          </p>

        </div>

      )}

    </button>

  );

}



export default function ProfessionalAppointmentsPage() {

  const router = useRouter();



  const [name, setName] = useState("Profissional");
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState<Appointment[]>(

    [],

  );

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Record<string, Order>>({});

  const [services, setServices] = useState<Record<string, Service>>(

    {},

  );

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [error, setError] = useState("");



  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



  useEffect(() => {

    let cancelled = false;



    async function loadPage() {

      setLoading(true);

      setError("");



      const {

        data: { user },

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();



      if (!user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      const { data: profile, error: profileError } =

        await supabase

          .from("profiles")

          .select("full_name, avatar_url, user_type")

          .eq("id", user.id)

          .maybeSingle();



      if (cancelled) return;



      if (profileError) {

        setError(

          `Não foi possível carregar seu perfil: ${profileError.message}`,

        );

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      if (!profile || profile.user_type !== "professional") {

        router.replace("/cliente");

        return;

      }



      const metadataName =
        typeof user.user_metadata?.full_name === "string"
          ? user.user_metadata.full_name
          : typeof user.user_metadata?.name === "string"
            ? user.user_metadata.name
            : "";
      const displayName = (profile.full_name || metadataName).trim();
      setName(displayName.split(/\s+/)[0] || "Profissional");

      // Carrega a foto do profissional; se o bucket for privado, usa URL assinada.
      setAvatarUrl(null);
      if (profile.avatar_url) {
        const storedAvatar = profile.avatar_url as string;
        if (storedAvatar.startsWith("https://") || storedAvatar.startsWith("http://")) {
          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);
        } else {
          const { data: signedImage, error: imageError } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(storedAvatar, 3600);

          if (!cancelled && !imageError && signedImage?.signedUrl) {
            setAvatarUrl(signedImage.signedUrl);
          }
        }
      }



      const {

        data: appointmentData,

        error: appointmentError,

      } = await supabase

        .from("appointments")

        .select(

          "id, order_id, professional_id, starts_at, ends_at, status, confirmed_at, cancelled_at, cancellation_reason",

        )

        .eq("professional_id", user.id)

        .order("starts_at", { ascending: true });



      if (cancelled) return;



      if (appointmentError) {

        setError(

          `Não foi possível carregar os agendamentos: ${appointmentError.message}`,

        );

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      const appointmentRows =

        (appointmentData || []) as Appointment[];



      setAppointments(appointmentRows);



      const orderIds = [

        ...new Set(

          appointmentRows.map(

            (appointment) => appointment.order_id,

          ),

        ),

      ];



      if (orderIds.length === 0) {

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      const { data: orderData, error: orderError } =

        await supabase

          .from("orders")

          .select(

            "id, client_id, professional_id, service_id, price, currency, status",

          )

          .in("id", orderIds);



      if (cancelled) return;



      if (orderError) {

        setError(

          `Não foi possível carregar os pedidos: ${orderError.message}`,

        );

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      const orderRows = (orderData || []) as Order[];



      const orderMap: Record<string, Order> = {};



      for (const order of orderRows) {

        orderMap[order.id] = order;

      }



      setOrders(orderMap);



      const serviceIds = [

        ...new Set(

          orderRows.map((order) => order.service_id),

        ),

      ];



      if (serviceIds.length > 0) {

        const { data: serviceData, error: serviceError } =

          await supabase

            .from("services")

            .select("id, name, code")

            .in("id", serviceIds);



        if (cancelled) return;



        if (!serviceError) {

          const serviceRows =

            (serviceData || []) as Service[];



          const serviceMap: Record<string, Service> = {};



          for (const service of serviceRows) {

            serviceMap[service.id] = service;

          }



          setServices(serviceMap);

        }

      }



      setLoading(false);

    }



    void loadPage();



    return () => {

      cancelled = true;

    };

  }, [router]);



  async function handleLogout() {

    await supabase.auth.signOut();

    router.replace("/login");

  }



  const pendingAppointments = useMemo(

    () =>

      appointments.filter(

        (appointment) => appointment.status === "scheduled",

      ),

    [appointments],

  );



  const confirmedAppointments = useMemo(

    () =>

      appointments.filter(

        (appointment) => appointment.status === "confirmed",

      ),

    [appointments],

  );



  const otherAppointments = useMemo(

    () =>

      appointments.filter(

        (appointment) =>

          appointment.status !== "scheduled" &&

          appointment.status !== "confirmed",

      ),

    [appointments],

  );



  if (loading) {

    return (

      <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] px-6 py-12 text-white">

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">

          <p className="text-zinc-400">

            Carregando agendamentos...

          </p>

        </div>

      </main>

    );

  }



  return (

    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] text-white">

      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-6 py-8">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-label="Voltar para a área profissional"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              priority
              unoptimized
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
            />
          </button>

          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center justify-end gap-4">
            <div className="min-w-0 text-right">
              <p className="text-sm text-orange-400">Área do profissional</p>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-white sm:text-2xl">
                Olá, {name}!
              </h1>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleLogout}
                className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-white"
              >
                Sair
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900">
              {avatarUrl ? (
                <Image
                  src={avatarUrl}
                  alt={`Foto de ${name}`}
                  width={56}
                  height={56}
                  unoptimized
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-400">
                  {name.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "P"}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>



      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">

        <button

          type="button"

          onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}

          className="text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:text-orange-300"

        >

          ← Voltar para minha área

        </button>



        <div className="mt-8">

          <div className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-orange-400">

            AGENDA PROFISSIONAL

          </div>



          <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold">

            Agendamento

          </h1>



          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">

            Gerencie solicitações de horário e serviços

            agendados.

          </p>

        </div>



        {error && (

          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 p-5">

            <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{error}</p>

          </div>

        )}



        {pendingAppointments.length > 0 && (

          <section className="mt-10">

            <div className="mb-4">

              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-orange-400">

                🔔 Solicitações aguardando resposta

              </h2>



              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                Clientes escolheram estes horários e aguardam

                sua confirmação.

              </p>

            </div>



            <div className="grid gap-4">

              {pendingAppointments.map((appointment) => {

                const order = orders[appointment.order_id];



                const service = order

                  ? services[order.service_id]

                  : undefined;



                return (

                  <AppointmentCard

                    key={appointment.id}

                    appointment={appointment}

                    order={order}

                    service={service}

                    pending

                  />

                );

              })}

            </div>

          </section>

        )}



        {confirmedAppointments.length > 0 && (

          <section className="mt-10">

            <div className="mb-4">

              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">

                Agendamentos confirmados

              </h2>



              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                Serviços que já possuem horário confirmado.

              </p>

            </div>



            <div className="grid gap-4">

              {confirmedAppointments.map((appointment) => {

                const order = orders[appointment.order_id];



                const service = order

                  ? services[order.service_id]

                  : undefined;



                return (

                  <AppointmentCard

                    key={appointment.id}

                    appointment={appointment}

                    order={order}

                    service={service}

                  />

                );

              })}

            </div>

          </section>

        )}



        {otherAppointments.length > 0 && (

          <section className="mt-10">

            <div className="mb-4">

              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">

                Histórico

              </h2>



              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                Agendamentos concluídos ou encerrados.

              </p>

            </div>



            <div className="grid gap-4">

              {otherAppointments.map((appointment) => {

                const order = orders[appointment.order_id];



                const service = order

                  ? services[order.service_id]

                  : undefined;



                return (

                  <AppointmentCard

                    key={appointment.id}

                    appointment={appointment}

                    order={order}

                    service={service}

                  />

                );

              })}

            </div>

          </section>

        )}



        {appointments.length === 0 && (

          <div className="mt-10 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-8 text-center">

            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 text-2xl">

              📅

            </div>



            <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold">

              Nenhum agendamento

            </h2>



            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500">

              Quando um cliente solicitar um horário, a

              solicitação aparecerá aqui.

            </p>

          </div>

        )}

      </section>

    </main>

  );

}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\profissional\disponibilidade\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Availability = {

  id: string;

  day_of_week: number;

  start_time: string;

  end_time: string;

  is_active: boolean;

};

const DAYS = [

  { value: 1, label: "Segunda-feira" },

  { value: 2, label: "Terça-feira" },

  { value: 3, label: "Quarta-feira" },

  { value: 4, label: "Quinta-feira" },

  { value: 5, label: "Sexta-feira" },

  { value: 6, label: "Sábado" },

  { value: 0, label: "Domingo" },

];

export default function ProfissionalDisponibilidadePage() {

  const router = useRouter();

  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState<Availability[]>([]);

  const [dayOfWeek, setDayOfWeek] = useState("1");

  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState("08:00");

  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState("18:00");

  const [name, setName] = useState("Profissional");

  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState("");

  const [avatarFailed, setAvatarFailed] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {

    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadAvailability() {

      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const {

        data: { user },

        error: authError,

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (authError || !user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase

        .from("profiles")

        .select("full_name, avatar_url, user_type, status")

        .eq("id", user.id)

        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (profileError || !profile) {

        setError("Não foi possível carregar seu perfil. Entre novamente.");

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }

      if (profile.status === "blocked") {

        await supabase.auth.signOut();

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }

      if (profile.user_type === "client") {

        router.replace("/cliente");

        return;

      }

      if (profile.user_type === "admin") {

        router.replace("/controle");

        return;

      }

      if (profile.user_type !== "professional") {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }

      const firstName = profile.full_name?.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || "Profissional";

      setName(firstName);

      // avatar_url pode ser uma URL completa ou o caminho de um arquivo privado.

      const storedAvatar =

        typeof profile.avatar_url === "string" ? profile.avatar_url.trim() : "";

      if (storedAvatar) {

        if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(storedAvatar)) {

          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);

          setAvatarFailed(false);

        } else {

          const storagePath = storedAvatar

            .replace(/^profile-photos\//, "")

            .replace(/^\//, "");

          const { data: signedUrlData, error: signedUrlError } = await supabase.storage

            .from("profile-photos")

            .createSignedUrl(storagePath, 60 * 60);

          if (cancelled) return;

          if (!signedUrlError && signedUrlData?.signedUrl) {

            setAvatarUrl(signedUrlData.signedUrl);

            setAvatarFailed(false);

          } else {

            setAvatarUrl("");

            setAvatarFailed(true);

          }

        }

      } else {

        setAvatarUrl("");

        setAvatarFailed(true);

      }

      const { data, error: availabilityError } = await supabase

        .from("professional_availability")

        .select("id, day_of_week, start_time, end_time, is_active")

        .eq("professional_id", user.id)

        .eq("is_active", true)

        .order("day_of_week", { ascending: true })

        .order("start_time", { ascending: true });

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (availabilityError) {

        setError(`Não foi possível carregar sua disponibilidade: ${availabilityError.message}`);

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }

      setAvailability(data || []);

      setLoading(false);

    }

    void loadAvailability();

    return () => {

      cancelled = true;

    };

  }, [router]);

  function getDayLabel(day: number) {

    return DAYS.find((item) => item.value === day)?.label || "Dia não identificado";

  }

  function handleAddAvailability() {

    setMessage("");

    setError("");

    if (!startTime || !endTime) {

      setError("Informe o horário inicial e final.");

      return;

    }

    if (startTime >= endTime) {

      setError("O horário inicial precisa ser anterior ao horário final.");

      return;

    }

    const selectedDay = Number(dayOfWeek);

    const alreadyExists = availability.some(

      (item) =>

        item.day_of_week === selectedDay &&

        item.start_time.slice(0, 5) === startTime &&

        item.end_time.slice(0, 5) === endTime,

    );

    if (alreadyExists) {

      setError("Esse horário já está cadastrado.");

      return;

    }

    setAvailability((current) => [

      ...current,

      {

        id: `new-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,

        day_of_week: selectedDay,

        start_time: startTime,

        end_time: endTime,

        is_active: true,

      },

    ].sort((a, b) => a.day_of_week - b.day_of_week || a.start_time.localeCompare(b.start_time)));

  }

  function handleRemoveAvailability(id: string) {

    setAvailability((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== id));

  }

  async function handleSave() {

    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

    setMessage("");

    setError("");

    if (availability.length === 0) {

      setError("Cadastre pelo menos um horário de disponibilidade.");

      return;

    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {

      const {

        data: { user },

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase

        .from("profiles")

        .select("user_type, status")

        .eq("id", user.id)

        .maybeSingle();

      if (

        profileError ||

        !profile ||

        profile.user_type !== "professional" ||

        profile.status !== "active"

      ) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }

      const { error: deactivateError } = await supabase

        .from("professional_availability")

        .update({ is_active: false })

        .eq("professional_id", user.id)

        .eq("is_active", true);

      if (deactivateError) {

        setError(`Não foi possível atualizar a disponibilidade: ${deactivateError.message}`);

        return;

      }

      const rows = availability.map((item) => ({

        professional_id: user.id,

        day_of_week: item.day_of_week,

        start_time: item.start_time,

        end_time: item.end_time,

        is_active: true,

      }));

      const { data: insertedAvailability, error: insertError } = await supabase

        .from("professional_availability")

        .insert(rows)

        .select("id, day_of_week, start_time, end_time, is_active");

      if (insertError) {

        setError(`Não foi possível salvar a disponibilidade: ${insertError.message}`);

        return;

      }

      setAvailability(insertedAvailability || []);

      setMessage("Disponibilidade salva com sucesso.");

    } catch {

      setError("Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao salvar a disponibilidade. Tente novamente.");

    } finally {

      setSaving(false);

    }

  }

  async function handleLogout() {

    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

    await supabase.auth.signOut();

    router.replace("/login");

  }

  if (loading) {

    return (

      <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] px-6 py-12 text-white">

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">

          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Carregando disponibilidade...</p>

        </div>

      </main>

    );

  }

  return (

    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] text-white">

      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">

        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[132px] max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">

          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}

            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"

            aria-label="Voltar para minha área profissional"

          >

            <Image

              src="/logo-vemgo.png"

              alt="VemGO"

              width={360}

              height={200}

              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"

              priority

              unoptimized

            />

          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 sm:gap-5">

            <div className="text-right">

              <p className="text-xs text-orange-400 sm:text-sm">Área do profissional</p>

              <p className="text-lg font-bold leading-tight text-white sm:text-2xl">

                Olá, {name}!

              </p>

              <button

                type="button"

                onClick={() => void handleLogout()}

                className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-orange-400"

              >

                Sair

              </button>

            </div>

            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-black">

              {avatarUrl && !avatarFailed ? (

                <Image

                  src={avatarUrl}

                  alt={`Foto de ${name}`}

                  width={56}

                  height={56}

                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
            unoptimized

                  onError={() => {

                    setAvatarUrl("");

                    setAvatarFailed(true);

                  }}

                />

              ) : (

                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-400" aria-label={`Avatar de ${name}`}>

                  {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}

                </span>

              )}

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-8">

        <button

          type="button"

          onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}

          className="mb-7 text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:text-orange-300"

        >

          ← Voltar para minha área

        </button>

        <div className="mb-8">

          <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-orange-400">

            Disponibilidade

          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold">Disponibilidade</h1>

          <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">

            Informe os dias e horários em que você pode atender serviços pela VemGO.

          </p>

        </div>

        {error && (

          <div role="alert" className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">

            {error}

          </div>

        )}

        {message && (

          <div role="status" className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-orange-900 bg-orange-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-orange-300">

            {message}

          </div>

        )}

        <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Adicionar horário</h2>

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">

            <div>

              <label htmlFor="day-of-week" className="mb-2 block text-sm text-zinc-400">

                Dia da semana

              </label>

              <select

                id="day-of-week"

                value={dayOfWeek}

                onChange={(event) => setDayOfWeek(event.target.value)}

                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition focus:border-orange-500"

              >

                {DAYS.map((day) => (

                  <option key={day.value} value={day.value}>

                    {day.label}

                  </option>

                ))}

              </select>

            </div>

            <div>

              <label htmlFor="start-time" className="mb-2 block text-sm text-zinc-400">

                Início

              </label>

              <input

                id="start-time"

                type="time"

                value={startTime}

                onChange={(event) => setStartTime(event.target.value)}

                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition focus:border-orange-500"

              />

            </div>

            <div>

              <label htmlFor="end-time" className="mb-2 block text-sm text-zinc-400">

                Fim

              </label>

              <input

                id="end-time"

                type="time"

                value={endTime}

                onChange={(event) => setEndTime(event.target.value)}

                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition focus:border-orange-500"

              />

            </div>

          </div>

          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={handleAddAvailability}

            className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-orange-600 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:bg-orange-950/30"

          >

            + Adicionar horário

          </button>

        </section>

        <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">

            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Minha disponibilidade</h2>

            <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">

              {availability.length} horário(s)

            </span>

          </div>

          {availability.length === 0 ? (

            <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-zinc-500">Nenhum horário cadastrado.</p>

          ) : (

            <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">

              {availability.map((item) => (

                <div

                  key={item.id}

                  className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"

                >

                  <div>

                    <p className="font-semibold text-white">{getDayLabel(item.day_of_week)}</p>

                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">

                      {item.start_time.slice(0, 5)} às {item.end_time.slice(0, 5)}

                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <button

                    type="button"

                    onClick={() => handleRemoveAvailability(item.id)}

                    className="rounded-lg border border-red-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-red-400 transition hover:bg-red-950/30"

                  >

                    Remover

                  </button>

                </div>

              ))}

            </div>

          )}

          <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">

            <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">

              Você pode cadastrar mais de um intervalo por dia.

            </p>

            <button

              type="button"

              onClick={() => void handleSave()}

              disabled={saving}

              className="rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"

            >

              {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar disponibilidade"}

            </button>

          </div>

        </section>

      </section>

    </main>

  );

}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\profissional\oportunidades\[id]\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";



import Image from "next/image";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";



type Opportunity = {

  match_id: string;

  request_id: string;

  description: string;

  urgency: string;

  request_status: string;

  service_code: string;

  service_name: string;

  price: number | null;

  city: string;

  state: string;

  neighborhood: string;

  match_score: number;

  match_status: string;

  sent_at: string;

};



type ResponseResult = {

  match_id: string;

  match_status: string;

  request_id: string;

  request_status: string;

  order_id: string | null;

};



export default function OportunidadeDetalhePage() {

  const router = useRouter();

  const params = useParams();



  const matchId =

    typeof params.id === "string"

      ? params.id

      : Array.isArray(params.id)

        ? params.id[0]

        : "";



  const [opportunity, setOpportunity] =

    useState<Opportunity | null>(null);



  const [name, setName] = useState("Profissional");

  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState("");

  const [avatarFailed, setAvatarFailed] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false);

  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);



  useEffect(() => {

    let cancelled = false;



    async function loadOpportunity() {

      if (!matchId) {

        setMessage("Oportunidade inválida.");

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



      const {

        data: { user },

      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();



      if (!user) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      const { data: profile, error: profileError } =

        await supabase

          .from("profiles")

          .select("full_name, avatar_url, user_type, status")

          .eq("id", user.id)

          .maybeSingle();



      if (cancelled) {

        return;

      }



      if (profileError || !profile) {

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      if (profile.status === "blocked") {

        await supabase.auth.signOut();

        router.replace("/login");

        return;

      }



      if (profile.user_type !== "professional") {

        if (profile.user_type === "client") {

          router.replace("/cliente");

        } else if (profile.user_type === "admin") {

          router.replace("/controle");

        } else if (profile.user_type === "company") {

          router.replace("/login");

        } else {

          router.replace("/login");

        }



        return;

      }



      const fullName = profile.full_name?.trim() || "";
      const firstName = fullName.split(/\s+/)[0] || "Profissional";
      setName(firstName);

      const storedAvatar =
        typeof profile.avatar_url === "string" ? profile.avatar_url.trim() : "";

      if (storedAvatar) {
        if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(storedAvatar)) {
          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);
        } else {
          const { data: signedData, error: signedError } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(storedAvatar, 3600);

          if (!cancelled && !signedError && signedData?.signedUrl) {
            setAvatarUrl(signedData.signedUrl);
          } else if (!cancelled) {
            setAvatarUrl("");
          }
        }
      } else {
        setAvatarUrl("");
      }

      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(

        "get_professional_opportunities",

      );



      if (cancelled) {

        return;

      }



      if (error) {

        setMessage(

          `Não foi possível carregar a oportunidade: ${error.message}`,

        );

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      const opportunities =

        (data || []) as Opportunity[];



      const selected = opportunities.find(

        (item) => item.match_id === matchId,

      );



      if (!selected) {

        setMessage(

          "Esta oportunidade não está mais disponível.",

        );

        setOpportunity(null);

        setLoading(false);

        return;

      }



      setOpportunity(selected);

      setLoading(false);

    }



    void loadOpportunity();



    return () => {

      cancelled = true;

    };

  }, [matchId, router]);



  async function handleLogout() {

    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



    await supabase.auth.signOut();



    router.replace("/login");

  }



  async function handleResponse(

    decision: "accepted" | "declined",

  ) {

    if (!matchId || processing || confirmed) {

      return;

    }



    setMessage("");

    setProcessing(true);



    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();



    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(

      "respond_to_professional_opportunity",

      {

        p_match_id: matchId,

        p_decision: decision,

      },

    );



    if (error) {

      setMessage(

        `Não foi possível responder à oportunidade: ${error.message}`,

      );

      setProcessing(false);

      return;

    }



    const result = Array.isArray(data)

      ? (data[0] as ResponseResult | undefined)

      : (data as ResponseResult | null);



    if (!result) {

      setMessage(

        "A resposta foi processada, mas não recebemos os dados do pedido.",

      );

      setProcessing(false);

      return;

    }



    if (decision === "declined") {

      router.push("/profissional/oportunidades");

      return;

    }



    setConfirmed(true);

    setOrderId(result.order_id);

    setProcessing(false);

  }



  function formatPrice(price: number | null) {

    if (price === null || price === undefined) {

      return "Preço não informado";

    }



    return price.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {

      style: "currency",

      currency: "BRL",

    });

  }



  function formatDate(date: string) {

    return new Date(date).toLocaleString("pt-BR", {

      dateStyle: "short",

      timeStyle: "short",

    });

  }



  if (loading) {

    return (

      <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] px-6 py-12 text-white">

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">

          Carregando oportunidade...

        </div>

      </main>

    );

  }



  return (

    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] text-white">

      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[136px] max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-label="Voltar para minha área profissional"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              priority
              unoptimized
            />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-xs text-orange-400">Área do profissional</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white sm:text-2xl">
                Olá, {name}!
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleLogout}
                className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-orange-400"
              >
                Sair
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950">
              {avatarUrl && !avatarFailed ? (
                <Image
                  src={avatarUrl}
                  alt={`Foto de ${name}`}
                  width={56}
                  height={56}
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  onError={() => setAvatarFailed(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-400">
                  {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "P"}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>



      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-8">

        {!confirmed && (

          <button

            type="button"

            onClick={() =>

              router.push("/profissional/oportunidades")

            }

            className="mb-6 text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:text-orange-300"

          >

            ← Voltar para oportunidades

          </button>

        )}



        {confirmed ? (

          <>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-700/40 bg-emerald-950/30 p-8">

              <div className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-emerald-400">

                SERVIÇO CONFIRMADO

              </div>



              <h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-bold">

                Serviço aceito com sucesso!

              </h1>



              <p className="mt-3 text-zinc-300">

                Você aceitou esta oportunidade. O pedido foi criado

                e o serviço agora está confirmado para você.

              </p>

            </div>



            {opportunity && (

              <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-start">

                  <div>

                    <span className="rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-orange-400">

                      {opportunity.service_code}

                    </span>



                    <h2 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold">

                      {opportunity.service_name}

                    </h2>



                    <p className="mt-3 text-zinc-300">

                      {opportunity.description}

                    </p>

                  </div>



                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-500/10 px-6 py-4 text-center">

                    <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">

                      Valor do serviço

                    </p>



                    <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-orange-400">

                      {formatPrice(opportunity.price)}

                    </p>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </div>

            )}



            {opportunity && (

              <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">

                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                  <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                    Local do serviço

                  </h2>



                  <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">

                    <div>

                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                        Bairro

                      </p>



                      <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                        {opportunity.neighborhood}

                      </p>

                    </div>



                    <div>

                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                        Cidade

                      </p>



                      <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                        {opportunity.city}/{opportunity.state}

                      </p>

                    </div>

                  </div>

                </div>



                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                  <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                    Pedido

                  </h2>



                  <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">

                    <div>

                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                        Status

                      </p>



                      <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-emerald-400">

                        Confirmado

                      </p>

                    </div>



                    <div>

                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                        Número do pedido

                      </p>



                      <p className="mt-1 break-all text-sm text-zinc-200">

                        {orderId || "Pedido criado"}

                      </p>

                    </div>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </div>

            )}



            <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                Próximo passo

              </h2>



              <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">

                Agora que você aceitou o serviço, escolha uma data

                e um horário dentro da sua disponibilidade para

                realizar o atendimento.

              </p>



              <button

                type="button"

                disabled={!orderId}

                onClick={() => {

                  if (!orderId) return;



                  router.push(

                    `/profissional/agendamento/${orderId}`,

                  );

                }}

                className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-4 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"

              >

                Agendar serviço

              </button>

            </div>



            <button

              type="button"

              onClick={() =>

                router.push("/profissional")

              }

              className="mt-4 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-6 py-4 font-semibold text-zinc-300 transition hover:border-orange-500 hover:text-orange-400"

            >

              Voltar para meu painel

            </button>

          </>

        ) : (

          <>

            <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-orange-600/40 bg-orange-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-orange-400">

              DETALHES DA OPORTUNIDADE

            </div>



            {message ? (

              <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 p-6">

                <p className="text-sm text-red-300">

                  {message}

                </p>



                <button

                  type="button"

                  onClick={() =>

                    router.push(

                      "/profissional/oportunidades",

                    )

                  }

                  className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400"

                >

                  Voltar para oportunidades

                </button>

              </div>

            ) : opportunity ? (

              <>

                <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                  <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-start">

                    <div>

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">

                        <span className="rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-orange-400">

                          {opportunity.service_code}

                        </span>



                        <span className="rounded-full border border-zinc-700 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-zinc-400">

                          Nova oportunidade

                        </span>

                      </div>



                      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold">

                        {opportunity.service_name}

                      </h1>



                      <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-zinc-300">

                        {opportunity.description}

                      </p>

                    </div>



                    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-500/10 px-6 py-4 text-center">

                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">

                        Valor do serviço

                      </p>



                      <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-orange-400">

                        {formatPrice(opportunity.price)}

                      </p>

                    </div>

                  </div>

                </div>



                <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">

                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                      Local do serviço

                    </h2>



                    <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">

                      <div>

                        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                          Bairro

                        </p>



                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                          {opportunity.neighborhood}

                        </p>

                      </div>



                      <div>

                        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                          Cidade

                        </p>



                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                          {opportunity.city}/{opportunity.state}

                        </p>

                      </div>

                    </div>

                  </div>



                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                      Informações

                    </h2>



                    <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">

                      <div>

                        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                          Urgência

                        </p>



                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                          {opportunity.urgency === "urgent"

                            ? "Urgente"

                            : "Normal"}

                        </p>

                      </div>



                      <div>

                        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                          Recebida em

                        </p>



                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                          {formatDate(opportunity.sent_at)}

                        </p>

                      </div>



                      <div>

                        <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">

                          Serviço

                        </p>



                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">

                          {opportunity.service_code}

                        </p>

                      </div>

                    </div>

                  </div>

                </div>



                <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6">

                  <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">

                    O que acontece agora?

                  </h2>



                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">

                    Esta oportunidade foi enviada porque o seu

                    cadastro possui o serviço solicitado e atende

                    à região informada pelo cliente.

                  </p>



                  <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 p-4">

                    <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">

                      Status da oportunidade

                    </p>



                    <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-orange-400">

                      Aguardando sua resposta

                    </p>

                  </div>

                </div>



                <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">

                  <button

                    type="button"

                    onClick={() =>

                      handleResponse("accepted")

                    }

                    disabled={processing}

                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-4 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"

                  >

                    {processing

                      ? "Processando..."

                      : "Aceitar oportunidade"}

                  </button>



                  <button

                    type="button"

                    onClick={() =>

                      handleResponse("declined")

                    }

                    disabled={processing}

                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-6 py-4 font-semibold text-zinc-300 transition hover:border-red-500 hover:text-red-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"

                  >

                    {processing

                      ? "Processando..."

                      : "Recusar oportunidade"}

                  </button>

                </div>



                {message && (

                  <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm text-red-300">

                    {message}

                  </div>

                )}

              </>

            ) : null}

          </>

        )}

      </section>

    </main>

  );

}

ARQUIVO: app\profissional\oportunidades\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

type Opportunity = {
  match_id: string;
  request_id: string;
  description: string;
  urgency: string;
  request_status: string;
  service_code: string;
  service_name: string;
  price: number | null;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  match_score: number;
  match_status: string;
  sent_at: string;
};

export default function OportunidadesPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [opportunities, setOpportunities] = useState<Opportunity[]>([]);
  const [name, setName] = useState("Profissional");
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [avatarFailed, setAvatarFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadOpportunities() {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("full_name, avatar_url, user_type, status")
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (profileError || !profile) {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      if (profile.status === "blocked") {
        await supabase.auth.signOut();
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      if (profile.user_type !== "professional") {
        if (profile.user_type === "client") {
          router.replace("/cliente");
        } else if (profile.user_type === "admin") {
          router.replace("/controle");
        } else {
          router.replace("/login");
        }
        return;
      }

      const fullName = profile.full_name?.trim() || "";
      const firstName = fullName.split(/\s+/)[0] || "Profissional";
      setName(firstName);

      // Reutiliza o campo avatar_url. Se ele guardar um caminho do bucket
      // privado, gera uma URL assinada; se já for uma URL, tenta usá-la.
      const storedAvatar = profile.avatar_url?.trim() || "";
      if (storedAvatar) {
        if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(storedAvatar)) {
          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);
        } else {
          const { data: signedData, error: signedError } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(storedAvatar, 3600);

          if (!cancelled && !signedError && signedData?.signedUrl) {
            setAvatarUrl(signedData.signedUrl);
          } else if (!cancelled) {
            setAvatarUrl("");
          }
        }
      } else {
        setAvatarUrl("");
      }

      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(
        "get_professional_opportunities",
      );

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (error) {
        setMessage(
          `Não foi possível carregar as oportunidades: ${error.message}`,
        );
        setOpportunities([]);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setOpportunities((data || []) as Opportunity[]);
      setLoading(false);
    }

    void loadOpportunities();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [router]);

  async function handleLogout() {
    const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    router.replace("/login");
  }

  function formatPrice(price: number | null) {
    if (price === null || price === undefined) {
      return "Preço não informado";
    }

    return price.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
      style: "currency",
      currency: "BRL",
    });
  }

  function formatDate(date: string) {
    const parsedDate = new Date(date);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime())) return "Data não disponível";

    return parsedDate.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
      dateStyle: "short",
      timeStyle: "short",
    });
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] px-6 py-12 text-white">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">Carregando oportunidades...</div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#0C0A09] text-white">
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-black">
        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[136px] max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-6 py-5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-label="Voltar para minha área profissional"
          >
            <Image
              src="/logo-vemgo.png"
              alt="VemGO"
              width={360}
              height={200}
              className="h-auto w-[190px] object-contain"
              priority
              unoptimized
            />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="text-xs text-orange-400">Área do profissional</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-white sm:text-2xl">
                Olá, {name}!
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleLogout}
                className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-orange-400"
              >
                Sair
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-950">
              {avatarUrl && !avatarFailed ? (
                <Image
                  src={avatarUrl}
                  alt={`Foto de ${name}`}
                  width={56}
                  height={56}
                  unoptimized
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  onError={() => setAvatarFailed(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-400">
                  {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "P"}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-8">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}
          className="mb-6 text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:text-orange-300"
        >
          ← Voltar para minha área
        </button>

        <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-orange-600/40 bg-orange-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-orange-400">
          OPORTUNIDADES
        </div>

        <h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-bold">Serviços disponíveis</h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">
          Aqui aparecem as solicitações compatíveis com seus serviços e região.
        </p>

        {message && (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        {opportunities.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-8 text-center">
            <p className="text-lg font-semibold">
              Nenhuma oportunidade no momento
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500">
              Quando surgir uma solicitação compatível, ela aparecerá aqui.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 grid gap-5">
            {opportunities.map((opportunity) => (
              <div
                key={opportunity.match_id}
                className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-black p-6 shadow-sm"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 md:flex-row">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-orange-400">
                        {opportunity.service_code}
                      </span>
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-zinc-700 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-zinc-400">
                        Nova oportunidade
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold">
                      {opportunity.service_name}
                    </h2>

                    <p className="mt-3 text-zinc-300">
                      {opportunity.description}
                    </p>

                    <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-zinc-500">Local</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">
                          {opportunity.neighborhood} — {opportunity.city}/
                          {opportunity.state}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <p className="text-zinc-500">Valor do serviço</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-orange-400">
                          {formatPrice(opportunity.price)}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <p className="text-zinc-500">Urgência</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">
                          {opportunity.urgency === "urgent" ? "Urgente" : "Normal"}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <p className="text-zinc-500">Recebida em</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-200">
                          {formatDate(opportunity.sent_at)}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex shrink-0 items-start">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        router.push(
                          `/profissional/oportunidades/${opportunity.match_id}`,
                        )
                      }
                      className="w-full rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 md:w-auto"
                    >
                      Ver oportunidade
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\profissional\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";
import {
  getProfessionalOnboardingRoute,
  PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_STEP,
} from "@/lib/professional-onboarding";

type Appointment = {
  id: string;
  order_id: string;
  starts_at: string;
  ends_at: string;
  status: string;
};

type Order = {
  id: string;
  service_id: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  status: string;
};

type Service = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  code: string;
};

type Profile = {
  full_name: string | null;
  avatar_url?: string | null;
  user_type: string;
  status: string;
  identity_status: string | null;
  professional_onboarding_step: number | null;
};

type Professional = {
  verification_status: string | null;
  service_status: string | null;
  is_accepting_calls: boolean;
};

function formatDate(value: string) {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", {
    weekday: "short",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
  });
}

function formatTime(value: string) {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", {
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

function formatPrice(value: number, currency = "BRL") {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
  }).format(Number(value));
}

function statusLabel(status: string) {
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    scheduled: "Agendado",
    confirmed: "Confirmado",
    in_progress: "Em andamento",
    completed: "Concluído",
    cancelled: "Cancelado",
    no_show: "Não compareceu",
  };

  return labels[status] ?? status;
}

function statusClass(status: string) {
  if (status === "confirmed") {
    return "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400";
  }

  if (status === "in_progress") {
    return "border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400";
  }

  if (status === "completed") {
    return "border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400";
  }

  if (status === "cancelled" || status === "no_show") {
    return "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 text-red-400";
  }

  return "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300";
}

export default function ProfissionalPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [professional, setProfessional] = useState<Professional | null>(null);
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState<Appointment[]>([]);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Record<string, Order>>({});
  const [services, setServices] = useState<Record<string, Service>>({});
  const [opportunityCount, setOpportunityCount] = useState(0);
  const [pendingAppointmentCount, setPendingAppointmentCount] = useState(0);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [savingAvailability, setSavingAvailability] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [availabilityMessage, setAvailabilityMessage] = useState("");

  const loadProfessional = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

      const {
        data: { user },
        error: userError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (userError || !user) {
        router.replace("/login?role=professional");
        return;
      }

      const { data: profileData, error: profileError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select(
          "full_name, avatar_url, user_type, status, identity_status, professional_onboarding_step",
        )
        .eq("id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (profileError || !profileData) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível carregar seu perfil: ${profileError?.message ?? "perfil não encontrado"}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const currentProfile = profileData as Profile;

      // O banco guarda o caminho do arquivo. Como o bucket é privado,
      // criamos uma URL assinada temporária para exibir a foto.
      setAvatarUrl(null);
      if (currentProfile.avatar_url) {
        const storedAvatar = currentProfile.avatar_url;
        if (storedAvatar.startsWith("https://") || storedAvatar.startsWith("http://")) {
          setAvatarUrl(storedAvatar);
        } else {
          const { data: signedImage, error: imageError } = await supabase.storage
            .from("profile-photos")
            .createSignedUrl(storedAvatar, 3600);

          if (!imageError && signedImage?.signedUrl) {
            setAvatarUrl(signedImage.signedUrl);
          }
        }
      }

      if (currentProfile.status === "blocked") {
        await supabase.auth.signOut();
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      if (currentProfile.user_type === "client") {
        router.replace("/cliente");
        return;
      }

      if (currentProfile.user_type === "admin") {
        router.replace("/controle");
        return;
      }

      if (currentProfile.user_type === "company") {
        setError(
          "Contas empresariais ainda não estão disponíveis nesta versão da VemGO.",
        );
        return;
      }

      if (currentProfile.user_type !== "professional") {
        router.replace("/login");
        return;
      }

      const onboardingStep =
        currentProfile.professional_onboarding_step ?? 1;

      if (onboardingStep < PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_STEP) {
        router.replace(getProfessionalOnboardingRoute(onboardingStep));
        return;
      }

      setProfile(currentProfile);

      const [
        { data: professionalData, error: professionalError },
        { data: appointmentData, error: appointmentError },
      ] = await Promise.all([
        supabase
          .from("professionals")
          .select(
            "verification_status, service_status, is_accepting_calls",
          )
          .eq("id", user.id)
          .maybeSingle(),

        supabase
          .from("appointments")
          .select("id, order_id, starts_at, ends_at, status")
          .eq("professional_id", user.id)
          .order("starts_at", { ascending: true }),
      ]);

      if (professionalError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível carregar o status profissional: ${professionalError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      if (appointmentError) {
        setError(
          `Não foi possível carregar seus agendamentos: ${appointmentError.message}`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const currentProfessional = professionalData as Professional | null;
      setProfessional(currentProfessional);

      // Só consulta oportunidades quando o perfil está liberado e ON.
      const canReceiveCalls =
        currentProfile.identity_status === "verified" &&
        currentProfessional?.verification_status === "approved" &&
        currentProfessional?.service_status === "active" &&
        currentProfessional?.is_accepting_calls === true;

      if (canReceiveCalls) {
        const { data: opportunityData, error: opportunityError } =
          await supabase.rpc("get_professional_opportunities");

        if (opportunityError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível carregar oportunidades: ${opportunityError.message}`,
          );
        } else {
          setOpportunityCount(
            (opportunityData ?? []).filter(
              (item: { match_status?: string }) =>
                item.match_status === "sent" ||
                item.match_status === "pending",
            ).length,
          );
        }
      } else {
        setOpportunityCount(0);
      }

      const appointmentRows = (appointmentData ?? []) as Appointment[];
      setAppointments(appointmentRows);
      const pendingAppointments = appointmentRows.filter(
        (appointment) => appointment.status === "scheduled",
      );
      setPendingAppointmentCount(pendingAppointments.length);

      const orderIds = Array.from(
        new Set(appointmentRows.map((item) => item.order_id)),
      );

      if (orderIds.length > 0) {
        const { data: orderData, error: orderError } = await supabase
          .from("orders")
          .select("id, service_id, price, currency, status")
          .in("id", orderIds);

        if (orderError) {
          setError(
            `Não foi possível carregar os serviços agendados: ${orderError.message}`,
          );
          return;
        }

        const orderMap: Record<string, Order> = {};

        for (const order of (orderData ?? []) as Order[]) {
          orderMap[order.id] = order;
        }

        setOrders(orderMap);

        const serviceIds = Array.from(
          new Set(
            (orderData ?? []).map((order) => order.service_id),
          ),
        );

        if (serviceIds.length > 0) {
          const { data: serviceData, error: serviceError } = await supabase
            .from("services")
            .select("id, name, code")
            .in("id", serviceIds);

          if (serviceError) {
            setError(
              `Não foi possível carregar os dados dos serviços: ${serviceError.message}`,
            );
            return;
          }

          const serviceMap: Record<string, Service> = {};

          for (const service of (serviceData ?? []) as Service[]) {
            serviceMap[service.id] = service;
          }

          setServices(serviceMap);
        } else {
          setServices({});
        }
      } else {
        setOrders({});
        setServices({});
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Ocorreu um erro inesperado ao carregar sua área profissional.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      void loadProfessional();
    }, 0);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    };
  }, [loadProfessional]);

  const upcomingAppointments = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) =>
      appointment.status === "confirmed" ||
      appointment.status === "in_progress",
  );

  const historyAppointments = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) =>
      appointment.status === "completed" ||
      appointment.status === "cancelled" ||
      appointment.status === "no_show",
  );

  const firstName =
    profile?.full_name?.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || "Profissional";

  const identityVerified = profile?.identity_status === "verified";
  const approved = professional?.verification_status === "approved";
  const servicesActive = professional?.service_status === "active";

  const canManageAvailability =
    identityVerified && approved && servicesActive;
