            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-zinc-700 bg-black">

              {avatarUrl ? (

                // Se a URL da foto estiver inválida, mostra o logo em vez de uma imagem quebrada.

                <Image

                  src={avatarUrl}

                  alt="Foto do profissional"

                  width={56}

                  height={56}

                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
            unoptimized

                  onError={() => setAvatarUrl("")}

                />

              ) : (

                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-400">

                  {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}

                </span>

              )}

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-6 py-8">

        <button

          type="button"

          onClick={() => router.push("/profissional")}

          className="mb-7 text-sm font-semibold text-orange-400 transition hover:text-orange-300"

        >

          ← Voltar para minha área

        </button>

        <div className="mb-8">

          <div className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-orange-500/20 bg-orange-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-orange-400">

            Serviços

          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold">Minhas competências</h1>

          <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">

            Selecione os serviços que você está habilitado a executar. Essas competências serão usadas pela VemGO para encontrar oportunidades compatíveis com seu perfil.

          </p>

        </div>

        {message && (

          <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border border-emerald-900/60 bg-emerald-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-300">

            {message}

          </div>

        )}

        {error && (

          <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border border-red-900/60 bg-red-950/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">

            {error}

          </div>

        )}

        <div className="mb-6 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-[1fr_260px_auto]">

          <input

            type="search"

            value={search}

            onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}

            placeholder="Buscar por serviço ou código..."

            className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:border-orange-500"

          />

          <select

            value={categoryFilter}

            onChange={(event) => setCategoryFilter(event.target.value)}

            className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-orange-500"

          >

            <option value="all">Todas as categorias</option>

            {categories.map((category) => (

              <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>

                {category.name}

              </option>

            ))}

          </select>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm">

            <span className="text-zinc-400">Selecionados:</span>

            <span className="ml-2 font-bold text-orange-400">{selectedServices.size}</span>

          </div>

        </div>

        {filteredServices.length === 0 ? (

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-10 text-center">

            <p className="font-semibold text-zinc-300">Nenhum serviço encontrado.</p>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500">Tente alterar a busca ou a categoria.</p>

          </div>

        ) : (

          <div className="space-y-6">

            {groupedServices.map(({ category, services: categoryServices }) => (

              <section

                key={category?.id || categoryServices[0]?.category_id || "sem-categoria"}

                className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-5"

              >

                <div className="mb-5">

                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">

                    {category?.name || "Serviços sem categoria"}

                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">{categoryServices.length} serviço(s)</p>

                </div>

                <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">

                  {categoryServices.map((service) => {

                    const selected = selectedServices.has(service.id);

                    return (

                      <button

                        key={service.id}

                        type="button"

                        onClick={() => toggleService(service.id)}

                        className={`rounded-xl border p-4 text-left transition ${

                          selected

                            ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-950/20"

                            : "border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 hover:border-zinc-600"

                        }`}

                      >

                        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">

                          <div

                            className={`mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border text-xs font-bold ${

                              selected

                                ? "border-orange-500 bg-orange-500 text-black"

                                : "border-zinc-700 text-transparent"

                            }`}

                          >

                            ✓

                          </div>

                          <div className="min-w-0">

                            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-orange-400">{service.code}</p>

                            <p className="mt-1 font-semibold leading-5 text-white">{service.name}</p>

                            {service.description && (

                              <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-500">

                                {service.description}

                              </p>

                            )}

                          </div>

                        </div>

                      </button>

                    );

                  })}

                </div>

              </section>

            ))}

          </div>

        )}

        <div className="sticky bottom-0 mt-8 border-t border-zinc-800 bg-[#0C0A09]/95 py-5 backdrop-blur">

          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">

            <div>

              <p className="font-semibold text-zinc-200">

                {selectedServices.size} serviço(s) selecionado(s)

              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">

                Mantenha apenas os serviços que você realmente executa.

              </p>

            </div>

            <button

              type="button"

              onClick={() => void handleSave()}

              disabled={saving || selectedServices.size === 0}

              className="rounded-xl bg-orange-500 px-7 py-3 font-bold text-black transition hover:bg-orange-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"

            >

              {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar competências"}

            </button>

          </div>

        </div>

      </section>

    </main>

  );

}


====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\recuperar-senha\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";
import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
import Input from "@/components/ui/Input";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";

export default function RecuperarSenhaPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");
    setSent(false);

    if (!email.trim()) {
      setError("Informe seu e-mail.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    const { error: resetError } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(
      email.trim(),
      {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/redefinir-senha`,
      },
    );

    if (resetError) {
      setError(
        "Não foi possível enviar o link de recuperação. Verifique o e-mail informado e tente novamente.",
      );
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setSent(true);
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-black px-4 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="mb-8 text-center">
          <Link href="/" className="inline-block">
            <span className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-white">
              Vem<span className="text-[#00a86b]">GO</span>
            </span>
          </Link>

          <h1 className="mt-8 text-2xl font-bold">
            Recuperar senha
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">
            Informe seu e-mail e enviaremos um link para criar uma nova senha.
          </p>
        </div>

        <Card>
          {sent ? (
            <div className="space-y-6 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#00a86b]/30 bg-[#00a86b]/10 text-2xl text-[#00a86b]">
                ✓
              </div>

              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
                  E-mail enviado
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-400">
                  Se o e-mail informado estiver cadastrado, você receberá
                  um link para redefinir sua senha.
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 break-all text-sm text-zinc-300">
                  {email}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="primary"
                  className="w-full"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSent(false);
                    setError("");
                  }}
                >
                  Enviar novamente
                </Button>

                <Link href="/login" className="block">
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant="secondary"
                    className="w-full"
                  >
                    Voltar para o login
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
              <Input
                label="E-mail"
                type="email"
                placeholder="seu@email.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
                autoComplete="email"
                required
              />

              {error && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                className="w-full"
                disabled={loading}
              >
                {loading ? "Enviando..." : "Enviar link de recuperação"}
              </Button>

              <div className="text-center">
                <Link
                  href="/login"
                  className="text-sm text-zinc-400 transition hover:text-white"
                >
                  ← Voltar para o login
                </Link>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </Card>

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-zinc-600">
          VemGO · Serviço rápido. Preço certo. Chama que vem!
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: app\redefinir-senha\page.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from "@/lib/supabase-browser";

export default function RedefinirSenhaPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] =
    useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function checkSession() {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (!session) {
        setError(
          "O link de recuperação é inválido ou expirou.",
        );
      }

      setChecking(false);
    }

    void checkSession();
  }, [supabase]);

  async function handleUpdatePassword(
    event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError(
        "A senha precisa ter pelo menos 8 caracteres.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("As senhas não conferem.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    const { error: updateError } =
      await supabase.auth.updateUser({
        password,
      });

    if (updateError) {
      setError(
        `Não foi possível atualizar a senha: ${updateError.message}`,
      );
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setMessage(
      "Senha alterada com sucesso. Você será direcionado para o login.",
    );

    setLoading(false);

    setTimeout(() => {
      router.replace("/login");
    }, 1500);
  }

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-zinc-950 text-white">
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          Validando link de recuperação...
        </p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-12 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[80vh] max-w-md flex-col justify-center">
        <div className="mb-8 text-center">
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400">
            VemGO
          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-6 text-3xl font-bold">
            Nova senha
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-zinc-400">
            Cadastre uma nova senha para sua conta.
          </p>
        </div>

        <form
          onSubmit={handleUpdatePassword}
          className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/40 p-6"
        >
          <label
            htmlFor="password"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
          >
            Nova senha
          </label>

          <input
            id="password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setPassword(event.target.value)
            }
            required
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-500"
          />

          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">
            Mínimo de 8 caracteres.
          </p>

          <label
            htmlFor="confirmPassword"
            className="mt-5 block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200"
          >
            Confirmar nova senha
          </label>

          <input
            id="confirmPassword"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            value={confirmPassword}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)
            }
            required
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-500"
          />

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-900 bg-red-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {message && (
            <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-900 bg-emerald-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-300">
              {message}
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || Boolean(error)}
            className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading
              ? "Salvando..."
              : "Salvar nova senha"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

              plataforma.

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              Ao realizar o cadastro profissional e utilizar os recursos
              destinados aos profissionais, o usuário declara que leu e
              concorda com estes Termos, com os Termos de Uso e com a Política
              de Privacidade da VemGO.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              2. Cadastro profissional
            </h2>

            <p className="text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O profissional deverá fornecer informações verdadeiras, completas
              e atualizadas durante o cadastro, incluindo seus dados pessoais,
              informações de identificação, endereço, competências, serviços,
              preços, disponibilidade e área de atendimento.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              A VemGO poderá solicitar informações ou documentos adicionais
              para validação de identidade, segurança, prevenção a fraudes ou
              análise cadastral.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              3. Verificação e aprovação
            </h2>

            <p className="text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O cadastro profissional poderá estar sujeito a processos de
              verificação de identidade e análise de segurança antes da
              disponibilização integral dos recursos da plataforma.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O envio de informações ou documentos não garante aprovação ou
              disponibilidade imediata do perfil profissional.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              4. Serviços e competências
            </h2>

            <p className="text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O profissional deverá selecionar somente serviços e competências
              que esteja efetivamente habilitado e preparado para executar.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O profissional é responsável pela qualidade, segurança,
              regularidade e execução dos serviços que aceitar realizar,
              observadas as condições apresentadas na plataforma e a legislação
              aplicável.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              5. Preços
            </h2>

            <p className="text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O profissional define os preços dos serviços que oferece na
              plataforma, observadas as funcionalidades e regras comerciais
              disponibilizadas pela VemGO.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              A VemGO poderá disponibilizar faixas ou referências de preço para
              determinados serviços. Essas referências não substituem o preço
              definido pelo profissional.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O profissional deverá manter seus preços atualizados e respeitar
              o valor apresentado ao cliente no momento da contratação.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              6. Disponibilidade e área de atendimento
            </h2>

            <p className="text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">

              oportunidades compatíveis.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-3 text-justify [text-indent:1.5rem]">
              O cadastro de disponibilidade ou região não garante o recebimento

            </p>
          </section>

            </p>

          VemGO — Serviço rápido. Preço certo. Chama que vem!
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );

ARQUIVO: components\ui\Badge.tsx

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";

type BadgeVariant =
  | "default"
  | "success"
  | "warning"
  | "danger"
  | "info"
  | "professional";

type BadgeProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
};

const variants: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  default: "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300",
  success: "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400",
  warning: "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400",
  danger: "border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 text-red-400",
  info: "border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400",
  professional: "border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-400",
};

export default function Badge({
  variant = "default",
  className = "",
  children,
  ...props
}: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={[
        "inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1",
        "text-xs font-semibold",
        variants[variant],
        className,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components\ui\Button.tsx
====================================================================================================
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";

type ButtonVariant =
  | "primary"
  | "secondary"
  | "outline"
  | "danger"
  | "professional";

type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  fullWidth?: boolean;
};

const variants: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary:
    "bg-emerald-600 text-black hover:bg-emerald-500 focus-visible:ring-emerald-500",
  secondary:
    "bg-zinc-800 text-white hover:bg-zinc-700 focus-visible:ring-zinc-500",
  outline:
    "border border-zinc-700 bg-transparent text-zinc-200 hover:border-emerald-500 hover:text-emerald-400 focus-visible:ring-emerald-500",
  danger:
    "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-500 focus-visible:ring-red-500",
  professional:
    "bg-orange-500 text-black hover:bg-orange-400 focus-visible:ring-orange-500",
};

export default function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  fullWidth = false,
  className = "",
  disabled,
  children,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      disabled={disabled}
      className={[
        "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl px-5 py-3",
        "text-sm font-bold transition-all duration-200",
        "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-black",
        "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
        fullWidth ? "w-full" : "",
        variants[variant],
        className,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: components\ui\Card.tsx

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";

type CardProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & {
  padding?: "none" | "sm" | "md" | "lg";
};

const paddingClasses = {
  none: "p-0",
  sm: "p-4",
  md: "p-5",
  lg: "p-6",
};

export default function Card({
  padding = "md",
  className = "",
  children,
  ...props
}: CardProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={[
        "rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/70",
        "shadow-lg shadow-black/20",
        paddingClasses[padding],
        className,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components\ui\Input.tsx
====================================================================================================
import type {
  InputHTMLAttributes,
  TextareaHTMLAttributes,
} from "react";

type InputProps = InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
};

type TextareaProps = TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
};

const baseClasses =
  "w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-zinc-500 transition-colors focus:border-emerald-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50";

export function Input({
  label,
  error,
  hint,
  id,
  className = "",
  ...props
}: InputProps) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      {label && (
        <label
          htmlFor={id}
          className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200"
        >
          {label}
        </label>
      )}

      <input
        id={id}
        className={[
          baseClasses,
          error
            ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500"
            : "",
          className,
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")}
        {...props}
      />

      {error && (
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-400">{error}</p>
      )}

      {!error && hint && (
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-500">{hint}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Textarea({
  label,
  error,
  hint,
  id,
  className = "",
  ...props
}: TextareaProps) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      {label && (
        <label
          htmlFor={id}
          className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200"
        >
          {label}
        </label>
      )}

      <textarea
        id={id}
        className={[
          baseClasses,
          "min-h-32 resize-y",
          error
            ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500"
            : "",
          className,
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")}
        {...props}
      />

      {error && (
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-400">{error}</p>
      )}

      {!error && hint && (
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-500">{hint}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default Input;

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components\ui\Progress.tsx
====================================================================================================
type ProgressProps = {
  value: number;
  max?: number;
  label?: string;
  showValue?: boolean;
  variant?: "client" | "professional";
};

export default function Progress({
  value,
  max = 100,
  label,
  showValue = false,
  variant = "client",
}: ProgressProps) {
  const safeMax = max > 0 ? max : 100;
  const percentage = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.max(0, (value / safeMax) * 100),
  );

  const barColor =
    variant === "professional" ? "bg-orange-500" : "bg-emerald-500";

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      {(label || showValue) && (
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          {label && (
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300">
              {label}
            </span>
          )}

          {showValue && (
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500">
              {Math.round(percentage)}%
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-zinc-800"
        role="progressbar"
        aria-valuemin={0}
        aria-valuemax={safeMax}
        aria-valuenow={Math.min(value, safeMax)}
      >
        <div
          className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${barColor}`}
          style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components\ui\Spinner.tsx
====================================================================================================
type SpinnerProps = {
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  variant?: "client" | "professional" | "light";
  label?: string;
};

const sizes = {
  sm: "h-4 w-4 border-2",
  md: "h-6 w-6 border-2",
  lg: "h-8 w-8 border-[3px]",
};

const colors = {
  client: "border-emerald-500/30 border-t-emerald-500",
  professional: "border-orange-500/30 border-t-orange-500",
  light: "border-white/30 border-t-white",
};

export default function Spinner({
  size = "md",
  variant = "client",
  label,
}: SpinnerProps) {
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3">
      <span
        className={[
          "animate-spin rounded-full",
          sizes[size],
          colors[variant],
        ].join(" ")}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {label && (
        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-400">{label}</span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
ARQUIVO: components\vemgo\OnboardingStepper.tsx
====================================================================================================
"use client";

type OnboardingSteps = Record<string, number>;

interface OnboardingStepperProps {
  currentStep: number;
  totalSteps?: number;
  steps?: OnboardingSteps;
  variant?: string;
}

const DEFAULT_TOTAL_STEPS = 9;

export function OnboardingStepper({
  currentStep,
  totalSteps = DEFAULT_TOTAL_STEPS,
  steps,
  variant = "professional",
}: OnboardingStepperProps) {
  const resolvedTotalSteps =
    steps && Object.keys(steps).length > 0
      ? Math.max(
          totalSteps,
          ...Object.values(steps),
        )
      : totalSteps;

  const safeCurrentStep = Math.min(
    Math.max(currentStep, 1),
    resolvedTotalSteps,
  );

  const percentage = Math.round(
    (safeCurrentStep / resolvedTotalSteps) * 100,
  );

  const progressColor =
    variant === "professional"
      ? "bg-vemgo-orange"
      : variant === "control"
        ? "bg-vemgo-blue"
        : "bg-vemgo-green";

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">
          Etapa {safeCurrentStep} de{" "}
          {resolvedTotalSteps}
        </span>

        <span className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">
          {percentage}%
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
        {Array.from({
          length: resolvedTotalSteps,
        }).map((_, index) => {
          const step = index + 1;

          const completed =
            step < safeCurrentStep;

          const active =
            step === safeCurrentStep;

          return (
            <div
              key={step}
              className="min-w-0 flex-1"
            >
              <div
                className={[
                  "h-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors duration-180",
                  completed || active
                    ? progressColor
                    : "bg-white/10",
                ].join(" ")}
              />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default OnboardingStepper;

ARQUIVO: lib\professional-onboarding.ts
====================================================================================================
export const PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_STEPS = {
  DATA: 1,
  ADDRESS: 2,
  PHOTO: 3,
  IDENTITY: 4,
  COMPETENCIES: 5,
  PRICES: 6,
  AVAILABILITY: 7,
  SERVICE_AREA: 8,
  PLAN: 9,
} as const;

export const PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_TOTAL_STEPS = 9;

export const PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_STEP = 10;

export function getProfessionalOnboardingRoute(step: number) {
  const routes: Record<number, string> = {
    1: "/cadastro-profissional",
    2: "/cadastro-profissional/endereco",
    3: "/cadastro-profissional/foto",
    4: "/cadastro-profissional/identidade",
    5: "/cadastro-profissional/competencias",
    6: "/cadastro-profissional/precos",
    7: "/cadastro-profissional/disponibilidade",
    8: "/cadastro-profissional/area-atendimento",
    9: "/cadastro-profissional/plano",
  };

  if (step >= PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_STEP) {
    return "/profissional";
  }

  return routes[step] ?? routes[1];
}

export function getNextProfessionalOnboardingStep(
  currentStep: number,
) {
  return Math.min(
    currentStep + 1,
    PROFESSIONAL_ONBOARDING_COMPLETED_STEP,
  );
}

ARQUIVO: lib\supabase-browser.ts

import { createBrowserClient } from "@supabase/ssr";

export function createSupabaseBrowserClient() {
  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const supabaseKey =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;

  if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseKey) {
    throw new Error(
      "Configuração do Supabase ausente. Confira NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL e NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY no .env.local.",
    );
  }

  return createBrowserClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey);
}

export const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash

# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."classify_request"("p_request_id" "uuid") RETURNS "void"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_request public.requests%rowtype;
  v_service_id uuid;
begin
  select *
    into v_request
  from public.requests
  where id = p_request_id
    and client_id = auth.uid();

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Solicitação não encontrada ou não pertence ao usuário.';
  end if;

  if v_request.status <> 'pending' then
    raise exception 'A solicitação não está pendente.';
  end if;

  if lower(v_request.description) like '%tomada%'
     and (
       lower(v_request.description) like '%sem energia%'
       or lower(v_request.description) like '%sem luz%'
     )
  then
    select id
      into v_service_id
    from public.services
    where code = 'ELE-098'
      and is_active = true
    limit 1;

    if v_service_id is null then
      raise exception 'Serviço ELE-098 não encontrado ou inativo.';
    end if;

    update public.requests
    set
      service_id = v_service_id,
      status = 'classified',
      updated_at = now()
    where id = p_request_id;
  else
    raise exception 'Nenhuma regra de classificação encontrada.';
  end if;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."classify_request"("p_request_id" "uuid") OWNER TO "postgres";

SET default_tablespace = '';

SET default_table_access_method = "heap";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."appointments" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "order_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "starts_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    "ends_at" timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'scheduled'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "confirmed_at" timestamp with time zone,
    "cancelled_at" timestamp with time zone,
    "cancellation_reason" "text",
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "professional_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "appointments_check" CHECK (("ends_at" > "starts_at")),
    CONSTRAINT "appointments_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['scheduled'::"text", 'confirmed'::"text", 'in_progress'::"text", 'completed'::"text", 'cancelled'::"text", 'no_show'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."appointments" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."create_professional_appointment"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) RETURNS "public"."appointments"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_order public.orders%rowtype;
  v_appointment public.appointments%rowtype;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if p_starts_at is null or p_ends_at is null then
    raise exception 'Data e horário são obrigatórios.';
  end if;

  if p_ends_at <= p_starts_at then
    raise exception 'O horário final deve ser posterior ao horário inicial.';
  end if;

  select *
  into v_order
  from public.orders
  where id = p_order_id
    and professional_id = v_user_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Pedido não encontrado.';
  end if;

  if v_order.status <> 'confirmed' then
    raise exception 'Este pedido não está disponível para agendamento.';
  end if;

  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.appointments a
    where a.order_id = p_order_id
      and a.status not in ('cancelled')
  ) then
    raise exception 'Este pedido já possui um agendamento.';
  end if;

  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.appointments a
    where a.status not in ('cancelled')
      and p_starts_at < a.ends_at
      and p_ends_at > a.starts_at
      and exists (
        select 1
        from public.orders o
        where o.id = a.order_id
          and o.professional_id = v_user_id
      )
  ) then
    raise exception 'O profissional já possui um agendamento neste horário.';
  end if;

  insert into public.appointments (
    order_id,
    starts_at,
    ends_at,
    status
  )
  values (
    p_order_id,
    p_starts_at,
    p_ends_at,
    'scheduled'
  )
  returning *
  into v_appointment;

  update public.orders
  set
    status = 'confirmed',
    scheduled_start = p_starts_at,
    scheduled_end = p_ends_at,
    updated_at = now()
  where id = p_order_id;

  return v_appointment;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."create_professional_appointment"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."get_client_order_schedule"("p_order_id" "uuid") RETURNS "jsonb"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_order public.orders%rowtype;
  v_availability jsonb;
  v_appointments jsonb;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  -- O pedido precisa pertencer ao cliente logado
  select *
  into v_order
  from public.orders
  where id = p_order_id
    and client_id = v_user_id
    and status = 'confirmed';

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Pedido não encontrado ou indisponível para agendamento.';
  end if;

  -- Disponibilidade recorrente do profissional
  select coalesce(
    jsonb_agg(
      jsonb_build_object(
        'id', pa.id,
        'day_of_week', pa.day_of_week,
        'start_time', pa.start_time,
        'end_time', pa.end_time,
        'is_active', pa.is_active
      )
      order by pa.day_of_week, pa.start_time
    ),
    '[]'::jsonb
  )
  into v_availability
  from public.professional_availability pa
  where pa.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
    and pa.is_active = true;

  -- Agendamentos que ocupam a agenda do profissional
  select coalesce(
    jsonb_agg(
      jsonb_build_object(
        'id', a.id,
        'starts_at', a.starts_at,
        'ends_at', a.ends_at,
        'status', a.status
      )
      order by a.starts_at
    ),
    '[]'::jsonb
  )
  into v_appointments
  from public.appointments a
  where a.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
    and a.status in ('scheduled', 'confirmed', 'in_progress')
    and a.ends_at >= now();

  return jsonb_build_object(
    'order_id', v_order.id,
    'professional_id', v_order.professional_id,
    'availability', v_availability,
    'appointments', v_appointments
  );
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."get_client_order_schedule"("p_order_id" "uuid") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."get_pending_identity_verifications"() RETURNS TABLE("verification_id" "uuid", "profile_id" "uuid", "full_name" "text", "user_type" "text", "identity_status" "text", "document_id" "uuid", "document_type" "text", "document_number" "text", "document_front_path" "text", "document_back_path" "text", "selfie_path" "text", "verification_status" "text", "created_at" timestamp with time zone)
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid;
begin
  current_user_id := auth.uid();

  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.profiles admin_profile
    where admin_profile.id = current_user_id
      and admin_profile.user_type = 'admin'
      and admin_profile.status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Acesso negado.';
  end if;

  return query
  select
    iv.id as verification_id,
    iv.profile_id as profile_id,
    p.full_name as full_name,
    p.user_type as user_type,
    p.identity_status as identity_status,
    d.id as document_id,
    d.document_type as document_type,
    d.document_number as document_number,
    d.document_front_path as document_front_path,
    d.document_back_path as document_back_path,
    iv.selfie_path as selfie_path,
    iv.status as verification_status,
    iv.created_at as created_at
  from public.identity_verifications iv
  join public.profiles p
    on p.id = iv.profile_id
  left join public.identity_documents d
    on d.id = iv.identity_document_id
  where iv.status = 'pending'
  order by iv.created_at asc;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."get_pending_identity_verifications"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."get_pending_professionals"() RETURNS TABLE("professional_id" "uuid", "profile_id" "uuid", "full_name" "text", "identity_status" "text", "verification_status" "text", "service_status" "text", "services_count" bigint, "prices_count" bigint, "regions_count" bigint, "availability_count" bigint)
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid;
begin
  current_user_id := auth.uid();

  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.profiles admin_profile
    where admin_profile.id = current_user_id
      and admin_profile.user_type = 'admin'
      and admin_profile.status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Acesso negado.';
  end if;

  return query
  select
    p.id as professional_id,
    p.id as profile_id,
    pr.full_name,
    pr.identity_status,
    p.verification_status,
    p.service_status,

    (
      select count(*)
      from public.professional_services ps
      where ps.professional_id = p.id
        and ps.is_active = true
    ) as services_count,

    (
      select count(*)
      from public.service_prices sp
      where sp.professional_id = p.id
        and sp.is_active = true
        and sp.valid_from <= now()
        and (
          sp.valid_until is null
          or sp.valid_until > now()
        )
    ) as prices_count,

    (
      select count(*)
      from public.professional_regions preg
      where preg.professional_id = p.id
        and preg.is_active = true
    ) as regions_count,

    (
      select count(*)
      from public.professional_availability pa
      where pa.professional_id = p.id
        and pa.is_active = true
    ) as availability_count

  from public.professionals p
  inner join public.profiles pr
    on pr.id = p.id

  where p.verification_status = 'pending'

  order by p.created_at asc;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."get_pending_professionals"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."get_professional_opportunities"() RETURNS TABLE("match_id" "uuid", "request_id" "uuid", "description" "text", "urgency" "text", "request_status" "text", "service_code" "text", "service_name" "text", "price" numeric, "city" "text", "state" "text", "neighborhood" "text", "match_score" numeric, "match_status" "text", "sent_at" timestamp with time zone)
    LANGUAGE "sql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
  select
    rm.id as match_id,
    r.id as request_id,
    r.description,
    r.urgency,
    r.status as request_status,
    s.code as service_code,
    s.name as service_name,
    sp.price,
    a.city,
    a.state,
    a.neighborhood,
    rm.match_score,
    rm.status as match_status,
    rm.sent_at
  from public.request_matches rm
  join public.requests r
    on r.id = rm.request_id
  join public.services s
    on s.id = r.service_id
  join public.addresses a
    on a.id = r.address_id
  left join public.service_prices sp
    on sp.professional_id = rm.professional_id
   and sp.service_id = r.service_id
   and sp.is_active = true
   and sp.valid_from <= now()
   and (
     sp.valid_until is null
     or sp.valid_until > now()
   )
  where rm.professional_id = auth.uid()
    and rm.status = 'pending'
    and r.status = 'matching'
  order by rm.sent_at desc;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."get_professional_opportunities"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."handle_new_user"() RETURNS "trigger"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  requested_type text;
begin
  requested_type :=
    case
      when new.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'user_type' = 'professional'
        then 'professional'
      else 'client'
    end;

  insert into public.profiles (
    id,
    full_name,
    user_type,
    status
  )
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'full_name', ''),
    requested_type,
    'active'
  );

  if requested_type = 'professional' then
    insert into public.professionals (
      id,
      verification_status,
      service_status
    )
    values (
      new.id,
      'pending',
      'inactive'
    );
  else
    insert into public.clients (
      id
    )
    values (
      new.id
    );
  end if;

  return new;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."handle_new_user"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."match_request"("p_request_id" "uuid") RETURNS integer
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_request public.requests%rowtype;
  v_address public.addresses%rowtype;
  v_count integer := 0;
begin
  select *
    into v_request
  from public.requests
  where id = p_request_id;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Solicitação não encontrada.';
  end if;

  if v_request.status <> 'classified' then
    raise exception 'A solicitação precisa estar classificada.';
  end if;

  if v_request.service_id is null then
    raise exception 'A solicitação não possui serviço definido.';
  end if;

  select *
    into v_address
  from public.addresses
  where id = v_request.address_id;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Endereço da solicitação não encontrado.';
  end if;

  insert into public.request_matches (
    request_id,
    professional_id,
    match_score,
    status,
    sent_at
  )
  select
    v_request.id,
    p.id,
    100,
    'pending',
    now()
  from public.professionals p
  join public.professional_services ps
    on ps.professional_id = p.id
   and ps.service_id = v_request.service_id
   and ps.is_active = true
  join public.service_prices sp
    on sp.professional_id = p.id
   and sp.service_id = v_request.service_id
   and sp.is_active = true
   and sp.valid_from <= now()
   and (
     sp.valid_until is null
     or sp.valid_until > now()
   )
  join public.professional_regions pr
    on pr.professional_id = p.id
   and pr.is_active = true
   and lower(trim(pr.city)) = lower(trim(v_address.city))
   and lower(trim(pr.state)) = lower(trim(v_address.state))
  where p.verification_status = 'approved'
    and p.service_status = 'active'
  on conflict (request_id, professional_id)
  do nothing;

  get diagnostics v_count = row_count;

  if v_count > 0 then
    update public.requests
    set
      status = 'matching',
      updated_at = now()
    where id = v_request.id;
  end if;

  return v_count;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."match_request"("p_request_id" "uuid") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."request_appointment_slot"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) RETURNS "public"."appointments"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_order public.orders%rowtype;
  v_professional public.professionals%rowtype;
  v_appointment public.appointments%rowtype;
  v_day_of_week integer;
  v_start_time time;
  v_end_time time;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if p_starts_at >= p_ends_at then
    raise exception 'Horário inválido.';
  end if;

  -- Localiza o pedido e garante que pertence ao cliente logado
  select *
  into v_order
  from public.orders
  where id = p_order_id
    and client_id = v_user_id
    and status = 'confirmed'
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Pedido não encontrado ou não está disponível para agendamento.';
  end if;

  -- Garante que existe profissional associado
  select *
  into v_professional
  from public.professionals
  where id = v_order.professional_id;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Profissional não encontrado.';
  end if;

  -- Converte o horário para o fuso de São Paulo
  v_day_of_week :=
    extract(
      dow from (p_starts_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo')
    )::integer;

  v_start_time :=
    (p_starts_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo')::time;

  v_end_time :=
    (p_ends_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo')::time;

  -- Verifica se o horário está dentro da disponibilidade
  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.professional_availability pa
    where pa.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
      and pa.day_of_week = v_day_of_week
      and pa.is_active = true
      and pa.start_time <= v_start_time
      and pa.end_time >= v_end_time
  ) then
    raise exception 'O horário escolhido está fora da disponibilidade do profissional.';
  end if;

  -- Verifica conflito com outro agendamento
  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.appointments a
    where a.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
      and a.status in ('scheduled', 'confirmed', 'in_progress')
      and a.starts_at < p_ends_at
      and a.ends_at > p_starts_at
  ) then
    raise exception 'Este horário não está mais disponível.';
  end if;

  -- Garante que este pedido ainda não possui agendamento
  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.appointments a
    where a.order_id = p_order_id
      and a.status in ('scheduled', 'confirmed', 'in_progress')
  ) then
    raise exception 'Este serviço já possui uma solicitação de horário.';
  end if;

  -- Cria a solicitação de horário
  insert into public.appointments (
    order_id,
    professional_id,
    starts_at,
    ends_at,
    status
  )
  values (
    p_order_id,
    v_order.professional_id,
    p_starts_at,
    p_ends_at,
    'scheduled'
  )
  returning *
  into v_appointment;

  return v_appointment;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."request_appointment_slot"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."reschedule_appointment_slot"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) RETURNS "public"."appointments"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO ''
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_order public.orders%rowtype;
  v_old_appointment public.appointments%rowtype;
  v_new_appointment public.appointments%rowtype;
  v_day_of_week integer;
  v_start_time time;
  v_end_time time;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if p_starts_at >= p_ends_at then
    raise exception 'Horário inválido.';
  end if;

  select *
  into v_order
  from public.orders
  where id = p_order_id
    and client_id = v_user_id
    and status = 'confirmed'
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Pedido não encontrado ou não está disponível para reagendamento.';
  end if;

  select *
  into v_old_appointment
  from public.appointments
  where order_id = p_order_id
    and professional_id = v_order.professional_id
    and status = 'confirmed'
  order by starts_at desc
  limit 1
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Este serviço não possui um agendamento confirmado para reagendar.';
  end if;

  v_day_of_week :=
    extract(
      dow from (
        p_starts_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo'
      )
    )::integer;

  v_start_time :=
    (
      p_starts_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo'
    )::time;

  v_end_time :=
    (
      p_ends_at at time zone 'America/Sao_Paulo'
    )::time;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.professional_availability pa
    where pa.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
      and pa.day_of_week = v_day_of_week
      and pa.is_active = true
      and pa.start_time <= v_start_time
      and pa.end_time >= v_end_time
  ) then
    raise exception 'O horário escolhido está fora da disponibilidade do profissional.';
  end if;

  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.appointments a
    where a.professional_id = v_order.professional_id
      and a.id <> v_old_appointment.id
      and a.status in (
        'scheduled',
        'confirmed',
        'in_progress'
      )
      and a.starts_at < p_ends_at
      and a.ends_at > p_starts_at
  ) then
    raise exception 'Este horário não está mais disponível.';
  end if;

  update public.appointments
  set
    status = 'cancelled',
    cancelled_at = now(),
    cancellation_reason = 'Reagendamento solicitado pelo cliente',
    updated_at = now()
  where id = v_old_appointment.id;

  update public.orders
  set
    scheduled_start = null,
    scheduled_end = null,
    updated_at = now()
  where id = v_order.id;

  insert into public.appointments (
    order_id,
    professional_id,
    starts_at,
    ends_at,
    status
  )
  values (
    v_order.id,
    v_order.professional_id,
    p_starts_at,
    p_ends_at,
    'scheduled'
  )
  returning *
  into v_new_appointment;

  return v_new_appointment;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."reschedule_appointment_slot"("p_order_id" "uuid", "p_starts_at" timestamp with time zone, "p_ends_at" timestamp with time zone) OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."respond_to_appointment_request"("p_appointment_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text") RETURNS "public"."appointments"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_appointment public.appointments%rowtype;
  v_order public.orders%rowtype;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision not in ('confirmed', 'declined') then
    raise exception 'Decisão inválida.';
  end if;

  -- Localiza a solicitação e garante que pertence ao profissional logado
  select *
  into v_appointment
  from public.appointments
  where id = p_appointment_id
    and professional_id = v_user_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Solicitação de horário não encontrada.';
  end if;

  -- Só pode responder a uma solicitação ainda pendente
  if v_appointment.status <> 'scheduled'
     or v_appointment.confirmed_at is not null then
    raise exception 'Esta solicitação já foi respondida.';
  end if;

  -- Localiza o pedido
  select *
  into v_order
  from public.orders
  where id = v_appointment.order_id
    and professional_id = v_user_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Pedido não encontrado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision = 'declined' then

    update public.appointments
    set
      status = 'cancelled',
      cancelled_at = now(),
      cancellation_reason = 'Horário recusado pelo profissional',
      updated_at = now()
    where id = p_appointment_id
    returning *
    into v_appointment;

    return v_appointment;
  end if;

  -- Confirma o horário
  update public.appointments
  set
    status = 'confirmed',
    confirmed_at = now(),
    updated_at = now()
  where id = p_appointment_id
  returning *
  into v_appointment;

  -- Só agora o pedido passa a ter horário definitivo
  update public.orders
  set
    scheduled_start = v_appointment.starts_at,
    scheduled_end = v_appointment.ends_at,
    updated_at = now()
  where id = v_order.id;

  return v_appointment;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."respond_to_appointment_request"("p_appointment_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."respond_to_professional_opportunity"("p_match_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text") RETURNS TABLE("match_id" "uuid", "match_status" "text", "request_id" "uuid", "request_status" "text", "order_id" "uuid")
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_match public.request_matches%rowtype;
  v_request public.requests%rowtype;
  v_order_id uuid;
  v_price numeric;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();

  if v_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision not in ('accepted', 'declined') then
    raise exception 'Decisão inválida.';
  end if;

  select *
  into v_match
  from public.request_matches
  where id = p_match_id
    and professional_id = v_user_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Oportunidade não encontrada.';
  end if;

  if v_match.status <> 'pending' then
    raise exception 'Esta oportunidade já recebeu uma resposta.';
  end if;

  select *
  into v_request
  from public.requests
  where id = v_match.request_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Solicitação não encontrada.';
  end if;

  if v_request.status <> 'matching' then
    raise exception 'Esta solicitação não está mais disponível.';
  end if;

  if p_decision = 'declined' then

    update public.request_matches
    set
      status = 'declined',
      responded_at = now()
    where id = p_match_id;

    return query
    select
      v_match.id,
      'declined'::text,
      v_request.id,
      v_request.status,
      null::uuid;

    return;
  end if;

  select sp.price
  into v_price
  from public.service_prices sp
  where sp.professional_id = v_user_id
    and sp.service_id = v_request.service_id
    and sp.is_active = true
    and sp.valid_from <= now()
    and (
      sp.valid_until is null
      or sp.valid_until > now()
    )
  order by sp.valid_from desc
  limit 1;

  if v_price is null then
    raise exception 'O profissional não possui preço ativo para este serviço.';
  end if;

  update public.request_matches
  set
    status = 'accepted',
    responded_at = now()
  where id = p_match_id;

  update public.requests
  set
    status = 'matched',
    updated_at = now()
  where id = v_request.id;

  select o.id
  into v_order_id
  from public.orders o
  where o.request_id = v_request.id
  limit 1;

  if v_order_id is null then
    insert into public.orders (
      request_id,
      client_id,
      professional_id,
      service_id,
      address_id,
      price,
      currency,
      status
    )
    values (
      v_request.id,
      v_request.client_id,
      v_user_id,
      v_request.service_id,
      v_request.address_id,
      v_price,
      'BRL',
      'confirmed'
    )
    returning id into v_order_id;
  end if;

  begin
    insert into public.audit_logs (
      actor_profile_id,
      action,
      entity_type,
      entity_id,
      details
    )
    values (
      v_user_id,
      'respond_opportunity',
      'request_match',
      p_match_id,
      jsonb_build_object(
        'decision', p_decision,
        'request_id', v_request.id,
        'order_id', v_order_id
      )
    );
  exception
    when undefined_table then
      null;
  end;

  return query
  select
    v_match.id,
    'accepted'::text,
    v_request.id,
    'matched'::text,
    v_order_id;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."respond_to_professional_opportunity"("p_match_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."review_identity_verification"("p_verification_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text", "p_rejection_reason" "text" DEFAULT NULL::"text") RETURNS "text"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid;
  target_profile_id uuid;
  target_document_id uuid;
begin
  current_user_id := auth.uid();

  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.profiles
    where id = current_user_id
      and user_type = 'admin'
      and status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Acesso negado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision not in (
    'approved',
    'rejected',
    'manual_review'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Decisão de identidade inválida.';
  end if;

  select
    iv.profile_id,
    iv.identity_document_id
  into
    target_profile_id,
    target_document_id
  from public.identity_verifications iv
  where iv.id = p_verification_id
  for update;

  if target_profile_id is null then
    raise exception 'Verificação não encontrada.';
  end if;

  if p_decision = 'approved' then

    update public.identity_verifications
    set
      status = 'approved',
      rejection_reason = null,
      verified_at = now(),
      provider = 'manual'
    where id = p_verification_id;

    if target_document_id is not null then
      update public.identity_documents
      set
        verification_status = 'approved',
        rejection_reason = null,
        verified_at = now(),
        verification_provider = 'manual'
      where id = target_document_id;
    end if;

    update public.profiles
    set identity_status = 'verified'
    where id = target_profile_id;

  elsif p_decision = 'rejected' then

    update public.identity_verifications
    set
      status = 'rejected',
      rejection_reason = p_rejection_reason,
      verified_at = null
    where id = p_verification_id;

    if target_document_id is not null then
      update public.identity_documents
      set
        verification_status = 'rejected',
        rejection_reason = p_rejection_reason,
        verified_at = null
      where id = target_document_id;
    end if;

    update public.profiles
    set identity_status = 'rejected'
    where id = target_profile_id;

  else

    update public.identity_verifications
    set
      status = 'manual_review',
      rejection_reason = null,
      verified_at = null
    where id = p_verification_id;

    if target_document_id is not null then
      update public.identity_documents
      set
        verification_status = 'manual_review',
        rejection_reason = null,
        verified_at = null
      where id = target_document_id;
    end if;

    update public.profiles
    set identity_status = 'manual_review'
    where id = target_profile_id;

  end if;

  insert into public.audit_logs (
    actor_profile_id,
    action,
    entity_type,
    entity_id,
    details
  )
  values (
    current_user_id,
    'identity_review',
    'identity_verification',
    p_verification_id,
    jsonb_build_object(
      'decision', p_decision,
      'target_profile_id', target_profile_id,
      'target_document_id', target_document_id,
      'rejection_reason', p_rejection_reason
    )
  );

  return p_decision;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."review_identity_verification"("p_verification_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text", "p_rejection_reason" "text") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."review_professional"("p_professional_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text", "p_rejection_reason" "text" DEFAULT NULL::"text") RETURNS "void"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid;
  professional_identity_status text;
begin
  current_user_id := auth.uid();

  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.profiles admin_profile
    where admin_profile.id = current_user_id
      and admin_profile.user_type = 'admin'
      and admin_profile.status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Acesso negado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision not in ('approved', 'rejected') then
    raise exception 'Decisão inválida.';
  end if;

  select pr.identity_status
  into professional_identity_status
  from public.profiles pr
  where pr.id = p_professional_id;

  if professional_identity_status is null then
    raise exception 'Profissional não encontrado.';
  end if;

  if p_decision = 'approved'
     and professional_identity_status <> 'verified' then
    raise exception 'O profissional só pode ser aprovado após a identidade ser verificada.';
  end if;

  if p_decision = 'approved' then

    update public.professionals
    set
      verification_status = 'approved',
      service_status = 'active',
      approved_at = now(),
      approved_by = current_user_id,
      rejection_reason = null,
      updated_at = now()
    where id = p_professional_id;

    update public.profiles
    set
      status = 'active',
      updated_at = now()
    where id = p_professional_id;

  else

    update public.professionals
    set
      verification_status = 'rejected',
      service_status = 'inactive',
      approved_at = null,
      approved_by = null,
      rejection_reason = nullif(trim(p_rejection_reason), ''),
      updated_at = now()
    where id = p_professional_id;

  end if;

  insert into public.audit_logs (
    actor_profile_id,
    action,
    entity_type,
    entity_id,
    details
  )
  values (
    current_user_id,
    case
      when p_decision = 'approved'
        then 'professional_approved'
      else
        'professional_rejected'
    end,
    'professional',
    p_professional_id,
    jsonb_build_object(
      'decision', p_decision,
      'rejection_reason',
      nullif(trim(p_rejection_reason), '')
    )
  );
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."review_professional"("p_professional_id" "uuid", "p_decision" "text", "p_rejection_reason" "text") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."set_profile_avatar_url"("p_avatar_url" "text") RETURNS "void"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado';
  end if;

  if p_avatar_url is null or length(trim(p_avatar_url)) = 0 then
    raise exception 'Caminho da foto inválido';
  end if;

  update public.profiles
  set
    avatar_url = trim(p_avatar_url),
    updated_at = now()
  where id = auth.uid();

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Perfil não encontrado';
  end if;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."set_profile_avatar_url"("p_avatar_url" "text") OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."set_service_slug"() RETURNS "trigger"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql"
    AS $$
begin
  if new.slug is null or trim(new.slug) = '' then
    new.slug := lower(new.code);
  end if;

  return new;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."set_service_slug"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."set_updated_at"() RETURNS "trigger"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql"
    AS $$
begin
  new.updated_at = now();
  return new;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."set_updated_at"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."submit_identity_for_review"() RETURNS "text"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql" SECURITY DEFINER
    SET "search_path" TO 'public'
    AS $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid;
begin
  current_user_id := auth.uid();

  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Usuário não autenticado.';
  end if;

  update public.profiles
  set identity_status = 'pending'
  where id = current_user_id;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Perfil não encontrado.';
  end if;

  return 'pending';
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."submit_identity_for_review"() OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION "public"."validate_identity_document"() RETURNS "trigger"
    LANGUAGE "plpgsql"
    AS $$
begin
  if new.document_number is null
     or trim(new.document_number) = '' then
    raise exception 'Número do documento é obrigatório.';
  end if;

  if new.document_front_path is null
     or trim(new.document_front_path) = '' then
    raise exception 'Documento da frente é obrigatório.';
  end if;

  if new.document_type in ('rg', 'cnh') then
    if new.document_back_path is null
       or trim(new.document_back_path) = '' then
      raise exception 'O verso do documento é obrigatório para RG e CNH.';
    end if;
  end if;

  return new;
end;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION "public"."validate_identity_document"() OWNER TO "postgres";

);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."professional_availability" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "professional_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "day_of_week" smallint NOT NULL,
    "start_time" time without time zone NOT NULL,
    "end_time" time without time zone NOT NULL,
    "is_active" boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "professional_availability_check" CHECK (("start_time" < "end_time")),
    CONSTRAINT "professional_availability_day_of_week_check" CHECK ((("day_of_week" >= 0) AND ("day_of_week" <= 6)))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."professional_availability" OWNER TO "postgres";

    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,

    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."professionals" (
    "id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "display_name" "text",
    "bio" "text",
    "document_type" "text",
    "document_number" "text",
    "verification_status" "text" DEFAULT 'pending'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "service_status" "text" DEFAULT 'inactive'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "approved_at" timestamp with time zone,
    "approved_by" "uuid",
    "rejection_reason" "text",
    CONSTRAINT "professionals_document_type_check" CHECK (("document_type" = ANY (ARRAY['cpf'::"text", 'cnpj'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "professionals_service_status_check" CHECK (("service_status" = ANY (ARRAY['inactive'::"text", 'active'::"text", 'paused'::"text", 'blocked'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "professionals_verification_status_check" CHECK (("verification_status" = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::"text", 'under_review'::"text", 'approved'::"text", 'rejected'::"text", 'suspended'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."professionals" OWNER TO "postgres";

    "id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "full_name" "text",
    "phone" "text",
    "avatar_url" "text",

    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'active'::"text" NOT NULL,

    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "identity_status" "text" DEFAULT 'unverified'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "professional_onboarding_step" integer DEFAULT 1,
    CONSTRAINT "profiles_identity_status_check" CHECK (("identity_status" = ANY (ARRAY['unverified'::"text", 'pending'::"text", 'verified'::"text", 'rejected'::"text", 'manual_review'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "profiles_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['active'::"text", 'inactive'::"text", 'blocked'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "profiles_user_type_check" CHECK (("user_type" = ANY (ARRAY['client'::"text", 'professional'::"text", 'company'::"text", 'admin'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."profiles" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."request_matches" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "request_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "professional_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "match_score" numeric(8,4),
    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'pending'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "sent_at" timestamp with time zone,
    "responded_at" timestamp with time zone,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "request_matches_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::"text", 'sent'::"text", 'accepted'::"text", 'declined'::"text", 'expired'::"text", 'cancelled'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."request_matches" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."requests" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "client_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "service_id" "uuid",
    "address_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "description" "text" NOT NULL,
    "urgency" "text" DEFAULT 'normal'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'pending'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "requests_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::"text", 'classified'::"text", 'matching'::"text", 'matched'::"text", 'scheduled'::"text", 'cancelled'::"text", 'expired'::"text", 'completed'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "requests_urgency_check" CHECK (("urgency" = ANY (ARRAY['normal'::"text", 'urgent'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."requests" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."reviews" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "order_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "client_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "professional_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "rating" smallint NOT NULL,
    "comment" "text",
    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'published'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "reviews_rating_check" CHECK ((("rating" >= 1) AND ("rating" <= 5))),
    CONSTRAINT "reviews_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['published'::"text", 'hidden'::"text", 'under_review'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."reviews" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."service_price_guidelines" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "service_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "minimum_price" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    "maximum_price" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    "currency" "text" DEFAULT 'BRL'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "source" "text" DEFAULT 'vemgo'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "confidence" numeric(5,2),
    "valid_from" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "valid_until" timestamp with time zone,
    "is_active" boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "service_price_guidelines_check" CHECK (("maximum_price" >= "minimum_price")),
    CONSTRAINT "service_price_guidelines_confidence_check" CHECK ((("confidence" >= (0)::numeric) AND ("confidence" <= (100)::numeric))),
    CONSTRAINT "service_price_guidelines_currency_check" CHECK (("currency" = 'BRL'::"text")),
    CONSTRAINT "service_price_guidelines_minimum_price_check" CHECK (("minimum_price" >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT "service_price_guidelines_source_check" CHECK (("source" = ANY (ARRAY['vemgo'::"text", 'ai'::"text", 'market'::"text", 'admin'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."service_price_guidelines" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."service_prices" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,
    "service_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    "price" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    "currency" "text" DEFAULT 'BRL'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "valid_from" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "valid_until" timestamp with time zone,
    "is_active" boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "professional_id" "uuid" NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "service_prices_check" CHECK ((("valid_until" IS NULL) OR ("valid_until" > "valid_from"))),
    CONSTRAINT "service_prices_price_check" CHECK (("price" >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT "service_prices_price_non_negative" CHECK (("price" >= (0)::numeric))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."service_prices" OWNER TO "postgres";


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "public"."services" (
    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,

    "slug" "text" NOT NULL,

    "is_active" boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL,
    "display_order" integer DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "services_pricing_type_check" CHECK (("pricing_type" = ANY (ARRAY['fixed'::"text", 'rule_based'::"text", 'visit'::"text", 'calculated'::"text", 'professional_defined'::"text"])))
);


ALTER TABLE "public"."services" OWNER TO "postgres";

    "id" "uuid" DEFAULT "gen_random_uuid"() NOT NULL,

    "amount" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
    "currency" "text" DEFAULT 'BRL'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "status" "text" DEFAULT 'pending'::"text" NOT NULL,
    "reference" "text",
    "created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    "updated_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT "now"() NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "transactions_amount_check" CHECK (("amount" >= (0)::numeric)),
    CONSTRAINT "transactions_status_check" CHECK (("status" = ANY (ARRAY['pending'::"text", 'completed'::"text", 'failed'::"text", 'cancelled'::"text"]))),
    CONSTRAINT "transactions_transaction_type_check" CHECK (("transaction_type" = ANY (ARRAY['charge'::"text", 'commission'::"text", 'payout'::"text", 'refund'::"text", 'adjustment'::"text"])))
);

CREATE POLICY "Users can view their own legal acceptance" ON "public"."legal_acceptances" FOR SELECT TO "authenticated" USING (("auth"."uid"() = "user_id"));



ALTER TABLE "public"."addresses" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;


CREATE POLICY "addresses_delete_own" ON "public"."addresses" FOR DELETE TO "authenticated" USING ((( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid") = "profile_id"));



CREATE POLICY "addresses_insert_own" ON "public"."addresses" FOR INSERT TO "authenticated" WITH CHECK ((( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid") = "profile_id"));



CREATE POLICY "addresses_select_own" ON "public"."addresses" FOR SELECT TO "authenticated" USING ((( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid") = "profile_id"));



CREATE POLICY "addresses_update_own" ON "public"."addresses" FOR UPDATE TO "authenticated" USING ((( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid") = "profile_id")) WITH CHECK ((( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid") = "profile_id"));



ALTER TABLE "public"."appointments" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;


CREATE POLICY "appointments_insert_own_professional" ON "public"."appointments" FOR INSERT TO "authenticated" WITH CHECK ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM "public"."orders" "o"
  WHERE (("o"."id" = "appointments"."order_id") AND ("o"."professional_id" = ( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid")) AND ("o"."status" = 'confirmed'::"text")))));



CREATE POLICY "appointments_select_own_client" ON "public"."appointments" FOR SELECT TO "authenticated" USING ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM "public"."orders" "o"
  WHERE (("o"."id" = "appointments"."order_id") AND ("o"."client_id" = ( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid"))))));



CREATE POLICY "appointments_select_own_professional" ON "public"."appointments" FOR SELECT TO "authenticated" USING ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM "public"."orders" "o"
  WHERE (("o"."id" = "appointments"."order_id") AND ("o"."professional_id" = ( SELECT "auth"."uid"() AS "uid"))))));
